April 06, 2026

Adaptive Action Chunking at Inference-time for Vision-Language-Action Models

Abstract (English): In Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, action chunking (i.e., executing a sequence of actions without intermediate replanning) is a key technique to improve robotic manipulation abilities. However, a large chunk size reduces the model's responsiveness to new information, while a small one increases the likelihood of mode-jumping, jerky behavior resulting from discontinuities between chunks. Therefore, selecting the optimal chunk size is an urgent demand to balance the model's reactivity and consistency. Unfortunately, a dominant trend in current VLA models is an empirical fixed chunk length at inference-time, hindering their superiority and scalability across diverse manipulation tasks. To address this issue, we propose a novel Adaptive Action Chunking (AAC) strategy, which exploits action entropy as the cue to adaptively determine the chunk size based on current predictions. Extensive experiments on a wide range of simulated and real-world robotic manipulation tasks have demonstrated that our approach substantially improves performance over the state-of-the-art alternatives. The videos and source code are publicly available at https://lance-lot.github.io/adaptive-chunking.github.io/.

摘要（中文）: 在视觉-语言-动作模型中，动作分块（即在无需中间重新规划的情况下执行一系列动作）是提升机器人操作能力的关键技术。然而，较大的分块尺寸会降低模型对新信息的响应能力，而较小的分块则可能增加模式跳跃（即分块间不连续导致的突兀行为）的风险。因此，选择最优的分块尺寸是平衡模型反应性与一致性的迫切需求。遗憾的是，当前视觉-语言-动作模型普遍采用推理时固定分块长度的经验性策略，这限制了其在不同操作任务中的优越性与可扩展性。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种新颖的自适应动作分块策略，该策略利用动作熵作为线索，依据当前预测动态调整分块尺寸。通过大量模拟与真实机器人操作任务的实验验证，我们的方法在性能上显著优于现有先进方案。相关视频与源代码已公开于 https://lance-lot.github.io/adaptive-chunking.github.io/。

TLDR: This paper proposes an adaptive action chunking strategy for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, using action entropy to dynamically adjust chunk size at inference-time to improve robot manipulation performance.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的自适应动作分块策略，在推理时利用动作熵动态调整分块大小，以提高机器人操作性能。

Authors: Yuanchang Liang, Xiaobo Wang, Kai Wang, Shuo Wang, Xiaojiang Peng, Haoyu Chen, David Kim Huat Chua, Prahlad Vadakkepat

Learning Dexterous Grasping from Sparse Taxonomy Guidance

Abstract (English): Dexterous manipulation requires planning a grasp configuration suited to the object and task, which is then executed through coordinated multi-finger control. However, specifying grasp plans with dense pose or contact targets for every object and task is impractical. Meanwhile, end-to-end reinforcement learning from task rewards alone lacks controllability, making it difficult for users to intervene when failures occur. To this end, we present GRIT, a two-stage framework that learns dexterous control from sparse taxonomy guidance. GRIT first predicts a taxonomy-based grasp specification from the scene and task context. Conditioned on this sparse command, a policy generates continuous finger motions that accomplish the task while preserving the intended grasp structure. Our result shows that certain grasp taxonomies are more effective for specific object geometries. By leveraging this relationship, GRIT improves generalization to novel objects over baselines and achieves an overall success rate of 87.9%. Moreover, real-world experiments demonstrate controllability, enabling grasp strategies to be adjusted through high-level taxonomy selection based on object geometry and task intent.

摘要（中文）: 灵巧操作需要规划适应物体与任务的抓取配置，再通过多指协调控制予以执行。然而，为每个物体和任务指定包含密集位姿或接触目标的抓取方案并不现实。同时，仅依靠任务奖励的端到端强化学习缺乏可控性，使用户难以在故障发生时进行干预。为此，我们提出GRIT框架——一种通过稀疏分类指导学习灵巧控制的两阶段系统。GRIT首先依据场景与任务上下文预测基于分类法的抓取规范，随后在该稀疏指令约束下，通过策略生成连续的指尖动作以完成任务，同时保持预设的抓取结构。实验结果表明，特定抓取分类法对特定物体几何形状具有更优适应性。通过利用这种关联关系，GRIT相比基线方法在新物体上展现出更强的泛化能力，总体成功率可达87.9%。此外，真实世界实验验证了系统的可控性，用户可根据物体几何特征与任务意图，通过高层级分类选择动态调整抓取策略。

TLDR: The paper presents GRIT, a two-stage framework for learning dexterous grasping from sparse, high-level taxonomy guidance, improving generalization and controllability over end-to-end reinforcement learning.

简要说明: 本文提出了GRIT框架，通过稀疏的、基于分类的指导来学习灵巧抓取，分为预测分类指令和执行策略两个阶段，相比端到端强化学习提升了泛化性和可控性。

Authors: Juhan Park, Taerim Yoon, Seungmin Kim, Joonggil Kim, Wontae Ye, Jeongeun Park, Yoonbyung Chai, Geonwoo Cho, Geunwoo Cho, Dohyeong Kim, Kyungjae Lee, Yongjae Kim, Sungjoon Choi

Dynamic Whole-Body Dancing with Humanoid Robots -- A Model-Based Control Approach

Abstract (English): This paper presents an integrated model-based framework for generating and executing dynamic whole-body dance motions on humanoid robots. The framework operates in two stages: offline motion generation and online motion execution, both leveraging future state prediction to enable robust and dynamic dance motions in real-world environments. In the offline motion generation stage, human dance demonstrations are captured via a motion capture (MoCap) system, retargeted to the robot by solving a Quadratic Programming (QP) problem, and further refined using Trajectory Optimization (TO) to ensure dynamic feasibility. In the online motion execution stage, a centroidal dynamics-based Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework tracks the planned motions in real time and proactively adjusts swing foot placement to adapt to real world disturbances. We validate our framework on the full-size humanoid robot Kuavo 4Pro, demonstrating the dynamic dance motions both in simulation and in a four-minute live public performance with a team of four robots. Experimental results show that longer prediction horizons improve both motion expressiveness in planning and stability in execution.

摘要（中文）: 本文提出一种基于模型的集成框架，用于在人形机器人上生成并执行动态全身舞蹈动作。该框架分为离线动作生成与在线动作执行两个阶段，二者均利用未来状态预测实现现实环境中的鲁棒动态舞蹈动作。在离线动作生成阶段，通过动作捕捉系统采集人类舞蹈演示数据，通过求解二次规划问题将其重定向至机器人模型，并进一步利用轨迹优化方法进行动态可行性修正。在线动作执行阶段，基于质心动力的模型预测控制框架实时跟踪规划动作，并主动调整摆动足落地点位以应对现实环境扰动。我们在全尺寸人形机器人Kuavo 4Pro上验证了该框架，通过四台机器人组成的团队在仿真环境和四分钟现场公开表演中展示了动态舞蹈动作。实验结果表明，更长的预测时域能同时提升动作规划的表现力与执行阶段的稳定性。

TLDR: This paper presents a two-stage model-based control framework for generating and executing dynamic whole-body dance motions on humanoid robots, validated in simulation and a live public performance.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种基于模型的两阶段控制框架，用于生成和执行人形机器人的动态全身舞蹈动作，并在仿真和公开现场表演中得到了验证。

Authors: Shibowen Zhang, Jiayang Wu, Guannan Liu, Helin Zhu, Junjie Liu, Zhehan Li, Junhong Guo, Xiaokun Leng, Hangxin Liu, Jingwen Zhang, Jikai Wang, Zonghai Chen, Zhicheng He, Jiayi Wang, Yao Su

VA-FastNavi-MARL: Real-Time Robot Control with Multimedia-Driven Meta-Reinforcement Learning

Abstract (English): Interpreting dynamic, heterogeneous multimedia commands with real-time responsiveness is critical for Human-Robot Interaction. We present VA-FastNavi-MARL, a framework that aligns asynchronous audio-visual inputs into a unified latent representation. By treating diverse instructions as a distribution of navigable goals via Meta-Reinforcement Learning, our method enables rapid adaptation to unseen directives with negligible inference overhead. Unlike approaches bottlenecked by heavy sensory processing, our modality-agnostic stream ensures seamless, low-latency control. Validation on a multi-arm workspace confirms that VA-FastNavi-MARL significantly outperforms baselines in sample efficiency and maintains robust, real-time execution even under noisy multimedia streams.

摘要（中文）: 实现具有实时响应能力的动态、异构多媒体指令解析对机器人人机交互至关重要。本文提出VA-FastNavi-MARL框架，该框架将异步视听输入对齐为统一的潜在表示。通过元强化学习将多样化指令视为可导航目标分布，该方法能够以可忽略的推理开销快速适应未见指令。不同于受繁重感知处理制约的传统方法，我们的模态无关流保证了无缝、低延迟的控制。在多机械臂工作空间中的验证表明，VA-FastNavi-MARL在样本效率上显著优于基线方法，即使在嘈杂多媒体流环境下仍能保持鲁棒的实时执行性能。

TLDR: A framework called VA-FastNavi-MARL uses meta-reinforcement learning to align asynchronous audio-visual inputs into a unified representation for real-time, low-latency robot navigation and control, enabling rapid adaptation to new instructions.

简要说明: VA-FastNavi-MARL框架利用元强化学习，将异步的视听输入对齐为统一的表示，以实现实时的、低延迟的机器人导航与控制，并能快速适应新指令。

Authors: Yang Zhang, Shengxi Jing, Fengxiang Wang, Yuan Feng, Hong Wang

Risk-Constrained Belief-Space Optimization for Safe Control under Latent Uncertainty

Abstract (English): Many safety-critical control systems must operate under latent uncertainty that sensors cannot directly resolve at decision time. Such uncertainty, arising from unknown physical properties, exogenous disturbances, or unobserved environment geometry, influences dynamics, task feasibility, and safety margins. Standard methods optimize expected performance and offer limited protection against rare but severe outcomes, while robust formulations treat uncertainty conservatively without exploiting its probabilistic structure. We consider partially observed dynamical systems whose dynamics, costs, and safety constraints depend on a latent parameter maintained as a belief distribution, and propose a risk-sensitive belief-space Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control framework that plans under this belief while enforcing a Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) constraint on a trajectory safety margin over the receding horizon. The resulting controller optimizes a risk-regularized performance objective while explicitly constraining the tail risk of safety violations induced by latent parameter variability. We establish three properties of the resulting risk-constrained controller: (1) the CVaR constraint implies a probabilistic safety guarantee, (2) the controller recovers the risk-neutral optimum as the risk weight in the objective tends to zero, and (3) a union-bound argument extends the per-horizon guarantee to cumulative safety over repeated solves. In physics-based simulations of a vision-guided dexterous stowing task in which a grasped object must be inserted into an occupied slot with pose uncertainty exceeding prescribed lateral clearance requirements, our method achieves 82% success with zero contact violations at high risk aversion, compared to 55% and 50% for a risk-neutral configuration and a chance-constrained baseline, both of which incur nonzero exterior contact forces.

摘要（中文）: 许多安全关键控制系统必须在潜在不确定性下运行，这些不确定性在决策时无法通过传感器直接解析。此类不确定性源于未知的物理特性、外部干扰或未观测到的环境几何结构，会影响系统动态、任务可行性及安全裕度。标准方法通常优化期望性能，对罕见但严重后果的防护能力有限，而鲁棒性处理方法虽保守应对不确定性，却未利用其概率结构。本文考虑动态、成本及安全约束均依赖于潜在参数（以置信分布形式维护）的部分可观测动态系统，提出一种风险敏感的置信空间模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制框架。该框架在置信分布下进行规划，同时针对滚动时域内的轨迹安全裕度施加条件风险价值（CVaR）约束。所构建的控制器在优化风险正则化性能目标的同时，显式约束了由潜在参数变异引发的安全违规尾部风险。我们证明了该风险约束控制器的三个特性：（1）CVaR约束蕴含概率安全性保证；（2）当目标函数中风险权重趋于零时，控制器退化为风险中性最优解；（3）通过并集界论证可将单次时域保证扩展至重复求解的累积安全性。在基于物理的视觉引导灵巧收纳任务仿真中（该任务需将抓取物体插入存在位姿不确定性的狭窄槽位，且不确定度超过预设横向间隙容限），本方法在高度风险规避设置下实现了82%的成功率且零接触违规，而风险中性配置和机会约束基线方法分别仅获得55%和50%的成功率，且均产生非零外部接触力。

TLDR: The paper proposes a risk-sensitive belief-space MPC framework for safe robot control under latent uncertainty, validated on a vision-guided dexterous stowing task with a physical robot simulation.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种用于潜在不确定性下安全机器人控制的风险敏感信念空间MPC框架，并在视觉引导的灵巧堆放任务中通过物理仿真验证了其有效性。

Authors: Clinton Enwerem, John S. Baras, Calin Belta

VLA-Forget: Vision-Language-Action Unlearning for Embodied Foundation Models

Abstract (English): Vision-language-action (VLA) models are emerging as embodied foundation models for robotic manipulation, but their deployment introduces a new unlearning challenge: removing unsafe, spurious, or privacy-sensitive behaviors without degrading perception, language grounding, and action control. In OpenVLA-style policies, behavior is produced through a fused visual encoder, a cross-modal projector, and a language backbone that predicts tokenized robot actions, so undesirable knowledge can be distributed across perception, alignment, and reasoning/action layers rather than confined to a single module. Consequently, partial unlearning applied only to the vision stack or only to the language backbone is often insufficient, while conventional unlearning baselines designed for standalone vision or language models may leave residual forgetting or incur unnecessary utility loss in embodied settings. We propose VLA-Forget, a hybrid unlearning framework that combines ratio-aware selective editing for perception and cross-modal specificity with layer-selective reasoning/action unlearning for utility-preserving forgetting. VLA-Forget jointly optimizes three objectives: targeted forgetting, perceptual preservation, and reasoning retention, through staged updates over the visual encoder, projector, and upper action-generating transformer blocks. Across forget-set behavior probes and retain-task evaluations, VLA-Forget improves forgetting efficacy by 10%, preserves perceptual specificity by 22%, retains reasoning and task success by 9%, and reduces post-quantization recovery by 55% relative to strong unlearning baselines.

摘要（中文）: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型正逐渐成为机器人操作中的具身基础模型，但它们的部署引入了新的“遗忘学习”挑战：如何在消除不安全、虚假或隐私敏感行为的同时，不损害感知、语言理解和动作控制能力。在OpenVLA类策略中，行为通过融合的视觉编码器、跨模态投影器和预测分词化机器人动作的语言主干网络生成，因此不良知识可能分散在感知、对齐及推理/动作层中，而非局限于单一模块。这导致仅对视觉模块或仅对语言主干进行部分遗忘学习往往效果不足，而为独立视觉或语言模型设计的传统遗忘基线方法在具身场景中可能残留遗忘痕迹或引发不必要的性能损失。本文提出VLA-Forget混合遗忘框架，结合面向感知与跨模态特性的比率感知选择性编辑技术，以及针对推理/动作层的选择性遗忘机制，实现性能保留的遗忘目标。该框架通过分阶段更新视觉编码器、投影器及上层动作生成Transformer模块，联合优化三个目标：定向遗忘、感知保持和推理保留。在遗忘集行为测试和保留任务评估中，相比现有强遗忘基线，VLA-Forget将遗忘效能提升10%，感知特异性保持提高22%，推理与任务成功率保留提升9%，并将量化后性能恢复率降低55%。

TLDR: Proposes VLA-Forget, a hybrid unlearning framework for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models used in robotic manipulation, to remove unsafe or undesirable behaviors while preserving overall model performance.

简要说明: 提出VLA-Forget，一种用于机器人操控的视觉-语言-行动（VLA）模型的混合式反学习框架，旨在移除不安全或不良行为，同时保持整体模型性能。

Authors: Ravi Ranjan, Agoritsa Polyzou

Primitive-based Truncated Diffusion for Efficient Trajectory Generation of Differential Drive Mobile Manipulators

Abstract (English): We present a learning-enhanced motion planner for differential drive mobile manipulators to improve efficiency, success rate, and optimality. For task representation encoder, we propose a keypoint sequence extraction module that maps boundary states to 3D space via differentiable forward kinematics. Point clouds and keypoints are encoded separately and fused with attention, enabling effective integration of environment and boundary states information. We also propose a primitive-based truncated diffusion model that samples from a biased distribution. Compared with vanilla diffusion model, this framework improves the efficiency and diversity of the solution. Denoised paths are refined by trajectory optimization to ensure dynamic feasibility and task-specific optimality. In cluttered 3D simulations, our method achieves higher success rate, improved trajectory diversity, and competitive runtime compared to vanilla diffusion and classical baselines. The source code is released at https://github.com/nmoma/nmoma .

摘要（中文）: 本文提出一种面向差分驱动移动机械臂的学习增强型运动规划器，旨在提升规划效率、成功率和轨迹最优性。在任务表征编码器方面，我们提出关键点序列提取模块，通过可微分运动学将边界状态映射至三维空间。点云与关键点分别进行编码后通过注意力机制融合，实现环境信息与边界状态的高效整合。此外，我们提出基于运动基元的截断扩散模型，从偏置分布中进行采样。相比传统扩散模型，该框架显著提升求解效率与轨迹多样性。去噪后的路径经由轨迹优化器精细化处理，确保动力学可行性及任务特定最优性。在复杂三维仿真环境中，相较于传统扩散模型与经典基线方法，本方法在成功率、轨迹多样性及运行时间方面均表现出更优性能。源代码发布于 https://github.com/nmoma/nmoma 。

TLDR: This paper presents a learning-based motion planner for differential drive mobile manipulators, using a primitive-based truncated diffusion model to generate diverse and efficient trajectories for robot control.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种用于差速驱动移动机械臂的学习增强型运动规划器，该方法基于截断的原始扩散模型，为机器人控制生成多样且高效的轨迹。

Authors: Long Xu, Choilam Wong, Yuhang Zhong, Junxiao Lin, Jialiang Hou, Fei Gao

Adapting Neural Robot Dynamics on the Fly for Predictive Control

Abstract (English): Accurate dynamics models are critical for the design of predictive controller for autonomous mobile robots. Physics-based models are often too simple to capture relevant real-world effects, while data-driven models are data-intensive and slow to train. We introduce an approach for fast adaptation of neural robot dynamic models that combines offline training with efficient online updates. Our approach learns an incremental neural dynamics model offline and performs low-rank second-order parameter adaptation online, enabling rapid updates without full retraining. We demonstrate the approach on a real quadrotor robot, achieving robust predictive tracking control in novel operational conditions.

摘要（中文）: 精确的动力学模型对于自主移动机器人预测控制器的设计至关重要。基于物理的模型通常过于简化，难以捕捉现实世界中的相关效应，而数据驱动模型则需大量数据且训练缓慢。我们提出一种神经机器人动力学模型快速自适应方法，该方法将离线训练与高效在线更新相结合。通过离线学习增量式神经动力学模型，并在线执行低秩二阶参数自适应，实现了无需完整重新训练的快速模型更新。我们在真实四旋翼机器人平台上验证了该方法，证明其能够在新型操作条件下实现鲁棒的预测跟踪控制。

TLDR: The paper introduces a method for fast online adaptation of neural robot dynamics models to enable robust predictive control, demonstrated on a real quadrotor.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种用于快速在线自适应神经机器人动力学模型的方法，以支持稳健的预测控制，并在真实四旋翼机器人上进行了验证。

Authors: Abdullah Altawaitan, Nikolay Atanasov

DC-Ada: Reward-Only Decentralized Observation-Interface Adaptation for Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Teams

Abstract (English): Heterogeneity is a defining feature of deployed multi-robot teams: platforms often differ in sensing modalities, ranges, fields of view, and failure patterns. Controllers trained under nominal sensing can degrade sharply when deployed on robots with missing or mismatched sensors, even when the task and action interface are unchanged. We present DC-Ada, a reward-only decentralized adaptation method that keeps a pretrained shared policy frozen and instead adapts compact per-robot observation transforms to map heterogeneous sensing into a fixed inference interface. DC-Ada is gradient-free and communication-minimal: it uses budgeted accept/reject random search with short common-random-number rollouts under a strict step budget. We evaluate DC-Ada against four baselines in a deterministic 2D multi-robot simulator covering warehouse logistics, search and rescue, and collaborative mapping, across four heterogeneity regimes (H0--H3) and five seeds with a matched budget of $200{,}000$ joint environment steps per run. Results show that heterogeneity can substantially degrade a frozen shared policy and that no single mitigation dominates across all tasks and metrics. Observation normalization is strongest for reward robustness in warehouse logistics and competitive in search and rescue, while the frozen shared policy is strongest for reward in collaborative mapping. DC-Ada offers a useful complementary operating point: it improves completion most clearly in severe coverage-based mapping while requiring only scalar team returns and no policy fine-tuning or persistent communication. These results position DC-Ada as a practical deploy-time adaptation method for heterogeneous teams.

摘要（中文）: 异构性是已部署多机器人团队的一个决定性特征：不同平台在感知模态、感知范围、视场及故障模式等方面往往存在差异。在标称感知条件下训练的控制策略，当部署于传感器缺失或不匹配的机器人时，即使任务与动作接口保持不变，其性能也可能急剧下降。本文提出DC-Ada——一种仅依赖奖励信号的去中心化适应方法，该方法保持预训练共享策略固定不变，转而通过自适应调整每个机器人的紧凑观测变换，将异构感知映射到统一的推理接口。DC-Ada无需梯度计算且通信开销极低：在严格的步数预算下，采用带预算的接受/拒绝随机搜索策略，并基于短时公共随机数仿真进行评估。我们在确定性二维多机器人仿真环境中将DC-Ada与四种基线方法进行对比，实验涵盖仓储物流、搜索救援和协作建图三类任务，包含四种异构模式（H0-H3）及五次随机种子运行，每次运行均采用20万次联合环境步数的匹配预算。结果表明：异构性会显著降低固定共享策略的性能，且没有任何一种缓解策略能在所有任务和指标上全面占优。观测归一化在仓储物流任务中对奖励鲁棒性提升最为显著，在搜索救援任务中亦具竞争力；而固定共享策略在协作建图任务中奖励表现最佳。DC-Ada提供了一个有效的补充解决方案：在严重依赖覆盖度的建图任务中能最显著提升任务完成率，同时仅需标量团队回报，无需策略微调或持续通信。这些结果确立了DC-Ada作为异构机器人团队实用化部署时适应方法的可行性。

TLDR: The paper proposes a reward-only decentralized adaptation method (DC-Ada) for multi-robot teams with heterogeneous sensors. It keeps a shared policy frozen and instead adapts per-robot observation transforms using a gradient-free random search, showing improved task completion in simulation.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种基于奖励的去中心化适应方法（DC-Ada），用于解决多机器人系统中的传感器异构问题。该方法将预训练的共享策略固定，转而调整每个机器人的观测转换器，使用无需梯度的随机搜索，在仿真中提升了任务完成度。

Authors: Saad Alqithami

From Prompt to Physical Action: Structured Backdoor Attacks on LLM-Mediated Robotic Control Systems

Abstract (English): The integration of large language models (LLMs) into robotic control pipelines enables natural language interfaces that translate user prompts into executable commands. However, this digital-to-physical interface introduces a critical and underexplored vulnerability: structured backdoor attacks embedded during fine-tuning. In this work, we experimentally investigate LoRA-based supply-chain backdoors in LLM-mediated ROS2 robotic control systems and evaluate their impact on physical robot execution. We construct two poisoned fine-tuning strategies targeting different stages of the command generation pipeline and reveal a key systems-level insight: back-doors embedded at the natural-language reasoning stage do not reliably propagate to executable control outputs, whereas backdoors aligned directly with structured JSON command formats successfully survive translation and trigger physical actions. In both simulation and real-world experiments, backdoored models achieve an average Attack Success Rate of 83% while maintaining over 93% Clean Performance Accuracy (CPA) and sub-second latency, demonstrating both reliability and stealth. We further implement an agentic verification defense using a secondary LLM for semantic consistency checking. Although this reduces the Attack Success Rate (ASR) to 20%, it increases end-to-end latency to 8-9 seconds, exposing a significant security-responsiveness trade-off in real-time robotic systems. These results highlight structural vulnerabilities in LLM-mediated robotic control architectures and underscore the need for robotics-aware defenses for embodied AI systems.

摘要（中文）: 将大型语言模型（LLM）集成到机器人控制流程中，实现了自然语言接口，能够将用户指令转化为可执行命令。然而，这种数字到物理的接口引入了一个关键且尚未被充分探索的漏洞：即微调过程中嵌入的结构化后门攻击。本研究通过实验，探究了基于LoRA的供应链后门在LLM介导的ROS2机器人控制系统中的表现，并评估了其对物理机器人执行的影响。我们构建了两种针对指令生成流程不同阶段的中毒微调策略，并揭示了一个关键的系统级发现：嵌入在自然语言推理阶段的后门无法可靠地传播至可执行控制输出，而与结构化JSON命令格式直接对齐的后门则能成功存活于翻译过程并触发物理动作。在仿真与真实世界实验中，后门模型的平均攻击成功率可达83%，同时保持超过93%的清洁性能准确率（CPA）及亚秒级延迟，展现出可靠性与隐蔽性。我们进一步实现了一种基于代理验证的防御机制，利用次级LLM进行语义一致性检查。尽管该机制将攻击成功率降至20%，但端到端延迟增加至8-9秒，这暴露了实时机器人系统中安全性与响应性之间显著的权衡关系。这些结果凸显了LLM介导的机器人控制架构存在的结构性漏洞，并强调了具身AI系统需要具备机器人感知能力的防御措施。

TLDR: This paper demonstrates how structured backdoor attacks can be embedded during the fine-tuning of LLMs used for robotic control, successfully triggering unintended physical actions while maintaining normal operation, and reveals a trade-off between security and latency when using LLM-based verification defenses.

简要说明: 本研究表明，在用于机器人控制的大语言模型微调过程中植入结构化后门攻击，可以成功触发非预期的物理动作，同时保持正常操作性能，并揭示了使用大语言模型进行验证防御时在安全性与延迟之间存在的权衡。

Authors: Mingyang Xie, Jin Wei-Kocsis

Hypothesis Graph Refinement: Hypothesis-Driven Exploration with Cascade Error Correction for Embodied Navigation

Abstract (English): Embodied agents must explore partially observed environments while maintaining reliable long-horizon memory. Existing graph-based navigation systems improve scalability, but they often treat unexplored regions as semantically unknown, leading to inefficient frontier search. Although vision-language models (VLMs) can predict frontier semantics, erroneous predictions may be embedded into memory and propagate through downstream inferences, causing structural error accumulation that confidence attenuation alone cannot resolve. These observations call for a framework that can leverage semantic predictions for directed exploration while systematically retracting errors once new evidence contradicts them. We propose Hypothesis Graph Refinement (HGR), a framework that represents frontier predictions as revisable hypothesis nodes in a dependency-aware graph memory. HGR introduces (1) semantic hypothesis module, which estimates context-conditioned semantic distributions over frontiers and ranks exploration targets by goal relevance, travel cost, and uncertainty, and (2) verification-driven cascade correction, which compares on-site observations against predicted semantics and, upon mismatch, retracts the refuted node together with all its downstream dependents. Unlike additive map-building, this allows the graph to contract by pruning erroneous subgraphs, keeping memory reliable throughout long episodes. We evaluate HGR on multimodal lifelong navigation (GOAT-Bench) and embodied question answering (A-EQA, EM-EQA). HGR achieves 72.41% success rate and 56.22% SPL on GOAT-Bench, and shows consistent improvements on both QA benchmarks. Diagnostic analysis reveals that cascade correction eliminates approximately 20% of structurally redundant hypothesis nodes and reduces revisits to erroneous regions by 4.5x, with specular and transparent surfaces accounting for 67% of corrected prediction errors.

摘要（中文）: 具身智能体必须在部分观测环境中进行探索，同时保持可靠的长期记忆。现有基于图谱的导航系统提升了可扩展性，但往往将未探索区域视为语义未知区域，导致前沿搜索效率低下。尽管视觉语言模型能够预测前沿语义，但错误的预测可能被嵌入记忆并向下游推理传播，引发单一置信度衰减无法解决的结构性错误累积。这些现象亟需一种能够利用语义预测进行定向探索、同时在新证据出现时系统性修正错误的框架。我们提出假设图谱优化框架，该框架将前沿预测表示为依赖感知图谱记忆中的可修订假设节点。该框架包含两个核心机制：（1）语义假设模块，通过上下文条件化语义分布评估前沿区域，并依据目标关联性、移动成本与不确定性对探索目标进行排序；（2）验证驱动的级联修正机制，通过实地观测与预测语义的比对，在发现不一致时撤回被证伪节点及其所有下游依赖节点。区别于传统的增量式地图构建方法，该框架能通过剪除错误子图实现图谱收缩，从而在长期任务中保持记忆可靠性。我们在多模态终身导航基准与具身问答任务上评估该框架，其在终身导航基准上获得72.41%的成功率与56.22%的路径长度加权成功率，在两项问答基准上均取得持续性能提升。诊断分析表明，级联修正机制能消除约20%的结构冗余假设节点，并将错误区域重访率降低至原来的1/4.5，其中镜面与透明表面导致的预测错误占修正总量的67%。

TLDR: Proposes Hypothesis Graph Refinement (HGR), a framework for embodied navigation that uses revisable semantic hypotheses and cascade error correction to improve long-horizon exploration and memory reliability.

简要说明: 提出假设图精炼（HGR）框架，用于具身导航，通过可修正的语义假设和级联错误校正，提升长期探索和记忆的可靠性。

Authors: Peixin Chen, Guoxi Zhang, Jianwei Ma, Qing Li

Optimization-Free Constrained Control with Guaranteed Recursive Feasibility: A CBF-Based Reference Governor Approach

Abstract (English): This letter presents a constrained control framework that integrates Explicit Reference Governors (ERG) with Control Barrier Functions (CBF) to ensure recursive feasibility without online optimization. We formulate the reference update as a virtual control input for an augmented system, governed by a smooth barrier function constructed from the softmin aggregation of Dynamic Safety Margins (DSMs). Unlike standard CBF formulations, the proposed method guarantees the feasibility of safety constraints by design, exploiting the forward invariance properties of the underlying Lyapunov level sets. This allows for the derivation of an explicit, closed-form reference update law that strictly enforces safety while minimizing deviation from a nominal reference trajectory. Theoretical results confirm asymptotic convergence, and numerical simulations demonstrate that the proposed method achieves performance comparable to traditional ERG frameworks.

摘要（中文）: 本文提出一种将显式参考规划器(ERG)与控制屏障函数(CBF)相结合的限制性控制框架，该框架无需在线优化即可确保递归可行性。我们将参考更新构建为增广系统的虚拟控制输入，其控制律由动态安全裕度(DSM)的软最小值聚合构造的光滑屏障函数所决定。与标准CBF方法不同，所提方案通过利用李雅普诺夫水平集的前向不变性，在系统设计层面保证了安全约束的可行性。该方法可推导出显式闭形式的参考更新律，在严格保证安全性的同时最小化对名义参考轨迹的偏离。理论结果证实了该方法的渐近收敛性，数值仿真表明所提方案能达到与传统ERG框架相当的性能表现。

TLDR: The paper proposes a constrained control framework integrating Explicit Reference Governors and Control Barrier Functions to guarantee recursive feasibility and safety without online optimization, using a closed-form reference update law.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种结合显式参考调节器和控制障碍函数的约束控制框架，保证无需在线优化的递归可行性和安全性，采用闭式参考更新律。

Authors: Satoshi Nakano, Emanuele Garone, Gennaro Notomista

DriveVA: Video Action Models are Zero-Shot Drivers

Abstract (English): Generalization is a central challenge in autonomous driving, as real-world deployment requires robust performance under unseen scenarios, sensor domains, and environmental conditions. Recent world-model-based planning methods have shown strong capabilities in scene understanding and multi-modal future prediction, yet their generalization across datasets and sensor configurations remains limited. In addition, their loosely coupled planning paradigm often leads to poor video-trajectory consistency during visual imagination. To overcome these limitations, we propose DriveVA, a novel autonomous driving world model that jointly decodes future visual forecasts and action sequences in a shared latent generative process. DriveVA inherits rich priors on motion dynamics and physical plausibility from well-pretrained large-scale video generation models to capture continuous spatiotemporal evolution and causal interaction patterns. To this end, DriveVA employs a DiT-based decoder to jointly predict future action sequences (trajectories) and videos, enabling tighter alignment between planning and scene evolution. We also introduce a video continuation strategy to strengthen long-duration rollout consistency. DriveVA achieves an impressive closed-loop performance of 90.9 PDM score on the challenge NAVSIM. Extensive experiments also demonstrate the zero-shot capability and cross-domain generalization of DriveVA, which reduces average L2 error and collision rate by 78.9% and 83.3% on nuScenes and 52.5% and 52.4% on the Bench2drive built on CARLA v2 compared with the state-of-the-art world-model-based planner.

摘要（中文）: 泛化能力是自动驾驶领域的核心挑战，因为现实世界的部署需要在未见场景、传感器域和环境条件下保持稳健性能。近期基于世界模型的规划方法在场景理解和多模态未来预测方面展现出强大能力，但其跨数据集与传感器配置的泛化能力仍显不足。此外，这些方法松耦合的规划范式常导致视觉想象过程中视频轨迹一致性较差。为克服这些局限，我们提出DriveVA——一种新型自动驾驶世界模型，通过在共享潜在生成过程中联合解码未来视觉预测与动作序列。DriveVA从预训练的大规模视频生成模型中继承了丰富的运动动力学和物理合理性先验知识，以捕捉连续时空演变与因果交互模式。为此，DriveVA采用基于DiT的解码器联合预测未来动作序列（轨迹）与视频，实现规划与场景演进更紧密的对齐。我们还引入了视频延续策略以增强长时推演一致性。在NAVSIM挑战中，DriveVA实现了90.9 PDM分数的卓越闭环性能。大量实验验证了DriveVA的零样本能力与跨域泛化性：相比当前最先进的基于世界模型的规划器，其在nuScenes数据集上将平均L2误差与碰撞率分别降低78.9%和83.3%，在基于CARLA v2构建的Bench2drive数据集上分别降低52.5%和52.4%。

TLDR: The paper introduces DriveVA, an autonomous driving world model that jointly predicts future videos and action sequences for improved generalization and planning consistency.

简要说明: 该论文提出了DriveVA，一种自动驾驶世界模型，它联合预测未来视频和动作序列，以提升泛化能力和规划一致性。

Authors: Mengmeng Liu, Diankun Zhang, Jiuming Liu, Jianfeng Cui, Hongwei Xie, Guang Chen, Hangjun Ye, Michael Ying Yang, Francesco Nex, Hao Cheng

Robots Need Some Education: On the complexity of learning in evolutionary robotics

Abstract (English): Evolutionary Robotics and Robot Learning are two fields in robotics that aim to automatically optimize robot designs. The key difference between them lies in what is being optimized and the time scale involved. Evolutionary Robotics is a field that applies evolutionary computation techniques to evolve the morphologies or controllers, or both. Robot Learning, on the other hand, involves any learning technique aimed at optimizing a robot's controller in a given morphology. In terms of time scales, evolution occurs across multiple generations, whereas learning takes place within the `lifespan' of an individual robot. Integrating Robot Learning with Evolutionary Robotics requires the careful design of suitable learning algorithms in the context of evolutionary robotics. The effects of introducing learning into the evolutionary process are not well-understood and can thus be tricky. This thesis investigates these intricacies and presents several learning algorithms developed for an Evolutionary Robotics context.

摘要（中文）: 进化机器人和机器人学习是机器人学中旨在自动优化机器人设计的两个领域。它们之间的关键区别在于优化的对象及所涉及的时间尺度。进化机器人学是应用进化计算技术来优化机器人形态、控制器或两者兼具的领域。而机器人学习则涵盖任何旨在给定形态下优化机器人控制器的学习技术。在时间尺度上，进化发生在多代之间，而学习则发生在单个机器人的“生命周期”内。将机器人学习与进化机器人学相结合，需要在进化机器人学的框架内精心设计合适的学习算法。在进化过程中引入学习的效果尚不明确，因此可能带来复杂挑战。本研究探讨了这些复杂问题，并提出了数种针对进化机器人学背景开发的学习算法。

TLDR: This thesis investigates the intricacies of integrating robot learning techniques (like optimizing controllers) into evolutionary robotics (which evolves morphologies/controllers), aiming to better understand and develop algorithms for this combined approach.

简要说明: 本论文研究将机器人学习（如优化控制器）整合到进化机器人学（进化形态/控制器）中的复杂性，旨在更好地理解并为这种组合方法开发算法。

Authors: Fuda van Diggelen

Provable Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning: A Representation Learning Framework with Low Rank Rewards

Abstract (English): Multi-task representation learning (MTRL) is an approach that learns shared latent representations across related tasks, facilitating collaborative learning that improves the overall learning efficiency. This paper studies MTRL for multi-task reinforcement learning (RL), where multiple tasks have the same state-action space and transition probabilities, but different rewards. We consider T linear Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) where the reward functions and transition dynamics admit linear feature embeddings of dimension d. The relatedness among the tasks is captured by a low-rank structure on the reward matrices. Learning shared representations across multiple RL tasks is challenging due to the complex and policy-dependent nature of data that leads to a temporal progression of error. Our approach adopts a reward-free reinforcement learning framework to first learn a data-collection policy. This policy then informs an exploration strategy for estimating the unknown reward matrices. Importantly, the data collected under this well-designed policy enable accurate estimation, which ultimately supports the learning of an near-optimal policy. Unlike existing approaches that rely on restrictive assumptions such as Gaussian features, incoherence conditions, or access to optimal solutions, we propose a low-rank matrix estimation method that operates under more general feature distributions encountered in RL settings. Theoretical analysis establishes that accurate low-rank matrix recovery is achievable under these relaxed assumptions, and we characterize the relationship between representation error and sample complexity. Leveraging the learned representation, we construct near-optimal policies and prove a regret bound. Experimental results demonstrate that our method effectively learns robust shared representations and task dynamics from finite data.

摘要（中文）: 多任务表示学习（MTRL）是一种通过跨相关任务学习共享潜在表示的方法，它促进了协同学习，从而提升了整体学习效率。本文针对多任务强化学习（RL）中的MTRL展开研究，其中多个任务具有相同的状态-动作空间与转移概率，但奖励函数不同。我们考虑T个线性马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），其奖励函数与转移动力学允许维度为d的线性特征嵌入，任务间的关联性通过奖励矩阵的低秩结构来刻画。由于数据具有复杂且依赖于策略的特性，导致误差随时间递进，在多任务RL中学习共享表示面临挑战。我们的方法采用无奖励强化学习框架，首先学习数据收集策略，该策略随后为估计未知奖励矩阵的探索策略提供依据。关键之处在于，这一精心设计的策略所收集的数据能够实现准确估计，最终支持学习近似最优策略。与现有方法依赖高斯特征、不相干条件或获取最优解等限制性假设不同，我们提出了一种低秩矩阵估计方法，适用于RL环境中更一般的特征分布。理论分析表明，在这些放宽的假设下可以实现准确的低秩矩阵恢复，并刻画了表示误差与样本复杂度之间的关系。利用学习到的表示，我们构建了近似最优策略，并证明了遗憾界。实验结果表明，我们的方法能够有效从有限数据中学习到鲁棒的共享表示与任务动态。

TLDR: The paper proposes a provable multi-task reinforcement learning method for linear MDPs with low-rank rewards, using a reward-free exploration strategy to learn shared representations and near-optimal policies.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种用于具有低秩奖励的线性MDPs的可证明的多任务强化学习方法，利用无奖励探索策略学习共享表示和近最优策略。

Authors: Yaoze Guo, Shana Moothedath

DINO-VO: Learning Where to Focus for Enhanced State Estimation

Abstract (English): We present DINO Patch Visual Odometry (DINO-VO), an end-to-end monocular visual odometry system with strong scene generalization. Current Visual Odometry (VO) systems often rely on heuristic feature extraction strategies, which can degrade accuracy and robustness, particularly in large-scale outdoor environments. DINO-VO addresses these limitations by incorporating a differentiable adaptive patch selector into the end-to-end pipeline, improving the quality of extracted patches and enhancing generalization across diverse datasets. Additionally, our system integrates a multi-task feature extraction module with a differentiable bundle adjustment (BA) module that leverages inverse depth priors, enabling the system to learn and utilize appearance and geometric information effectively. This integration bridges the gap between feature learning and state estimation. Extensive experiments on the TartanAir, KITTI, Euroc, and TUM datasets demonstrate that DINO-VO exhibits strong generalization across synthetic, indoor, and outdoor environments, achieving state-of-the-art tracking accuracy.

摘要（中文）: 本文提出了DINO-VO，一种具有强大场景泛化能力的端到端单目视觉里程计系统。当前视觉里程计系统通常依赖于启发式特征提取策略，这可能导致精度和鲁棒性下降，特别是在大规模户外环境中。DINO-VO通过在端到端流程中引入可微分自适应局部块选择器，提升了特征提取的质量，并增强了跨数据集的泛化能力。此外，系统结合了多任务特征提取模块与基于逆深度先验的可微分光束法平差模块，能够有效学习并利用外观和几何信息。这种集成弥合了特征学习与状态估计之间的鸿沟。在TartanAir、KITTI、Euroc和TUM数据集上的大量实验表明，DINO-VO在合成、室内和室外环境中均表现出卓越的泛化性能，达到了当前最优的轨迹跟踪精度。

TLDR: DINO-VO is a monocular visual odometry system that uses a differentiable adaptive patch selector and a multi-task feature extraction module with differentiable bundle adjustment to improve accuracy and generalization across diverse scenes.

Authors: Qi Chen, Guanghao Li, Sijia Hu, Xin Gao, Junpeng Ma, Xiangyang Xue, Jian Pu

Comparative reversal learning reveals rigid adaptation in LLMs under non-stationary uncertainty

Abstract (English): Non-stationary environments require agents to revise previously learned action values when contingencies change. We treat large language models (LLMs) as sequential decision policies in a two-option probabilistic reversal-learning task with three latent states and switch events triggered by either a performance criterion or timeout. We compare a deterministic fixed transition cycle to a stochastic random schedule that increases volatility, and evaluate DeepSeek-V3.2, Gemini-3, and GPT-5.2, with human data as a behavioural reference. Across models, win-stay was near ceiling while lose-shift was markedly attenuated, revealing asymmetric use of positive versus negative evidence. DeepSeek-V3.2 showed extreme perseveration after reversals and weak acquisition, whereas Gemini-3 and GPT-5.2 adapted more rapidly but still remained less loss-sensitive than humans. Random transitions amplified reversal-specific persistence across LLMs yet did not uniformly reduce total wins, demonstrating that high aggregate payoff can coexist with rigid adaptation. Hierarchical reinforcement-learning (RL) fits indicate dissociable mechanisms: rigidity can arise from weak loss learning, inflated policy determinism, or value polarisation via counterfactual suppression. These results motivate reversal-sensitive diagnostics and volatility-aware models for evaluating LLMs under non-stationary uncertainty.

摘要（中文）: 非稳态环境要求智能体在突发情况变化时修正先前习得的行动价值。本研究将大语言模型视为序列决策策略，在包含三个潜在状态的双选项概率反转学习任务中展开实验，切换事件由性能标准或超时机制触发。我们比较了确定性固定转换周期与增加波动性的随机调度方案，并以人类行为数据为参照基准，评估了DeepSeek-V3.2、Gemini-3和GPT-5.2的表现。所有模型均展现出接近上限的"赢则守成"策略，而"输则转变"策略显著弱化，揭示了对正负反馈证据的不对称运用。DeepSeek-V3.2在反转后表现出极端持续性且学习能力较弱，而Gemini-3和GPT-5.2适应更快但仍较人类损失敏感性不足。随机转换机制强化了大语言模型的反转特异性持续现象，但并未普遍降低总胜率，表明高累计收益可与僵化适应模式共存。分层强化学习拟合结果表明存在可分离机制：行为僵化可能源于弱损失学习、政策确定性膨胀或通过反事实抑制产生的价值极化。这些发现启示应当建立反转敏感性诊断框架和波动感知模型，以评估非稳态不确定性下的大语言模型表现。

TLDR: This paper evaluates LLMs like DeepSeek, Gemini, and GPT in a reversal learning task, analyzing their adaptation under non-stationary uncertainty. It focuses on AI decision-making rigidity, not robotics or embodied control, but offers insights into sequential decision-making policies relevant to potential agent architectures.

简要说明: 本文在反转学习任务中评估大型语言模型，分析其在非稳态不确定性下的适应性，聚焦于AI决策的僵化机制，而非机器人或具身控制，但对智能体架构的序列决策具有潜在参考价值。

Authors: Haomiaomiao Wang, Tomás E Ward, Lili Zhang

Profile-Then-Reason: Bounded Semantic Complexity for Tool-Augmented Language Agents

Abstract (English): Large language model agents that use external tools are often implemented through reactive execution, in which reasoning is repeatedly recomputed after each observation, increasing latency and sensitivity to error propagation. This work introduces Profile--Then--Reason (PTR), a bounded execution framework for structured tool-augmented reasoning, in which a language model first synthesizes an explicit workflow, deterministic or guarded operators execute that workflow, a verifier evaluates the resulting trace, and repair is invoked only when the original workflow is no longer reliable. A mathematical formulation is developed in which the full pipeline is expressed as a composition of profile, routing, execution, verification, repair, and reasoning operators; under bounded repair, the number of language-model calls is restricted to two in the nominal case and three in the worst case. Experiments against a ReAct baseline on six benchmarks and four language models show that PTR achieves the pairwise exact-match advantage in 16 of 24 configurations. The results indicate that PTR is particularly effective on retrieval-centered and decomposition-heavy tasks, whereas reactive execution remains preferable when success depends on substantial online adaptation.

摘要（中文）: 摘要：使用外部工具的大型语言模型代理通常通过反应式执行实现，即在每次观察后反复重新计算推理过程，这增加了延迟且容易受到错误传播的影响。本研究提出了一种面向结构化工具增强推理的有限执行框架——先规划后执行（PTR）。该框架中，语言模型首先合成显式工作流，随后由确定性或受保护的运算符执行该工作流，验证器对执行轨迹进行评估，仅当原始工作流不可靠时才触发修复机制。我们建立了数学形式化模型，将完整流程表述为规划、路由、执行、验证、修复和推理算子的组合；在有限修复条件下，名义情况下语言模型调用次数限制为两次，最坏情况下不超过三次。通过在六个基准测试和四种语言模型上与ReAct基线进行对比实验，PTR在24种配置中的16种实现了成对精确匹配优势。结果表明，PTR在检索密集型及分解密集型任务中表现尤为突出，而当任务成功依赖于大量在线适应性调整时，反应式执行仍更具优势。

TLDR: This paper introduces Profile-Then-Reason (PTR), a framework for tool-augmented language agents that creates a workflow first to reduce latency and error propagation, outperforming reactive baselines on retrieval and decomposition tasks.

简要说明: 本文提出了Profile-Then-Reason（PTR），一种工具增强语言代理框架，其先合成工作流以降低延迟和错误传播，在检索和分解任务上优于反应式基线。

Authors: Paulo Akira F. Enabe

HOIGS: Human-Object Interaction Gaussian Splatting

Abstract (English): Reconstructing dynamic scenes with complex human-object interactions is a fundamental challenge in computer vision and graphics. Existing Gaussian Splatting methods either rely on human pose priors while neglecting dynamic objects, or approximate all motions within a single field, limiting their ability to capture interaction-rich dynamics. To address this gap, we propose Human-Object Interaction Gaussian Splatting (HOIGS), which explicitly models interaction-induced deformation between humans and objects through a cross-attention-based HOI module. Distinct deformation baselines are employed to extract features: HexPlane for humans and Cubic Hermite Spline (CHS) for objects. By integrating these heterogeneous features, HOIGS effectively captures interdependent motions and improves deformation estimation in scenarios involving occlusion, contact, and object manipulation. Comprehensive experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art human-centric and 4D Gaussian approaches, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling human-object interactions for high-fidelity reconstruction.

摘要（中文）: 重建包含复杂人-物交互的动态场景是计算机视觉与图形学领域的基础性挑战。现有高斯溅射方法或依赖人体姿态先验而忽略动态物体，或将所有运动近似于单一场内，限制了其捕捉交互密集型动态的能力。为弥补这一空白，我们提出人-物交互高斯溅射（HOIGS），该方法通过基于交叉注意力机制的HOI模块显式建模人与人、人与物之间的交互引发形变。系统采用差异化形变基线提取特征：人体采用HexPlane表示，物体采用三次厄米特样条（CHS）表示。通过整合这些异构特征，HOIGS能有效捕捉相互依赖的运动，并在涉及遮挡、接触及物体操控的场景中提升形变估计精度。在多个数据集上的综合实验表明，我们的方法在人体中心化与四维高斯重建任务中均持续优于现有先进方法，凸显了显式建模人-物交互对实现高保真重建的重要价值。

TLDR: HOIGS models dynamic human-object interactions for 3D scene reconstruction using Gaussian Splatting and a novel cross-attention module. It combines HexPlane and spline-based deformation models to improve reconstruction fidelity in complex interactive scenarios.

简要说明: HOIGS通过高斯泼溅和一个新颖的交叉注意力模块，对动态人-物交互进行3D场景重建。它结合了HexPlane和样条基变形模型，以提升复杂交互场景下的重建保真度。

Authors: Taewoo Kim, Suwoong Yeom, Jaehyun Pyun, Geonho Cha, Dongyoon Wee, Joonsik Nam, Yun-Seong Jeong, Kyeongbo Kong, Suk-Ju Kang

TraceGuard: Structured Multi-Dimensional Monitoring as a Collusion-Resistant Control Protocol

Abstract (English): AI control protocols use monitors to detect attacks by untrusted AI agents, but standard single-score monitors face two limitations: they miss subtle attacks where outputs look clean but reasoning is off, and they collapse to near-zero safety when the monitor is the same model as the agent (collusion). We present TraceGuard, a structured multi-dimensional monitoring protocol that evaluates agent actions across five dimensions -- goal alignment, constraint adherence, reasoning coherence, safety awareness, and action-trace consistency -- scored in parallel by independent LLM calls, augmented by seven heuristic detectors and an LLM-based intent analyzer. We evaluate on BashArena (637 bash tasks, 4 attack categories) within the ControlArena framework. Our results on 519 samples (279 honest, 240 attack) show that: (1) the hybrid approach achieves clear attack-honest separation (attack mean 0.616 vs. honest mean 0.206, Delta=0.410); (2) structured scoring constrains collusion -- the untrusted structured monitor achieves 95% safety vs. 0% for single-score untrusted monitoring; (3) goal alignment and constraint adherence are the most discriminative dimensions; and (4) a separation-of-duties variant splitting dimensions across trusted and untrusted models achieves 100% safety while preventing any single model from seeing the full evaluation. TraceGuard is implemented as a new monitor type for the open-source ControlArena framework.

摘要（中文）: 人工智能控制协议通过监控器检测不可信AI代理的攻击，但传统单维评分监控存在两大局限：既会遗漏那些输出看似正常但推理过程存在偏差的微妙攻击，又会在监控器与代理采用相同模型时（共谋场景）使安全性趋近于零。本文提出TraceGuard——一种结构化多维监控协议，通过五个并行维度评估代理行为：目标对齐度、约束遵循度、推理连贯性、安全认知度及行为轨迹一致性，各维度由独立大语言模型调用并行评分，并辅以七种启发式检测器与基于大语言模型的意图分析器。我们在ControlArena框架内的BashArena环境（637项bash任务，4类攻击）进行评估。对519个样本（279个诚实样本，240个攻击样本）的实验结果表明：（1）混合方法实现显著的攻击-诚实样本分离（攻击样本均值0.616 vs 诚实样本均值0.206，Δ=0.410）；（2）结构化评分机制有效抑制共谋——不可信结构化监控器实现95%安全性，而单维评分不可信监控的安全性为0%；（3）目标对齐度与约束遵循度成为最具判别力的维度；（4）职责分离变体将各维度分配给可信与不可信模型分别评估，在实现100%安全性的同时确保任何单一模型都无法获取完整评估信息。TraceGuard已作为新型监控器集成至开源ControlArena框架。

TLDR: TraceGuard is a collusion-resistant AI control protocol using structured multi-dimensional monitoring of agent actions, evaluated in a bash terminal task environment.

简要说明: TraceGuard 是一种抗共谋的 AI 控制协议，通过在 bash 终端任务环境中对智能体行为进行结构化多维度监控来实现。

Authors: Khanh Linh Nguyen, Hoa Nghiem, Tu Tran

Interpreting Video Representations with Spatio-Temporal Sparse Autoencoders

Abstract (English): We present the first systematic study of Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) on video representations. Standard SAEs decompose video into interpretable, monosemantic features but destroy temporal coherence: hard TopK selection produces unstable feature assignments across frames, reducing autocorrelation by 36%. We propose spatio-temporal contrastive objectives and Matryoshka hierarchical grouping that recover and even exceed raw temporal coherence. The contrastive loss weight controls a tunable trade-off between reconstruction and temporal coherence. A systematic ablation on two backbones and two datasets shows that different configurations excel at different goals: reconstruction fidelity, temporal coherence, action discrimination, or interpretability. Contrastive SAE features improve action classification by +3.9% over raw features and text-video retrieval by up to 2.8xR@1. A cross-backbone analysis reveals that standard monosemanticity metrics contain a backbone-alignment artifact: both DINOv2 and VideoMAE produce equally monosemantic features under neutral (CLIP) similarity. Causal ablation confirms that contrastive training concentrates predictive signal into a small number of identifiable features.

摘要（中文）: 我们首次对视频表征中的稀疏自编码器(SAE)进行了系统研究。传统SAE能够将视频分解为可解释的单语义特征，但会破坏时间连贯性：硬性TopK选择导致跨帧特征分配不稳定，使自相关降低36%。我们提出时空对比目标与俄罗斯套娃式层级分组方法，不仅恢复甚至超越了原始的时间连贯性。对比损失权重可调节重建精度与时间连贯性之间的平衡。在两种骨干网络和两个数据集上的系统性消融实验表明，不同配置在不同目标上表现卓越：重建保真度、时间连贯性、动作区分度或可解释性。相比原始特征，对比SAE特征将动作分类准确率提升3.9%，文本-视频检索R@1指标最高提升2.8倍。跨骨干网络分析揭示，传统单语义性度量存在骨干网络对齐伪影：在中性(CLIP)相似度下，DINOv2和VideoMAE均能生成同等单语义性的特征。因果消融实验证实，对比训练能将预测信号集中到少量可识别特征中。

TLDR: The paper introduces a method to improve the temporal coherence of Sparse Autoencoders on video representations using contrastive objectives, enhancing action classification and text-video retrieval.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种改进视频表示中稀疏自编码器时间一致性的方法，通过对比目标增强动作分类和文本-视频检索性能。

Authors: Atahan Dokme, Sriram Vishwanath

A Physics-Informed, Behavior-Aware Digital Twin for Robust Multimodal Forecasting of Core Body Temperature in Precision Livestock Farming

Abstract (English): Precision livestock farming requires accurate and timely heat stress prediction to ensure animal welfare and optimize farm management. This study presents a physics-informed digital twin (DT) framework combined with an uncertainty-aware, expert-weighted stacked ensemble for multimodal forecasting of Core Body Temperature (CBT) in dairy cattle. Using the high-frequency, heterogeneous MmCows dataset, the DT integrates an ordinary differential equation (ODE)-based thermoregulation model that simulates metabolic heat production and dissipation, a Gaussian process for capturing cow-specific deviations, a Kalman filter for aligning predictions with real-time sensor data, and a behavioral Markov chain that models activity-state transitions under varying environmental conditions. The DT outputs key physiological indicators, such as predicted CBT, heat stress probability, and behavioral state distributions are fused with raw sensor data and enriched through multi-scale temporal analysis and cross-modal feature engineering to form a comprehensive feature set. The predictive methodology is designed in a three-stage stacked ensemble, where stage 1 trains modality-specific LightGBM 'expert' models on distinct feature groups, stage 2 collects their predictions as meta-features, and at stage 3 Optuna-tuned LightGBM meta-model yields the final CBT forecast. Predictive uncertainty is quantified via bootstrapping and validated using Prediction Interval Coverage Probability (PICP). Ablation analysis confirms that incorporating DT-derived features and multimodal fusion substantially enhances performance. The proposed framework achieves a cross-validated R2 of 0.783, F1 score of 84.25% and PICP of 92.38% for 2-hour ahead forecasting, providing a robust, uncertainty-aware, and physically principled system for early heat stress detection and precision livestock management.

摘要（中文）: 精准畜牧业需要准确及时的热应激预测，以确保动物福利并优化养殖管理。本研究提出了一种物理信息数字孪生（DT）框架，结合不确定性感知的专家加权堆叠集成方法，用于奶牛核心体温（CBT）的多模态预测。该框架基于高频异构的MmCows数据集，数字孪生整合了基于常微分方程（ODE）的热调节模型以模拟代谢产热与散热、用于捕捉个体偏差的高斯过程、基于卡尔曼滤波器的实时传感器数据对齐机制，以及模拟动态环境条件下行为状态转换的马尔可夫链。数字孪生输出的关键生理指标（如预测CBT、热应激概率和行为状态分布）与原始传感器数据融合，并通过多尺度时间分析和跨模态特征工程构建出综合特征集。预测方法采用三阶段堆叠集成设计：第一阶段训练基于不同特征组的模态专用LightGBM“专家”模型；第二阶段收集其预测结果作为元特征；第三阶段通过Optuna优化的LightGBM元模型生成最终CBT预测。预测不确定性通过自助法进行量化，并使用预测区间覆盖概率（PICP）进行验证。消融分析证实，结合数字孪生特征与多模态融合能显著提升预测性能。该框架在提前2小时的预测中实现了交叉验证R²为0.783、F1分数达84.25%、PICP为92.38%的指标，为早期热应激检测和精准畜牧管理提供了一套稳健、不确定性感知且符合物理原理的系统。

TLDR: This paper presents a physics-informed digital twin framework with an uncertainty-aware ensemble model for forecasting dairy cattle core body temperature to predict heat stress, using a multimodal sensor dataset and integrating thermoregulation models with behavioral state analysis.

Authors: Riasad Alvi, Mohaimenul Azam Khan Raiaan, Sadia Sultana Chowa, Arefin Ittesafun Abian, Reem E Mohamed, Md Rafiqul Islam, Yakub Sebastian, Sheikh Izzal Azid, Sami Azam

Hierarchical Point-Patch Fusion with Adaptive Patch Codebook for 3D Shape Anomaly Detection

Abstract (English): 3D shape anomaly detection is a crucial task for industrial inspection and geometric analysis. Existing deep learning approaches typically learn representations of normal shapes and identify anomalies via out-of-distribution feature detection or decoder-based reconstruction. They often fail to generalize across diverse anomaly types and scales, such as global geometric errors (e.g., planar shifts, angle misalignments), and are sensitive to noisy or incomplete local points during training. To address these limitations, we propose a hierarchical point-patch anomaly scoring network that jointly models regional part features and local point features for robust anomaly reasoning. An adaptive patchification module integrates self-supervised decomposition to capture complex structural deviations. Beyond evaluations on public benchmarks (Anomaly-ShapeNet and Real3D-AD), we release an industrial test set with real CAD models exhibiting planar, angular, and structural defects. Experiments on public and industrial datasets show superior AUC-ROC and AUC-PR performance, including over 40% point-level improvement on the new industrial anomaly type and average object-level gains of 7% on Real3D-AD and 4% on Anomaly-ShapeNet, demonstrating strong robustness and generalization.

摘要（中文）: 三维形状异常检测是工业检测与几何分析中的关键任务。现有深度学习方法通常学习正常形状的表征，并通过分布外特征检测或基于解码器的重建来识别异常。这些方法难以泛化至不同类型的异常尺度（如全局几何误差：平面偏移、角度错位等），且在训练过程中对噪声或局部点云缺失较为敏感。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了一种层次化点-块异常评分网络，该网络通过联合建模区域部件特征与局部点特征实现鲁棒的异常推理。自适应分块模块融合了自监督分解策略以捕捉复杂结构偏差。除在公共基准数据集（Anomaly-ShapeNet与Real3D-AD）上的评估外，我们还发布了包含真实CAD模型的工业测试集，其中涵盖平面、角度及结构缺陷。在公开与工业数据集上的实验表明，该方法在AUC-ROC与AUC-PR指标上均取得优异性能：在新工业异常类型上实现超过40%的点级性能提升，在Real3D-AD数据集上平均物体级指标提升7%，在Anomaly-ShapeNet上提升4%，展现出强大的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

TLDR: This paper proposes a hierarchical point-patch fusion method with an adaptive patch codebook for detecting geometric anomalies in 3D shapes, primarily for industrial inspection and quality control.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种具有自适应贴片码本的层次化点-块融合方法，用于检测3D形状中的几何异常，主要应用于工业检测和质量控制。

Authors: Xueyang Kang, Zizhao Li, Tian Lan, Dong Gong, Kourosh Khoshelham, Liangliang Nan

Learning 3D Reconstruction with Priors in Test Time

Abstract (English): We introduce a test-time framework for multiview Transformers (MVTs) that incorporates priors (e.g., camera poses, intrinsics, and depth) to improve 3D tasks without retraining or modifying pre-trained image-only networks. Rather than feeding priors into the architecture, we cast them as constraints on the predictions and optimize the network at inference time. The optimization loss consists of a self-supervised objective and prior penalty terms. The self-supervised objective captures the compatibility among multi-view predictions and is implemented using photometric or geometric loss between renderings from other views and each view itself. Any available priors are converted into penalty terms on the corresponding output modalities. Across a series of 3D vision benchmarks, including point map estimation and camera pose estimation, our method consistently improves performance over base MVTs by a large margin. On the ETH3D, 7-Scenes, and NRGBD datasets, our method reduces the point-map distance error by more than half compared with the base image-only models. Our method also outperforms retrained prior-aware feed-forward methods, demonstrating the effectiveness of our test-time constrained optimization (TCO) framework for incorporating priors into 3D vision tasks.

摘要（中文）: 我们提出一种适用于多视角变换器（MVTs）的测试时框架，该框架通过融入先验信息（如相机位姿、内参和深度）来提升三维任务性能，且无需重新训练或修改预训练的纯图像网络。我们不将先验信息直接输入网络架构，而是将其转化为对预测结果的约束，并在推理阶段对网络进行优化。优化损失包含自监督目标函数与先验惩罚项两部分：自监督目标函数通过其他视角渲染结果与当前视角之间的光度或几何损失，实现多视角预测的一致性度量；而现有先验信息则被转化为对应输出模态的惩罚项。在一系列三维视觉基准测试（包括点云地图估计和相机位姿估计）中，本方法均显著提升了基础多视角变换器的性能。在ETH3D、7-Scenes和NRGBD数据集上，相比基础纯图像模型，本方法将点云地图距离误差降低了超过50%。本方法性能亦优于经过重新训练的、具备先验感知能力的前馈方法，这验证了测试时约束优化（TCO）框架在三维视觉任务中融合先验信息的有效性。

TLDR: A test-time constrained optimization framework for multi-view Transformers that incorporates prior knowledge like camera poses and depth to improve 3D reconstruction and vision tasks without retraining.

Authors: Lei Zhou, Haoyu Wu, Akshat Dave, Dimitris Samaras

Efficient Onboard Spacecraft Pose Estimation with Event Cameras and Neuromorphic Hardware

Abstract (English): Reliable relative pose estimation is a key enabler for autonomous rendezvous and proximity operations, yet space imagery is notoriously challenging due to extreme illumination, high contrast, and fast target motion. Event cameras provide asynchronous, change-driven measurements that can remain informative when frame-based imagery saturates or blurs, while neuromorphic processors can exploit sparse activations for low-latency, energy-efficient inferences. This paper presents a spacecraft 6-DoF pose-estimation pipeline that couples event-based vision with the BrainChip Akida neuromorphic processor. Using the SPADES dataset, we train compact MobileNet-style keypoint regression networks on lightweight event-frame representations, apply quantization-aware training (8/4-bit), and convert the models to Akida-compatible spiking neural networks. We benchmark three event representations and demonstrate real-time, low-power inference on Akida V1 hardware. We additionally design a heatmap-based model targeting Akida V2 and evaluate it on Akida Cloud, yielding improved pose accuracy. To our knowledge, this is the first end-to-end demonstration of spacecraft pose estimation running on Akida hardware, highlighting a practical route to low-latency, low-power perception for future autonomous space missions.

摘要（中文）: 可靠的相对姿态估计是自主交会与抵近操作的关键使能技术，然而空间图像因极端光照、高对比度和目标快速运动而极具挑战性。事件相机通过异步的基于变化的测量机制，能够在传统帧图像饱和或模糊时保持有效信息，而神经形态处理器可利用稀疏激活实现低延迟、高能效的推理。本文提出一种航天器六自由度姿态估计流程，将事件视觉与BrainChip Akida神经形态处理器深度结合。基于SPADES数据集，我们在轻量化事件帧表示上训练紧凑型MobileNet风格的关键点回归网络，应用量化感知训练（8位/4位）并将模型转换为兼容Akida的脉冲神经网络。我们对三种事件表示进行基准测试，并在Akida V1硬件上实现实时低功耗推理。此外，针对Akida V2设计了基于热图预测的模型，在Akida Cloud平台上进行评估，获得了更高的姿态估计精度。据我们所知，这是首次在Akida硬件上实现端到端航天器姿态估计的完整验证，为未来自主空间任务探索了一条实现低延迟、低功耗感知的实用路径。

TLDR: This paper presents a spacecraft 6-DoF pose estimation pipeline using event cameras and a neuromorphic processor for low-latency, low-power perception in space rendezvous and proximity operations.

Authors: Arunkumar Rathinam, Jules Lecomte, Jost Reelsen, Gregor Lenz, Axel von Arnim, Djamila Aouada

Periodic Event-Triggered Explicit Reference Governor for Constrained Attitude Control on SO(3)

Abstract (English): This letter addresses the constrained attitude control problem for rigid bodies directly on the special orthogonal group SO(3), avoiding singularities associated with parameterizations such as Euler angles. We propose a novel Periodic Event-Triggered Explicit Reference Governor (PET-ERG) that enforces input saturation and geometric pointing constraints without relying on online optimization. A key feature is a periodic event-triggered supervisory update: the auxiliary reference is updated only at sampled instants when a robust safety condition is met, thereby avoiding continuous-time reference updates and enabling a rigorous stability analysis of the cascade system on the manifold. Through this structured approach, we rigorously establish the asymptotic stability and exponential convergence of the closed-loop system for almost all initial configurations. Numerical simulations validate the effectiveness of the proposed control architecture and demonstrate constraint satisfaction and convergence properties.

摘要（中文）: 本通讯研究了直接在特殊正交群SO(3)上处理刚体姿态约束控制的问题，避免了欧拉角等参数化方法带来的奇异性。我们提出了一种新颖的周期性事件触发显式参考调节器（PET-ERG），该调节器能够在无需依赖在线优化的情况下，同时满足输入饱和约束与几何指向约束。其核心特性在于采用周期性事件触发的监控更新机制：仅当满足鲁棒安全条件时，系统才在采样时刻更新辅助参考量，从而避免了连续时间参考更新，并实现了流形上级联系统的严格稳定性分析。通过这种结构化设计，我们严格证明了闭环系统在几乎所有初始配置下都具有渐近稳定性与指数收敛性。数值仿真验证了所提控制架构的有效性，并展示了其满足约束条件与收敛特性的性能。

TLDR: The paper proposes a periodic event-triggered control scheme for constrained attitude control on SO(3), ensuring safety and stability for rigid bodies without online optimization.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种用于SO(3)上约束姿态控制的周期性事件触发控制方案，确保刚性体的安全性和稳定性，无需在线优化。

Authors: Satoshi Nakano, Masahiro Suzuki, Misa Ohashi, Noboru Chikami, Shusuke Otabe

Element-based Formation Control: a Unified Perspective from Continuum Mechanics

Abstract (English): This paper establishes a unified element-based framework for formation control by introducing the concept of the deformation gradient from continuum mechanics. Unlike traditional methods that rely on geometric constraints defined on graph edges, we model the formation as a discrete elastic body composed of simplicial elements. By defining a generalized distortion energy based on the local deformation gradient tensor, we derive a family of distributed control laws that can enforce various geometric invariances, including translation, rotation, scaling, and affine transformations. The convergence properties and the features of the proposed controllers are analyzed in detail. Theoretically, we show that the proposed framework serves as a bridge between existing rigidity-based and Laplacian-based approaches. Specifically, we show that rigidity-based controllers are mathematically equivalent to minimizing specific projections of the deformation energy tensor. Furthermore, we establish a rigorous link between the proposed energy minimization and Laplacian-based formation control. Numerical simulations in 2D and 3D validate the effectiveness and the unified nature of the proposed framework.

摘要（中文）: 本文通过引入连续介质力学中的变形梯度概念，建立了一个基于单元的统一编队控制框架。与依赖图边几何约束的传统方法不同，我们将编队建模为由单纯形单元构成的离散弹性体。通过基于局部变形梯度张量定义广义畸变能量，我们推导出一类能够保持平移、旋转、缩放及仿射变换等多种几何不变性的分布式控制律。文中详细分析了所提出控制器的收敛特性与性能特征。理论上，我们证明该框架可作为现有基于刚性与基于拉普拉斯方法的桥梁。具体而言，我们揭示了基于刚性的控制器在数学上等价于最小化变形能量张量的特定投影，并建立了所提出的能量最小化方法与基于拉普拉斯的编队控制之间的严格数学联系。通过二维与三维数值仿真，验证了该框架的有效性及其统一性特征。

TLDR: This paper presents a unified framework for multi-agent formation control using concepts from continuum mechanics, deriving distributed control laws for geometric transformations, and linking existing rigidity-based and Laplacian-based approaches.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种基于连续介质力学的统一多智能体编队控制框架，通过定义局部变形梯度张量，推导出分布式控制律，并建立了现有刚性编队与拉普拉斯编队控制方法的理论联系。

Authors: Kun Cao, Lihua Xie

ClawArena: Benchmarking AI Agents in Evolving Information Environments

Abstract (English): AI agents deployed as persistent assistants must maintain correct beliefs as their information environment evolves. In practice, evidence is scattered across heterogeneous sources that often contradict one another, new information can invalidate earlier conclusions, and user preferences surface through corrections rather than explicit instructions. Existing benchmarks largely assume static, single-authority settings and do not evaluate whether agents can keep up with this complexity. We introduce ClawArena, a benchmark for evaluating AI agents in evolving information environments. Each scenario maintains a complete hidden ground truth while exposing the agent only to noisy, partial, and sometimes contradictory traces across multi-channel sessions, workspace files, and staged updates. Evaluation is organized around three coupled challenges: multi-source conflict reasoning, dynamic belief revision, and implicit personalization, whose interactions yield a 14-category question taxonomy. Two question formats, multi-choice (set-selection) and shell-based executable checks, test both reasoning and workspace grounding. The current release contains 64 scenarios across 8 professional domains, totaling 1{,}879 evaluation rounds and 365 dynamic updates. Experiments on five agent frameworks and five language models show that both model capability (15.4% range) and framework design (9.2%) substantially affect performance, that self-evolving skill frameworks can partially close model-capability gaps, and that belief revision difficulty is determined by update design strategy rather than the mere presence of updates. Code is available at https://github.com/aiming-lab/ClawArena.

摘要（中文）: 部署为持久性助手的人工智能体必须在其信息环境演变过程中保持正确的信念。实践中，证据分散于相互矛盾的多源异构信息中，新信息可能推翻早期结论，而用户偏好往往通过修正而非明确指令得以体现。现有基准大多假设静态单源权威场景，未能评估智能体是否能够应对这种复杂性。本文提出ClawArena——一个用于评估动态信息环境中人工智能体的基准测试平台。每个场景都维护着完整的隐藏事实基准，而智能体仅能通过多通道会话、工作空间文件和阶段性更新接触带有噪声、不完整且有时相互矛盾的痕迹信息。评估围绕三个相互关联的挑战展开：多源冲突推理、动态信念修正与隐式个性化，三者的交互作用形成了包含14个类别的问题分类体系。通过多选题（集合选择）和基于Shell的可执行检查两种题型，同步测试推理能力与工作空间落地能力。当前版本涵盖8个专业领域的64个场景，共计1,879个评估轮次和365次动态更新。对五种智能体框架和五种语言模型的实验表明：模型能力（15.4%差异区间）与框架设计（9.2%）均对性能产生显著影响；自演进技能框架可部分弥补模型能力差距；信念修正难度取决于更新设计策略而非单纯更新频次。项目代码已发布于https://github.com/aiming-lab/ClawArena。

TLDR: Introduces ClawArena, a benchmark for evaluating AI agents' ability to maintain correct beliefs in evolving, multi-source, and contradictory information environments.

简要说明: 介绍ClawArena，这是一个用于评估AI智能体在不断变化、多源且矛盾的信息环境中保持正确信念能力的基准。

Authors: Haonian Ji, Kaiwen Xiong, Siwei Han, Peng Xia, Shi Qiu, Yiyang Zhou, Jiaqi Liu, Jinlong Li, Bingzhou Li, Zeyu Zheng, Cihang Xie, Huaxiu Yao

PATHFINDER: Multi-objective discovery in structural and spectral spaces

Abstract (English): Automated decision-making is becoming key for automated characterization including electron and scanning probe microscopies and nano indentation. Most machine learning driven workflows optimize a single predefined objective and tend to converge prematurely on familiar responses, overlooking rare but scientifically important states. More broadly, the challenge is not only where to measure next, but how to coordinate exploration across structural, spectral, and measurement spaces under finite experimental budgets while balancing target-driven optimization with novelty discovery. Here we introduce PATHFINDER, a framework for autonomous microscopy that combines novelty driven exploration with optimization, helping the system discover more diverse and useful representations across structural, spectral, and measurement spaces. By combining latent space representations of local structure, surrogate modeling of functional response, and Pareto-based acquisition, the framework selects measurements that balance novelty discovery in feature and object space and are informative and experimentally actionable. Benchmarked on pre acquired STEM EELS data and realized experimentally in scanning probe microscopy of ferroelectric materials, this approach expands the accessible structure property landscape and avoids collapse onto a single apparent optimum. These results point to a new mode of autonomous microscopy that is not only optimization-driven, but also discovery-oriented, broad in its search, and responsive to human guidance.

摘要（中文）: 自动化决策正逐渐成为自动表征技术（包括电子显微镜、扫描探针显微镜和纳米压痕）的关键。大多数机器学习驱动的工作流程仅优化单一预定义目标，往往过早收敛于常见响应，而忽略了罕见却具有重要科学意义的状态。更广泛而言，挑战不仅在于确定下一步测量位置，还在于如何在有限实验资源的约束下，协调结构空间、谱空间和测量空间的探索，同时平衡目标驱动优化与新现象发现。本文提出PATHFINDER框架——一种结合新颖性驱动探索与优化功能的自主显微技术框架，可帮助系统在结构、谱和测量空间中发现更丰富且更具价值的表征模式。该框架通过融合局部结构的隐空间表征、功能响应的代理建模以及基于帕累托准则的采集策略，选择既能平衡特征空间与对象空间的新颖性发现，又具备信息价值与实验可行性的测量方案。基于预先采集的扫描透射电子显微镜电子能量损失谱数据进行基准测试，并在铁电材料扫描探针显微镜实验中实现验证，该方法扩展了可探索的结构-性能关系图谱，避免了向单一表观最优解的过早收敛。这些成果展示了一种新型自主显微模式——不仅以优化为导向，同时注重科学发现，具备广泛搜索能力，并能灵活响应人工指导。

TLDR: PATHFINDER is a framework for autonomous scientific microscopy that balances targeted optimization with novelty-driven exploration across multiple data spaces, demonstrated on materials characterization tasks.

简要说明: PATHFINDER 是一个用于自主科学显微镜的框架，它在多个数据空间中平衡了目标优化和新颖性探索，并在材料表征任务中进行了演示。

Authors: Kamyar Barakati, Boris N. Slautin, Utkarsh Pratiush, Hiroshi Funakubo, Sergei V. Kalinin

GENFIG1: Visual Summaries of Scholarly Work as a Challenge for Vision-Language Models

Abstract (English): In many science papers, "Figure 1" serves as the primary visual summary of the core research idea. These figures are visually simple yet conceptually rich, often requiring significant effort and iteration by human authors to get right, highlighting the difficulty of science visual communication. With this intuition, we introduce GENFIG1, a benchmark for generative AI models (e.g., Vision-Language Models). GENFIG1 evaluates models for their ability to produce figures that clearly express and motivate the central idea of a paper (title, abstract, introduction, and figure caption) as input. Solving GENFIG1 requires more than producing visually appealing graphics: the task entails reasoning for text-to-image generation that couples scientific understanding with visual synthesis. Specifically, models must (i) comprehend and grasp the technical concepts of the paper, (ii) identify the most salient ones, and (iii) design a coherent and aesthetically effective graphic that conveys those concepts visually and is faithful to the input. We curate the benchmark from papers published at top deep-learning conferences, apply stringent quality control, and introduce an automatic evaluation metric that correlates well with expert human judgments. We evaluate a suite of representative models on GENFIG1 and demonstrate that the task presents significant challenges, even for the best-performing systems. We hope this benchmark serves as a foundation for future progress in multimodal AI.

摘要（中文）: 在许多科学论文中，"图1"是核心研究思想的主要视觉概括。这些图表视觉上简洁却概念丰富，通常需要作者投入大量精力并反复修改才能成型，这凸显了科学视觉传达的难度。基于此观察，我们提出了GENFIG1——一个面向生成式人工智能模型（如视觉语言模型）的基准测试。GENFIG1通过评估模型生成图表的能力，检验其能否以论文核心要素（标题、摘要、引言和图表说明）为输入，清晰表达并阐释论文中心思想。解决GENFIG1任务不仅需要生成视觉吸引人的图像，更要求模型在文本到图像的生成过程中进行推理，将科学理解与视觉合成相结合。具体而言，模型必须（1）理解并把握论文的技术概念，（2）识别其中最核心的要素，（3）设计出连贯且具有美学效果的视觉图表，以可视化方式传达这些概念并忠实于输入内容。我们从顶级深度学习会议发表的论文中精心构建该基准数据集，实施严格的质量控制，并引入与专家评判高度一致的自动评估指标。通过对一系列代表性模型在GENFIG1上的测试，我们发现即使当前最优系统也面临显著挑战。我们期待该基准能为未来多模态人工智能的发展奠定基础。

TLDR: This paper introduces GENFIG1, a benchmark for Vision-Language Models that evaluates their ability to generate the 'Figure 1' visual summary of a scientific paper, requiring comprehension, reasoning, and visual synthesis of technical concepts. It is not directly related to robotics or embodied AI, focusing instead on scientific figure generation and evaluation.

简要说明: 本文提出了GENFIG1基准，用于评估视觉语言模型根据论文标题、摘要等生成概念性核心图表（Figure 1）的能力，这需要模型理解科学概念并进行视觉合成。该工作与机器人或具身智能无直接关联。

Authors: Yaohan Guan, Pristina Wang, Najim Dehak, Alan Yuille, Jieneng Chen, Daniel Khashabi

Readable Minds: Emergent Theory-of-Mind-Like Behavior in LLM Poker Agents

Abstract (English): Theory of Mind (ToM) -- the ability to model others' mental states -- is fundamental to human social cognition. Whether large language models (LLMs) can develop ToM has been tested exclusively through static vignettes, leaving open whether ToM-like reasoning can emerge through dynamic interaction. Here we report that autonomous LLM agents playing extended sessions of Texas Hold'em poker progressively develop sophisticated opponent models, but only when equipped with persistent memory. In a 2x2 factorial design crossing memory (present/absent) with domain knowledge (present/absent), each with five replications (N = 20 experiments, ~6,000 agent-hand observations), we find that memory is both necessary and sufficient for ToM-like behavior emergence (Cliff's delta = 1.0, p = 0.008). Agents with memory reach ToM Level 3-5 (predictive to recursive modeling), while agents without memory remain at Level 0 across all replications. Strategic deception grounded in opponent models occurs exclusively in memory-equipped conditions (Fisher's exact p < 0.001). Domain expertise does not gate ToM-like behavior emergence but enhances its application: agents without poker knowledge develop equivalent ToM levels but less precise deception (p = 0.004). Agents with ToM deviate from game-theoretically optimal play (67% vs. 79% TAG adherence, delta = -1.0, p = 0.008) to exploit specific opponents, mirroring expert human play. All mental models are expressed in natural language and directly readable, providing a transparent window into AI social cognition. Cross-model validation with GPT-4o yields weighted Cohen's kappa = 0.81 (almost perfect agreement). These findings demonstrate that functional ToM-like behavior can emerge from interaction dynamics alone, without explicit training or prompting, with implications for understanding artificial social intelligence and biological social cognition.

摘要（中文）: 心理理论——即模拟他人心理状态的能力——是人类社会认知的基础。大型语言模型能否发展出心理理论一直仅通过静态情境测试，尚不清楚类心理理论推理能否通过动态互动形成。本研究发现：在长时间德州扑克对局中，自主运作的大型语言模型智能体逐步发展出复杂的对手模型，但这仅当它们具备持久记忆时才会发生。通过2×2因子设计（记忆[有/无]×领域知识[有/无]）并各进行五次重复实验（N=20组实验，约6000次智能体手牌观测），我们发现记忆是类心理理论行为涌现的必要且充分条件（克利夫效应值δ=1.0，p=0.008）。具备记忆的智能体达到心理理论3-5级（预测性至递归建模），而无记忆智能体在所有重复实验中始终停留于0级。基于对手模型的策略性欺骗行为仅出现在配备记忆的条件下（费希尔精确检验p<0.001）。领域专业知识不限制类心理理论行为的涌现，但能增强其应用效果：缺乏扑克知识的智能体可发展出同等心理理论水平，但实施欺骗的精准度较低（p=0.004）。具备心理理论的智能体会偏离博弈论最优策略（紧凶策略遵循率67% vs. 79%，δ=-1.0，p=0.008）以针对特定对手实施剥削，这与人类专家行为模式一致。所有心理模型均以自然语言表达且可直接阅读，为理解人工智能社会认知提供了透明窗口。使用GPT-4o进行跨模型验证所得加权科恩卡帕系数为0.81（几乎完全一致）。这些发现证明功能性类心理理论行为可仅通过互动动态涌现，无需显式训练或提示，这对理解人工智能社会智能与生物社会认知具有重要意义。

TLDR: LLM agents playing poker develop a form of Theory of Mind, including opponent modeling and strategic deception, when equipped with persistent memory, emerging purely from interaction.

简要说明: 在配备持久记忆的情况下，玩扑克游戏的LLM智能体会发展出包括对手建模和战略欺骗在内的心理理论能力，这些能力完全从互动中涌现。

Authors: Hsieh-Ting Lin, Tsung-Yu Hou

Extracting and Steering Emotion Representations in Small Language Models: A Methodological Comparison

Abstract (English): Small language models (SLMs) in the 100M-10B parameter range increasingly power production systems, yet whether they possess the internal emotion representations recently discovered in frontier models remains unknown. We present the first comparative analysis of emotion vector extraction methods for SLMs, evaluating 9 models across 5 architectural families (GPT-2, Gemma, Qwen, Llama, Mistral) using 20 emotions and two extraction methods (generation-based and comprehension-based). Generation-based extraction produces statistically superior emotion separation (Mann-Whitney p = 0.007; Cohen's d = -107.5), with the advantage modulated by instruction tuning and architecture. Emotion representations localize at middle transformer layers (~50% depth), following a U-shaped curve that is architecture-invariant from 124M to 3B parameters. We validate these findings against representational anisotropy baselines across 4 models and confirm causal behavioral effects through steering experiments, independently verified by an external emotion classifier (92% success rate, 37/40 scenarios). Steering reveals three regimes -- surgical (coherent text transformation), repetitive collapse, and explosive (text degradation) -- quantified by perplexity ratios and separated by model architecture rather than scale. We document cross-lingual emotion entanglement in Qwen, where steering activates semantically aligned Chinese tokens that RLHF does not suppress, raising safety concerns for multilingual deployment. This work provides methodological guidelines for emotion research on open-weight models and contributes to the Model Medicine series by bridging external behavioral profiling with internal representational analysis.

摘要（中文）: 在1亿至100亿参数规模的小型语言模型正日益成为生产系统的核心驱动力，然而这些模型是否具备前沿模型中新近发现的内在情感表征机制仍属未知。本研究首次对小型语言模型的情感向量提取方法进行系统性比较分析，通过20种情感维度和两种提取方法（基于生成与基于理解），评估了涵盖5大架构系列（GPT-2、Gemma、Qwen、Llama、Mistral）的9个模型。基于生成的提取方法在情感分离度上呈现统计显著性优势（曼-惠特尼检验p=0.007；科恩d值=-107.5），其优势受指令微调和模型架构的调节影响。情感表征定位于Transformer中间层（约50%深度），呈现U型分布曲线，这种124M至3B参数范围内的分布规律具有跨架构稳定性。我们通过4个模型的表征各向异性基线验证了这些发现，并借助引导实验证实了因果行为效应，该效应经外部情感分类器独立验证（成功率92%，40个场景中37个有效）。引导实验揭示了三种调控机制——精准调控（连贯的文本转换）、重复性塌缩和爆炸性退化（文本质量劣化），这些机制通过困惑度比率量化，其表现差异主要受模型架构而非规模影响。研究还发现Qwen模型存在跨语言情感纠缠现象，引导过程会激活与目标情感语义对齐的中文词汇单元，而该现象未被RLHF训练有效抑制，这为多语言部署场景下的安全性提出了警示。本工作为开源权重模型的情感研究提供了方法论指导，并通过外部行为刻画与内部表征分析的结合，为“模型医学”系列研究作出贡献。

TLDR: This paper presents a comparative analysis of emotion representation extraction and steering in small language models, evaluating methods and model architectures, but does not involve robotics or embodied AI.

简要说明: 本文对小型语言模型中的情感表征提取和引导方法进行了比较分析，评估了不同模型架构和方法，但未涉及机器人或具身人工智能。

Authors: Jihoon Jeong

Causality Laundering: Denial-Feedback Leakage in Tool-Calling LLM Agents

Abstract (English): Tool-calling LLM agents can read private data, invoke external services, and trigger real-world actions, creating a security problem at the point of tool execution. We identify a denial-feedback leakage pattern, which we term causality laundering, in which an adversary probes a protected action, learns from the denial outcome, and exfiltrates the inferred information through a later seemingly benign tool call. This attack is not captured by flat provenance tracking alone because the leaked information arises from causal influence of the denied action, not direct data flow. We present the Agentic Reference Monitor (ARM), a runtime enforcement layer that mediates every tool invocation by consulting a provenance graph over tool calls, returned data, field-level provenance, and denied actions. ARM propagates trust through an integrity lattice and augments the graph with counterfactual edges from denied-action nodes, enabling enforcement over both transitive data dependencies and denial-induced causal influence. In a controlled evaluation on three representative attack scenarios, ARM blocks causality laundering, transitive taint propagation, and mixed-provenance field misuse that a flat provenance baseline misses, while adding sub-millisecond policy evaluation overhead. These results suggest that denial-aware causal provenance is a useful abstraction for securing tool-calling agent systems.

摘要（中文）: 工具调用大型语言模型代理能够读取私有数据、调用外部服务并触发现实世界行动，这在工具执行层面产生了安全隐患。本文提出一种"拒绝反馈泄漏"模式——我们称之为因果洗白，攻击者通过探测受保护操作，从拒绝结果中学习信息，并通过后续看似良性的工具调用外泄推断所得信息。这种攻击无法被扁平溯源追踪单独捕获，因为泄漏信息源于被拒操作产生的因果影响，而非直接数据流。我们提出代理化参考监控器，这是一个运行时执行层，通过构建覆盖工具调用、返回数据、字段级溯源和被拒操作的全方位溯源图来仲裁每次工具调用。该系统通过完整性格传播信任度，并在溯源图中添加来自被拒操作节点的反事实边，从而实现对传递性数据依赖和拒绝引发因果影响的双重管控。在三种典型攻击场景的受控评估中，该机制能有效阻断因果洗白攻击、传递性污点传播以及扁平溯源基线遗漏的混合溯源字段滥用问题，同时仅增加亚毫秒级策略评估开销。这些结果表明，具备拒绝感知能力的因果溯源机制是保障工具调用代理系统安全性的有效抽象框架。

TLDR: This paper identifies and proposes a defense against a new security vulnerability in tool-calling LLM agents, where information is leaked through the causal influence of denied actions, rather than direct data flow.

简要说明: 本文识别并提出了一种针对工具调用LLM智能体新型安全漏洞的防御方法，该漏洞通过被拒绝动作的因果影响，而非直接数据流来泄露信息。

Authors: Mohammad Hossein Chinaei

SKILLFOUNDRY: Building Self-Evolving Agent Skill Libraries from Heterogeneous Scientific Resources

Abstract (English): Modern scientific ecosystems are rich in procedural knowledge across repositories, APIs, scripts, notebooks, documentation, databases, and papers, yet much of this knowledge remains fragmented across heterogeneous artifacts that agents cannot readily operationalize. This gap between abundant scientific know-how and usable agent capabilities is a key bottleneck for building effective scientific agents. We present SkillFoundry, a self-evolving framework that converts such resources into validated agent skills, reusable packages that encode task scope, inputs and outputs, execution steps, environment assumptions, provenance, and tests. SkillFoundry organizes a target domain as a domain knowledge tree, mines resources from high-value branches, extracts operational contracts, compiles them into executable skill packages, and then iteratively expands, repairs, merges, or prunes the resulting library through a closed-loop validation process. SkillFoundry produces a substantially novel and internally valid skill library, with 71.1\% of mined skills differing from existing skill libraries such as SkillHub and SkillSMP. We demonstrate that these mined skills improve coding agent performance on five of the six MoSciBench datasets. We further show that SkillFoundry can design new task-specific skills on demand for concrete scientific objectives, and that the resulting skills substantially improve performance on two challenging genomics tasks: cell type annotation and the scDRS workflow. Together, these results show that automatically mined skills improve agent performance on benchmarks and domain-specific tasks, expand coverage beyond hand-crafted skill libraries, and provide a practical foundation for more capable scientific agents.

摘要（中文）: 现代科学生态系统在各类存储库、API、脚本、笔记本文档、数据库及论文中蕴含着丰富的程序性知识，然而这些知识大多分散于异构的、智能体无法直接操作的数字痕迹中。海量科学操作知识与可用智能体能力之间的鸿沟，成为构建高效科学智能体的关键瓶颈。本文提出SkillFoundry——一个自演化的框架，能够将此类资源转化为经过验证的智能体技能，即封装任务范围、输入输出、执行步骤、环境假设、溯源信息和测试用例的可复用技能包。该框架通过领域知识树组织目标领域，从高价值分支挖掘资源，提取操作契约并将其编译为可执行技能包，随后通过闭环验证流程迭代扩展、修复、合并或剪枝技能库。SkillFoundry生成的技能库具有显著新颖性和内部有效性，其挖掘的技能中71.1%不同于SkillHub、SkillSMP等现有技能库。实验表明，这些挖掘技能在MoSciBench六个数据集中的五个上提升了编程智能体性能。进一步研究证明，SkillFoundry能够针对具体科学目标按需设计新任务特定技能，所生成的技能显著提升了细胞类型注释和scDRS工作流两项高难度基因组学任务的性能。这些结果共同表明：自动挖掘的技能不仅能提升智能体在基准测试和领域特定任务中的表现，还能超越人工构建技能库的覆盖范围，为构建更强大的科学智能体提供实用基础。

TLDR: This paper introduces SkillFoundry, a framework for automatically mining and validating reusable agent skills from heterogeneous scientific resources, such as code and documentation, to improve the performance of scientific AI agents on domain-specific tasks.

简要说明: 本文介绍了 SkillFoundry，一个从异构科学资源（如代码和文档）中自动挖掘和验证可重用智能体技能的框架，旨在提升科学AI智能体在特定领域任务上的性能。
