Authors: Shuaike Shen, Wenduo Cheng, Mingqian Ma, Alistair Turcan, Martin Jinye Zhang, Jian Ma

Symbolic-Vector Attention Fusion for Collective Intelligence

Abstract (English): When autonomous agents observe different domains of a shared environment, each signal they exchange mixes relevant and irrelevant dimensions. No existing mechanism lets the receiver evaluate which dimensions to absorb. We introduce Symbolic-Vector Attention Fusion (SVAF), the content-evaluation half of a two-level coupling engine for collective intelligence. SVAF decomposes each inter-agent signal into 7 typed semantic fields, evaluates each through a learned fusion gate, and produces a remix -- new knowledge from the intersection of two domains. A band-pass model yields four outcomes (redundant, aligned, guarded, rejected), solving both selectivity and redundancy. The fusion gate independently discovers a cross-domain relevance hierarchy: mood emerges as the highest-weight field by epoch 1, before accuracy plateaus -- consistent with independent mechanistic evidence that LLM emotion representations are structurally embedded along valence-arousal axes. SVAF forms Layer 4 of the Mesh Memory Protocol (MMP); the other half of the coupling engine is a per-agent Closed-form Continuous-time (CfC) neural network at Layer 6, whose learned per-neuron time constants (tau) create the temporal dynamics from which collective intelligence emerges: fast neurons synchronise affect across agents in seconds, while slow neurons preserve domain expertise indefinitely. SVAF determines what enters each agent's cognitive state; CfC determines how that state evolves. Trained on 237K samples from 273 narrative scenarios, SVAF achieves 78.7% three-class accuracy. We verify the complete mesh cognition loop -- from per-field evaluation through remix, CfC state evolution, tau-modulated peer blending, and autonomous action -- in a live deployment with 7 nodes across macOS, iOS, and web.

摘要（中文）: 摘要： 当自主智能体观测共享环境的不同领域时，其交换的每个信号均混杂着相关与无关维度。现有机制尚无法使接收方评估应吸收哪些维度。我们提出了符号-向量注意力融合机制（SVAF），这是一种双层耦合引擎中负责内容评估的模块。该机制将智能体间信号分解为7类语义场，通过学习的融合门对各场进行评估，并生成重混信号——即两个领域交集产生的新知识。带通模型可产生四种结果（冗余、对齐、防护、拒绝），同步解决选择性与冗余问题。融合门独立发现了跨领域相关性层级：在准确度达到平台期前，情绪已在第1训练周期成为最高权重场——这与独立机械证据相符，即LLM情感表征在效价-唤醒度轴向上存在结构性嵌入。SVAF构成网状记忆协议（MMP）的第四层；耦合引擎的另一半是位于第六层的单智能体闭式连续时间（CfC）神经网络，其习得的神经元级时间常数（τ）形成了集体智能涌现的时序动态：快速神经元在数秒内同步跨智能体情感，而慢速神经元可永久保持领域专长。SVAF决定何种信息进入各智能体的认知状态；CfC决定该状态如何演化。基于来自273个叙事场景的237K样本训练后，SVAF在三分类任务中达到78.7%准确率。我们在横跨macOS、iOS和网页端的7节点实时部署中，完整验证了从逐场评估、重混合成、CfC状态演化、τ调制对等融合到自主行动的网状认知闭环。

TLDR: This paper introduces Symbolic-Vector Attention Fusion (SVAF), a mechanism for agents to evaluate and selectively integrate information from different domains within a shared environment, as part of a larger system called the Mesh Memory Protocol for collective intelligence.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种符号-向量注意力融合（SVAF）机制，用于智能体在共享环境中评估和选择性整合来自不同领域的信息，该机制是用于集体智能的网状记忆协议（MMP）系统的一部分。

Authors: Hongwei Xu

PolySwarm: A Multi-Agent Large Language Model Framework for Prediction Market Trading and Latency Arbitrage

Abstract (English): This paper presents PolySwarm, a novel multi-agent large language model (LLM) framework designed for real-time prediction market trading and latency arbitrage on decentralized platforms such as Polymarket. PolySwarm deploys a swarm of 50 diverse LLM personas that concurrently evaluate binary outcome markets, aggregating individual probability estimates through confidence-weighted Bayesian combination of swarm consensus with market-implied probabilities, and applying quarter-Kelly position sizing for risk-controlled execution. The system incorporates an information-theoretic market analysis engine using Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence and Jensen-Shannon (JS) divergence to detect cross-market inefficiencies and negation pair mispricings. A latency arbitrage module exploits stale Polymarket prices by deriving CEX-implied probabilities from a log-normal pricing model and executing trades within the human reaction-time window. We provide a full architectural description, implementation details, and evaluation methodology using Brier scores, calibration analysis, and log-loss metrics benchmarked against human superforecaster performance. We further discuss open challenges including hallucination in agent pools, computational cost at scale, regulatory exposure, and feedback-loop risk, and outline five priority directions for future research. Experimental results demonstrate that swarm aggregation consistently outperforms single-model baselines in probability calibration on Polymarket prediction tasks.

摘要（中文）: 本文提出了PolySwarm——一种新型多智能体大语言模型框架，专为去中心化预测市场平台（如Polymarket）的实时交易与延迟套利而设计。该框架部署了50个多样化的大语言模型智能体，通过并行情景评估二元结果市场，采用基于置信度加权的贝叶斯组合算法聚合群体共识概率与市场隐含概率，并应用四分之一凯利仓位策略进行风险控制执行。系统集成了信息论市场分析引擎，利用Kullback-Leibler散度与Jensen-Shannon散度检测跨市场效率缺口与对立合约定价偏差。延迟套利模块通过对数正态定价模型推导中心化交易所隐含概率，捕捉Polymarket的价格滞后性，并在人类反应时间窗口内完成交易。我们提供了完整的架构描述、实现细节及评估方法，包括基于人类超级预测者基准的Brier分数、校准分析和对数损失度量。同时深入探讨了智能体群体幻觉、规模化计算成本、监管风险及反馈循环风险等开放性挑战，并规划了未来研究的五个优先方向。实验结果表明，在Polymarket预测任务中，群体共识聚合策略在概率校准方面持续优于单模型基线。

TLDR: This paper presents PolySwarm, a multi-agent LLM framework for trading and arbitrage in prediction markets, focusing on probability aggregation and market inefficiency detection.

简要说明: 本文提出PolySwarm，一个用于预测市场交易和套利的多智能体大语言模型框架，侧重于概率聚合和市场无效性检测。

Authors: Rajat M. Barot, Arjun S. Borkhatariya

Scale-Aware Vision-Language Adaptation for Extreme Far-Distance Video Person Re-identification

Abstract (English): Extreme far-distance video person re-identification (ReID) is particularly challenging due to scale compression, resolution degradation, motion blur, and aerial-ground viewpoint mismatch. As camera altitude and subject distance increase, models trained on close-range imagery degrade significantly. In this work, we investigate how large-scale vision-language models can be adapted to operate reliably under these conditions. Starting from a CLIP-based baseline, we upgrade the visual backbone from ViT-B/16 to ViT-L/14 and introduce backbone-aware selective fine-tuning to stabilize adaptation of the larger transformer. To address noisy and low-resolution tracklets, we incorporate a lightweight temporal attention pooling mechanism that suppresses degraded frames and emphasizes informative observations. We retain adapter-based and prompt-conditioned cross-view learning to mitigate aerial-ground domain shifts, and further refine retrieval using improved optimization and k-reciprocal re-ranking. Experiments on the DetReIDX stress-test benchmark show that our approach achieves mAP scores of 46.69 (A2G), 41.23 (G2A), and 22.98 (A2A), corresponding to an overall mAP of 35.73. These results show that large-scale vision-language backbones, when combined with stability-focused adaptation, significantly enhance robustness in extreme far-distance video person ReID.

摘要（中文）: 极端远距离视频行人重识别由于尺度压缩、分辨率降低、运动模糊及空地视角失配而面临巨大挑战。随着相机高度与拍摄距离的增加，基于近距离影像训练的模型性能显著下降。本研究探讨如何将大规模视觉语言模型适应于此类极端条件下可靠运行。我们以CLIP框架为基础，将视觉骨干网络从ViT-B/16升级至ViT-L/14，并引入骨干感知选择性微调策略以增强大尺寸Transformer模型的适应稳定性。为处理噪声多、分辨率低的轨迹片段，我们融合轻量级时序注意力池化机制，有效抑制低质量帧并强化信息丰富的观测特征。通过保留基于适配器的提示条件跨视角学习方法缓解空地领域偏移，并采用改进的优化策略与k互逆重排序进一步优化检索性能。在DetReIDX压力测试基准上的实验表明，该方法在A2G、G2A和A2A模式下分别取得46.69、41.23和22.98的mAP分数，综合mAP达到35.73。结果表明，大规模视觉语言骨干网络结合以稳定性为核心的适应策略，能显著增强极端远距离视频行人重识别的鲁棒性。

TLDR: This paper presents an adaptation method for large-scale vision-language models (specifically CLIP-based) to improve person re-identification in extreme far-distance video, addressing challenges like scale compression and viewpoint mismatch. 4C4D: 4 Camera 4D Gaussian Splatting

Abstract (English): This paper tackles the challenge of recovering 4D dynamic scenes from videos captured by as few as four portable cameras. Learning to model scene dynamics for temporally consistent novel-view rendering is a foundational task in computer graphics, where previous works often require dense multi-view captures using camera arrays of dozens or even hundreds of views. We propose \textbf{4C4D}, a novel framework that enables high-fidelity 4D Gaussian Splatting from video captures of extremely sparse cameras. Our key insight lies that the geometric learning under sparse settings is substantially more difficult than modeling appearance. Driven by this observation, we introduce a Neural Decaying Function on Gaussian opacities for enhancing the geometric modeling capability of 4D Gaussians. This design mitigates the inherent imbalance between geometry and appearance modeling in 4DGS by encouraging the 4DGS gradients to focus more on geometric learning. Extensive experiments across sparse-view datasets with varying camera overlaps show that 4C4D achieves superior performance over prior art. Project page at: https://junshengzhou.github.io/4C4D.

摘要（中文）: 本文致力于解决仅使用四台便携式相机拍摄的视频恢复4D动态场景的挑战。学习对场景动态进行建模以实现时间一致的新视角渲染，是计算机图形学中的一项基础任务，而以往的研究通常需要使用数十甚至上百个视角的相机阵列进行密集的多视角捕捉。我们提出了**4C4D**，一种新颖的框架，能够从极稀疏相机拍摄的视频中实现高保真度的4D高斯泼溅重建。我们的核心见解在于，稀疏设置下的几何学习比外观建模要困难得多。基于这一观察，我们引入了一种用于高斯不透明度的神经衰减函数，以增强4D高斯的几何建模能力。这一设计通过鼓励4DGS梯度更专注于几何学习，缓解了4DGS中几何与外观建模之间固有的不平衡问题。在不同相机重叠程度的稀疏视图数据集上进行的大量实验表明，4C4D在性能上优于现有技术。项目页面：https://junshengzhou.github.io/4C4D。

TLDR: Proposes 4C4D, a framework for high-fidelity 4D Gaussian Splatting of dynamic scenes from as few as four camera videos, using a Neural Decaying Function to improve geometric modeling under sparse views.

简要说明: 提出4C4D框架，能够仅用四台摄像机拍摄的视频实现高保真的4D高斯溅射动态场景重建，通过神经衰减函数在稀疏视角下增强几何建模能力。

Authors: Junsheng Zhou, Zhifan Yang, Liang Han, Wenyuan Zhang, Kanle Shi, Shenkun Xu, Yu-Shen Liu

1.x-Distill: Breaking the Diversity, Quality, and Efficiency Barrier in Distribution Matching Distillation

Abstract (English): Diffusion models produce high-quality text-to-image results, but their iterative denoising is computationally expensive.Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) emerges as a promising path to few-step distillation, but suffers from diversity collapse and fidelity degradation when reduced to two steps or fewer. We present 1.x-Distill, the first fractional-step distillation framework that breaks the integer-step constraint of prior few-step methods and establishes 1.x-step generation as a practical regime for distilled diffusion models.Specifically, we first analyze the overlooked role of teacher CFG in DMD and introduce a simple yet effective modification to suppress mode collapse. Then, to improve performance under extreme steps, we introduce Stagewise Focused Distillation, a two-stage strategy that learns coarse structure through diversity-preserving distribution matching and refines details with inference-consistent adversarial distillation. Furthermore, we design a lightweight compensation module for Distill--Cache co-Training, which naturally incorporates block-level caching into our distillation pipeline.Experiments on SD3-Medium and SD3.5-Large show that 1.x-Distill surpasses prior few-step methods, achieving better quality and diversity at 1.67 and 1.74 effective NFEs, respectively, with up to 33x speedup over original 28x2 NFE sampling.

摘要（中文）: 扩散模型能够生成高质量的文本到图像结果，但其迭代去噪过程计算成本高昂。分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）为少步数蒸馏提供了有前景的路径，但在缩减至两步或更少时会出现多样性崩溃和保真度下降的问题。我们提出了1.x-Distill，这是首个分数步蒸馏框架，它打破了先前少步方法中的整数步限制，并将1.x步生成确立为蒸馏扩散模型的实际可行方案。具体而言，我们首先分析了DMD中常被忽视的教师CFG（分类器引导）的作用，并引入一种简单而有效的改进方法来抑制模式崩溃。接着，为了提升极端步数下的性能，我们提出了阶段化聚焦蒸馏策略——一种两阶段方法，通过保持多样性的分布匹配学习粗略结构，并借助推理一致对抗蒸馏细化细节。此外，我们设计了一种轻量级补偿模块，用于蒸馏-缓存协同训练，将块级缓存自然地整合到我们的蒸馏流程中。在SD3-Medium和SD3.5-Large上的实验表明，1.x-Distill在1.67和1.74有效NFE（网络函数评估）下均超越了先前的少步方法，实现了更优的质量与多样性，同时相比原始28x2 NFE采样取得了高达33倍的加速效果。

TLDR: The paper proposes 1.x-Distill, a fractional-step distillation framework for text-to-image diffusion models that improves quality, diversity, and efficiency in extremely few-step generation.

简要说明: 该论文提出1.x-Distill，一种用于文生图扩散模型的小数步蒸馏框架，在极少的推理步数下提升了生成质量、多样性和效率。

Authors: Haoyu Li, Tingyan Wen, Lin Qi, Zhe Wu, Yihuang Chen, Xing Zhou, Lifei Zhu, Xueqian Wang, Kai Zhang

OASIC: Occlusion-Agnostic and Severity-Informed Classification

Abstract (English): Severe occlusions of objects pose a major challenge for computer vision. We show that two root causes are (1) the loss of visible information and (2) the distracting patterns caused by the occluders. Our approach addresses both causes at the same time. First, the distracting patterns are removed at test-time, via masking of the occluding patterns. This masking is independent of the type of occlusion, by handling the occlusion through the lens of visual anomalies w.r.t. the object of interest. Second, to deal with less visual details, we follow standard practice by masking random parts of the object during training, for various degrees of occlusions. We discover that (a) it is possible to estimate the degree of the occlusion (i.e. severity) at test-time, and (b) that a model optimized for a specific degree of occlusion also performs best on a similar degree during test-time. Combining these two insights brings us to a severity-informed classification model called OASIC: Occlusion Agnostic Severity Informed Classification. We estimate the severity of occlusion for a test image, mask the occluder, and select the model that is optimized for the degree of occlusion. This strategy performs better than any single model optimized for any smaller or broader range of occlusion severities. Experiments show that combining gray masking with adaptive model selection improves $\text{AUC}_\text{occ}$ by +18.5 over standard training on occluded images and +23.7 over finetuning on unoccluded images.

摘要（中文）: 物体的严重遮挡是计算机视觉领域面临的主要挑战之一。我们认为两大根本原因在于：（1）可见信息的损失，以及（2）遮挡物所产生的干扰性模式。我们的方法同时应对这两个问题：首先，通过在测试时对遮挡区域进行掩码处理，以消除干扰性模式。该掩码操作独立于遮挡类型，其核心思想是将遮挡视为相对于目标物体的视觉异常进行处理。其次，针对视觉细节减少的问题，我们遵循标准做法，在训练过程中对物体的不同部位进行随机掩码，以模拟不同程度的遮挡。研究发现：（a）在测试时能够估计遮挡的严重程度；（b）针对特定遮挡程度优化的模型，在测试时遇到相似程度遮挡时也表现最佳。结合这两项发现，我们提出了一个名为OASIC的遮挡感知严重度分类模型：该模型通过估计测试图像的遮挡严重程度，掩蔽遮挡区域，并选择针对该遮挡程度优化的对应模型进行预测。这一策略优于任何针对更窄或更广遮挡范围优化的单一模型。实验表明，将灰度掩码与自适应模型选择相结合，相较于在遮挡图像上进行标准训练，可将$\text{AUC}_\text{occ}$提升18.5；相较于在无遮挡图像上进行微调，可提升23.7。

TLDR: Proposes a classification method for handling severely occluded objects by masking occluders at test-time and selecting a model optimized for the estimated severity of occlusion, showing improved performance on occluded images.

简要说明: 提出了一种用于处理严重遮挡物体的分类方法，通过在测试时遮挡物进行掩码处理，并选择针对估计的遮挡程度优化的模型，在遮挡图像上展现出改进的性能。

Authors: Kay Gijzen, Gertjan J. Burghouts, Daniël M. Pelt

A Systematic Study of Cross-Modal Typographic Attacks on Audio-Visual Reasoning

Abstract (English): As audio-visual multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, understanding their vulnerabilities is crucial. To this end, we introduce Multi-Modal Typography, a systematic study examining how typographic attacks across multiple modalities adversely influence MLLMs. While prior work focuses narrowly on unimodal attacks, we expose the cross-modal fragility of MLLMs. We analyze the interactions between audio, visual, and text perturbations and reveal that coordinated multi-modal attack creates a significantly more potent threat than single-modality attacks (attack success rate = $83.43\%$ vs $34.93\%$).Our findings across multiple frontier MLLMs, tasks, and common-sense reasoning and content moderation benchmarks establishes multi-modal typography as a critical and underexplored attack strategy in multi-modal reasoning. Code and data will be publicly available.

摘要（中文）: 随着视听多模态大语言模型在安全关键应用中的部署日益广泛，理解其脆弱性变得至关重要。为此，本文提出多模态字体攻击，系统性地研究了跨多个模态的字体攻击如何对多模态大语言模型产生不利影响。已有研究大多局限于单模态攻击，而本文揭示了多模态大语言模型在跨模态层面的脆弱性。我们分析了音频、视觉和文本扰动之间的相互作用，发现协调的多模态攻击比单模态攻击构成显著更强的威胁（攻击成功率分别为83.43%与34.93%）。通过对多个前沿多模态大语言模型、任务以及常识推理与内容审核基准测试的验证，本研究确立了多模态字体攻击作为多模态推理中一个关键且尚未被充分探索的攻击策略。代码与数据将公开提供。

TLDR: The paper studies coordinated typographic attacks across audio, visual, and text modalities to exploit vulnerabilities in multi-modal large language models, significantly increasing attack success rates over unimodal attacks.

简要说明: 本文研究了跨音频、视觉和文本模态的协调排版攻击，以利用多模态大语言模型的脆弱性，相比单模态攻击显著提高了攻击成功率。

Authors: Tianle Chen, Deepti Ghadiyaram

SafeCtrl: Region-Aware Safety Control for Text-to-Image Diffusion via Detect-Then-Suppress

Abstract (English): The widespread deployment of text-to-image diffusion models is significantly challenged by the generation of visually harmful content, such as sexually explicit content, violence, and horror imagery. Common safety interventions, ranging from input filtering to model concept erasure, often suffer from two critical limitations: (1) a severe trade-off between safety and context preservation, where removing unsafe concepts degrades the fidelity of the safe content, and (2) vulnerability to adversarial attacks, where safety mechanisms are easily bypassed. To address these challenges, we propose SafeCtrl, a Region-Aware safety control framework operating on a Detect-Then-Suppress paradigm. Unlike global safety interventions, SafeCtrl first employs an attention-guided Detect module to precisely localize specific risk regions. Subsequently, a localized Suppress module, optimized via image-level Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), neutralizes harmful semantics only within the detected areas, effectively transforming unsafe objects into safe alternatives while leaving the surrounding context intact. Extensive experiments across multiple risk categories demonstrate that SafeCtrl achieves a superior trade-off between safety and fidelity compared to state-of-the-art methods. Crucially, our approach exhibits improved resilience against adversarial prompt attacks, offering a precise and robust solution for responsible generation.

摘要（中文）: 文本到图像扩散模型的广泛部署正面临生成有害视觉内容（如色情、暴力和恐怖图像）的重大挑战。常见的安全干预措施，从输入过滤到模型概念消除，通常存在两个关键局限：（1）安全性与内容保真度之间存在严重权衡——消除不安全概念会损害安全内容的生成质量；（2）对对抗性攻击的脆弱性——安全机制易被绕过。为应对这些挑战，我们提出SafeCtrl，一种基于"检测-抑制"范式的区域感知安全控制框架。与全局安全干预不同，SafeCtrl首先采用注意力引导的检测模块精确定位特定风险区域，随后通过图像级直接偏好优化训练的局部抑制模块，仅对检测区域内的有害语义进行无害化处理，将不安全对象转化为安全替代物，同时保持周边内容完整。跨多风险类别的广泛实验表明，相较于现有最优方法，SafeCtrl在安全性与保真度间实现了更优的权衡。尤为重要的是，该方法展现出更强的对抗性提示攻击抵御能力，为负责任的内容生成提供了精准且鲁棒的解决方案。

TLDR: The paper introduces SafeCtrl, a region-aware safety control framework for text-to-image diffusion models that uses a detect-then-suppress approach to neutralize harmful content while preserving image fidelity.

简要说明: 本文提出了SafeCtrl，一种用于文本到图像扩散模型的区域感知安全控制框架，采用检测后抑制的方法来中和有害内容，同时保持图像保真度。

Authors: Lingyun Zhang, Yu Xie, Zhongli Fang, Yu Liu, Ping Chen

Training a Student Expert via Semi-Supervised Foundation Model Distillation

Abstract (English): Foundation models deliver strong perception but are often too computationally heavy to deploy, and adapting them typically requires costly annotations. We introduce a semi-supervised knowledge distillation (SSKD) framework that compresses pre-trained vision foundation models (VFMs) into compact experts using limited labeled and abundant unlabeled data, and instantiate it for instance segmentation where per-pixel labels are particularly expensive. The framework unfolds in three stages: (1) domain adaptation of the VFM(s) via self-training with contrastive calibration, (2) knowledge transfer through a unified multi-objective loss, and (3) student refinement to mitigate residual pseudo-label bias. Central to our approach is an instance-aware pixel-wise contrastive loss that fuses mask and class scores to extract informative negatives and enforce clear inter-instance margins. By maintaining this contrastive signal across both adaptation and distillation, we align teacher and student embeddings and more effectively leverage unlabeled images. On Cityscapes and ADE20K, our $\approx 11\times$ smaller student improves over its zero-shot VFM teacher(s) by +11.9 and +8.6 AP, surpasses adapted teacher(s) by +3.4 and +1.5 AP, and outperforms state-of-the-art SSKD methods on benchmarks.

摘要（中文）: 基础模型具备强大的感知能力，但通常因计算量过大而难以部署，且适应新任务通常需要高昂的标注成本。我们提出一种半监督知识蒸馏框架，利用有限的标注数据和大量无标注数据，将预训练的视觉基础模型压缩为紧凑的专家模型，并在像素级标注成本极高的实例分割任务中进行了具体实现。该框架分为三个阶段：（1）通过结合对比校准的自训练方法对视觉基础模型进行领域适应；（2）通过统一的多目标损失函数进行知识迁移；（3）对学生模型进行精炼以减少残留伪标签偏差。我们方法的核心在于实例感知的像素级对比损失，该损失融合了掩码与类别得分以提取信息丰富的负样本，并强化实例间的清晰边界。通过在适应和蒸馏阶段持续保持这种对比信号，我们实现了师生模型嵌入空间的对齐，并更有效地利用了无标注图像。在Cityscapes和ADE20K数据集上，我们体积缩小约11倍的学生模型相比其零样本视觉基础模型教师提升了11.9和8.6平均精度，较适应后的教师模型提升了3.4和1.5平均精度，并在基准测试中超越了现有最先进的半监督知识蒸馏方法。

Authors: Pardis Taghavi, Tian Liu, Renjie Li, Reza Langari, Zhengzhong Tu

Beyond Task-Driven Features for Object Detection

Abstract (English): Task-driven features learned by modern object detectors optimize end task loss yet often capture shortcut correlations that fail to reflect underlying annotation structure. Such representations limit transfer, interpretability, and robustness when task definitions change or supervision becomes sparse. This paper introduces an annotation-guided feature augmentation framework that injects embeddings into an object detection backbone. The method constructs dense spatial feature grids from annotation-guided latent spaces and fuses them with feature pyramid representations to influence region proposal and detection heads. Experiments across wildlife and remote sensing datasets evaluate classification, localization, and data efficiency under multiple supervision regimes. Results show consistent improvements in object focus, reduced background sensitivity, and stronger generalization to unseen or weakly supervised tasks. The findings demonstrate that aligning features with annotation geometry yields more meaningful representations than purely task optimized features.

摘要（中文）: 现代物体检测器学习到的任务驱动特征虽然优化了终端任务的损失函数，却往往捕捉到无法反映底层标注结构的捷径相关性。当任务定义发生变化或监督信息变得稀疏时，此类表征会限制模型的迁移能力、可解释性和鲁棒性。本文提出一种标注引导的特征增强框架，将嵌入向量注入物体检测主干网络。该方法通过标注引导的潜在空间构建稠密空间特征网格，并将其与特征金字塔表征融合，从而影响区域提案与检测头部。通过在野生动物和遥感数据集上进行多监督机制下的分类、定位及数据效率实验，结果表明该方法在目标聚焦、背景敏感性降低以及面向未见或弱监督任务的泛化能力方面均取得持续改进。研究证明，将特征与标注几何结构对齐所获得的表征比纯粹任务优化的特征更具实际意义。

TLDR: This paper proposes an annotation-guided feature augmentation framework for object detection, aiming to create more robust and generalizable features by aligning them with annotation geometry, rather than relying solely on task-driven optimization.

Authors: Meilun Zhou, Alina Zare

Cost-Efficient Multi-Scale Fovea for Semantic-Based Visual Search Attention

Abstract (English): Semantics are one of the primary sources of top-down preattentive information. Modern deep object detectors excel at extracting such valuable semantic cues from complex visual scenes. However, the size of the visual input to be processed by these detectors can become a bottleneck, particularly in terms of time costs, affecting an artificial attention system's biological plausibility and real-time deployability. Inspired by classical exponential density roll-off topologies, we apply a new artificial foveation module to our novel attention prediction pipeline: the Semantic-based Bayesian Attention (SemBA) framework. We aim at reducing detection-related computational costs without compromising visual task accuracy, thereby making SemBA more biologically plausible. The proposed multi-scale pyramidal field-of-view retains maximum acuity at an innermost level, around a focal point, while gradually increasing distortion for outer levels to mimic peripheral uncertainty via downsampling. In this work we evaluate the performance of our novel Multi-Scale Fovea, incorporated into \textit{SemBA}, on target-present visual search. We also compare it against other artificial foveal systems, and conduct ablation studies with different deep object detection models to assess the impact of the new topology in terms of computational costs. We experimentally demonstrate that including the new Multi-Scale Fovea module effectively reduces inherent processing costs while improving SemBA's scanpath prediction accuracy. Remarkably, we show that SemBA closely approximates human consistency while retaining the actual human fovea's proportions.

摘要（中文）: 语义是自上而下的前注意信息的主要来源之一。现代深度目标检测器擅长从复杂视觉场景中提取此类有价值的语义线索。然而，这些检测器需处理的视觉输入规模可能成为瓶颈，尤其在时间成本方面，会影响人工注意力系统的生物合理性与实时部署能力。受经典指数密度衰减拓扑的启发，我们将新的人工凹化模块应用于新颖的注意力预测流程：基于语义的贝叶斯注意力（SemBA）框架。我们的目标是在不影响视觉任务准确性的前提下降低检测相关的计算成本，从而增强SemBA的生物合理性。所提出的多尺度金字塔视野在围绕焦点的最内层保持最高清晰度，同时通过下采样逐步增加外层畸变以模拟外周不确定性。本研究评估了集成于\textit{SemBA}的新型多尺度凹化模块在目标存在视觉搜索任务中的表现，并与其他人造凹化系统进行对比。此外，我们通过采用不同深度目标检测模型开展消融实验，以评估新拓扑结构对计算成本的影响。实验证明，引入新型多尺度凹化模块能有效降低固有处理成本，同时提升SemBA扫描路径预测的准确性。值得注意的是，我们表明SemBA在保持人类实际凹区比例的同时，能高度逼近人类行为一致性。

Authors: João Luzio, Alexandre Bernardino, Plinio Moreno

ActivityForensics: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Localizing Manipulated Activity in Videos

Abstract (English): Temporal forgery localization aims to temporally identify manipulated segments in videos. Most existing benchmarks focus on appearance-level forgeries, such as face swapping and object removal. However, recent advances in video generation have driven the emergence of activity-level forgeries that modify human actions to distort event semantics, resulting in highly deceptive forgeries that critically undermine media authenticity and public trust. To overcome this issue, we introduce ActivityForensics, the first large-scale benchmark for localizing manipulated activity in videos. It contains over 6K forged video segments that are seamlessly blended into the video context, rendering high visual consistency that makes them almost indistinguishable from authentic content to the human eye. We further propose Temporal Artifact Diffuser (TADiff), a simple yet effective baseline that exposes artifact cues through a diffusion-based feature regularizer. Based on ActivityForensics, we introduce comprehensive evaluation protocols covering intra-domain, cross-domain, and open-world settings, and benchmark a wide range of state-of-the-art forgery localizers to facilitate future research. The dataset and code are available at https://activityforensics.github.io.

摘要（中文）: 时间伪造定位旨在从时间维度上识别视频中被篡改的片段。现有基准数据集多关注外观层面的伪造，如人脸替换和物体移除。然而，随着视频生成技术的进步，行为级伪造技术逐渐兴起——这类方法通过修改人物动作来扭曲事件语义，产生极具欺骗性的伪造内容，严重损害了媒体真实性与公众信任。为应对此问题，我们推出了ActivityForensics，首个针对视频行为篡改定位的大规模基准数据集。该数据集包含超过6000个伪造视频片段，这些片段被无缝嵌入原始视频语境中，呈现出高度的视觉一致性，使人眼几乎难以将其与真实内容区分。我们进一步提出时序伪影扩散器（TADiff），这是一种简单而有效的基线方法，通过基于扩散的特征正则化器来揭示伪影线索。基于ActivityForensics，我们构建了涵盖域内、跨域及开放世界场景的完整评估体系，并对当前最先进的多种伪造定位方法进行基准测试，以推动未来研究。数据集与代码已公开于https://activityforensics.github.io。

TLDR: This paper introduces ActivityForensics, a benchmark and method for detecting and temporally localizing manipulated human activities in videos, focusing on action-level forgeries.

Authors: Peijun Bao, Anwei Luo, Gang Pan, Alex C. Kot, Xudong Jiang

The Geometric Alignment Tax: Tokenization vs. Continuous Geometry in Scientific Foundation Models

Abstract (English): Foundation models for biology and physics optimize predictive accuracy, but their internal representations systematically fail to preserve the continuous geometry of the systems they model. We identify the root cause: the Geometric Alignment Tax, an intrinsic cost of forcing continuous manifolds through discrete categorical bottlenecks. Controlled ablations on synthetic dynamical systems demonstrate that replacing cross-entropy with a continuous head on an identical encoder reduces geometric distortion by up to 8.5x, while learned codebooks exhibit a non-monotonic double bind where finer quantization worsens geometry despite improving reconstruction. Under continuous objectives, three architectures differ by 1.3x; under discrete tokenization, they diverge by 3,000x. Evaluating 14 biological foundation models with rate-distortion theory and MINE, we identify three failure regimes: Local-Global Decoupling, Representational Compression, and Geometric Vacuity. A controlled experiment confirms that Evo 2's reverse-complement robustness on real DNA reflects conserved sequence composition, not learned symmetry. No model achieves simultaneously low distortion, high mutual information, and global coherence.

摘要（中文）: 生物学与物理学的基础模型虽然优化了预测准确性，但其内部表征系统性地无法保持所建模系统的连续几何结构。我们发现根本原因在于几何对齐代价——这是将连续流形强行通过离散分类瓶颈所固有的成本。通过对合成动力系统的受控消融实验，我们证明在相同编码器上使用连续输出头代替交叉熵损失，可将几何失真降低高达8.5倍；而学习型码本则呈现非单调的双重约束，即更精细的量化虽能改善重建质量，却会使几何结构进一步恶化。在连续目标函数下，三种架构的差异仅为1.3倍；而在离散标记化条件下，其差异会扩大至3000倍。运用率失真理论与MINE方法对14个生物基础模型进行评估，我们识别出三种失效机制：局部-全局解耦、表征压缩与几何空泛化。受控实验证实，Evo 2模型在真实DNA数据上表现出的反向互补稳健性反映的是序列组成的保守性，而非习得的对称性。当前没有任何模型能同时实现低失真度、高互信息量与全局连贯性。

TLDR: This paper identifies a 'Geometric Alignment Tax' where discrete tokenization in scientific foundation models (primarily for biology/physics) fails to preserve the continuous geometry of the systems they model, which is crucial for accurate representation.

简要说明: 该论文发现了一个'几何对齐税'：在科学基础模型中，离散化的标记化处理会破坏其建模系统的连续几何结构，这对于精准表示至关重要。

Authors: Prashant C. Raju

Jellyfish: Zero-Shot Federated Unlearning Scheme with Knowledge Disentanglement

Abstract (English): With the increasing importance of data privacy and security, federated unlearning emerges as a new research field dedicated to ensuring that once specific data is deleted, federated learning models no longer retain or disclose related information. In this paper, we propose a zero-shot federated unlearning scheme, named Jellyfish. It distinguishes itself from conventional federated unlearning frameworks in four key aspects: synthetic data generation, knowledge disentanglement, loss function design, and model repair. To preserve the privacy of forgotten data, we design a zero-shot unlearning mechanism that generates error-minimization noise as proxy data for the data to be forgotten. To maintain model utility, we first propose a knowledge disentanglement mechanism that regularises the output of the final convolutional layer by restricting the number of activated channels for the data to be forgotten and encouraging activation sparsity. Next, we construct a comprehensive loss function that incorporates multiple components, including hard loss, confusion loss, distillation loss, model weight drift loss, gradient harmonization, and gradient masking, to effectively align the learning trajectories of the objectives of ``forgetting" and ``retaining". Finally, we propose a zero-shot repair mechanism that leverages proxy data to restore model accuracy within acceptable bounds without accessing users' local data. To evaluate the performance of the proposed zero-shot federated unlearning scheme, we conducted comprehensive experiments across diverse settings. The results validate the effectiveness and robustness of the scheme.

摘要（中文）: 随着数据隐私与安全的重要性日益凸显，联邦遗忘学习作为一个新兴研究领域，致力于确保特定数据被删除后，联邦学习模型不再保留或泄露相关信息。本文提出了一种名为Jellyfish的零样本联邦遗忘学习方案。该方案在合成数据生成、知识解耦、损失函数设计和模型修复四个方面区别于传统联邦遗忘学习框架。为保护遗忘数据的隐私，我们设计了一种零样本遗忘机制，通过生成误差最小化噪声作为待遗忘数据的代理数据。为维持模型效用，我们首先提出一种知识解耦机制，通过限制待遗忘数据的激活通道数量并鼓励激活稀疏性，对最终卷积层输出进行正则化处理。其次，构建了包含硬损失、混淆损失、蒸馏损失、模型权重漂移损失、梯度协调与梯度掩蔽等多组件的综合损失函数，以有效对齐"遗忘"与"保留"目标的学习轨迹。最后，我们提出一种零样本修复机制，利用代理数据在不访问用户本地数据的情况下，将模型精度恢复至可接受范围。为评估所提出的零样本联邦遗忘学习方案性能，我们在多种场景下进行了全面实验，结果验证了该方案的有效性和鲁棒性。

TLDR: This paper proposes 'Jellyfish,' a zero-shot federated unlearning scheme that uses synthetic proxy data and knowledge disentanglement to remove specific data influences from a federated learning model without accessing local data.

Authors: Houzhe Wang, Xiaojie Zhu, Chi Chen

Nearly Optimal Best Arm Identification for Semiparametric Bandits

Abstract (English): We study fixed-confidence Best Arm Identification (BAI) in semiparametric bandits, where rewards are linear in arm features plus an unknown additive baseline shift. Unlike linear-bandit BAI, this setting requires orthogonalized regression, and its instance-optimal sample complexity has remained open. For the transductive setting, we establish an attainable instance-dependent lower bound characterized by the corresponding linear-bandit complexity on shifted features. We then propose a computationally efficient phase-elimination algorithm based on a new $XY$-design for orthogonalized regression. Our analysis yields a nearly optimal high-probability sample-complexity upper bound, up to log factors and an additive $d^2$ term, and experiments on synthetic instances and the Jester dataset show clear gains over prior baselines.

摘要（中文）: 本文研究了半参数多臂赌博机中固定置信度下的最优臂识别问题，其中奖励是臂特征的线性函数加上一个未知的加性基线偏移。与线性赌博机最优臂识别不同，此设定需要进行正交化回归，且其实例最优样本复杂度一直未有定论。在转导学习设定下，我们建立了一个可达的实例相关下界，该下界由偏移特征对应的线性赌博机复杂度刻画。随后，我们提出了一种基于新型$XY$设计正交化回归的计算高效阶段消除算法。理论分析表明，该算法在去除对数因子和附加$d^2$项后，能以高概率获得近乎最优的样本复杂度上界。在合成实例和Jester数据集上的实验结果显示，该算法较现有基线方法具有显著优势。

Authors: Seok-Jin Kim

BWTA: Accurate and Efficient Binarized Transformer by Algorithm-Hardware Co-design

Abstract (English): Ultra low-bit quantization brings substantial efficiency for Transformer-based models, but the accuracy degradation and limited GPU support hinder its wide usage. In this paper, we analyze zero-point distortion in binarization and propose a Binary Weights & Ternary Activations (BWTA) quantization scheme, which projects tiny values to zero and preserves the accuracy of extremely low-bit models. For training, we propose Smooth Multi-Stage Quantization, combining a Levelwise Degradation Strategy and a Magnitude-Alignment Projection Factor to enable stable and fast convergence. For inference, we develop a BWTA MatMul CUDA kernel with instruction-level parallel bit-packing and comprehensive binary/ternary MatMul implementations for both linear and attention operators, allowing seamless integration across Transformer architectures. Experiments show that BWTA approaches full-precision performance for BERT, with an average 3.5% drop on GLUE and less than 2% drop on five tasks, and achieves comparable perplexity and accuracy for LLMs. In efficiency, it delivers 16 to 24 times kernel-level speedup over FP16 on NVIDIA GPUs, and 216 to 330 tokens/s end-to-end prefill speedup with lower memory footprint on LLMs. As an algorithm-hardware co-design, BWTA demonstrates practical, low-latency ultra-low-bit inference without sacrificing model quality.

摘要（中文）: 超低位量化为基于Transformer的模型带来了显著的效率提升，但精度下降和有限的GPU支持阻碍了其广泛应用。本文分析了二值化过程中的零点失真问题，提出了一种二值权重与三值激活（BWTA）量化方案，该方案将微小值投影至零，从而保持极低位模型的精度。在训练阶段，我们提出平滑多阶段量化方法，结合层级递减策略与幅度对齐投影因子，以实现稳定快速的收敛。在推理阶段，我们开发了BWTA矩阵乘法的CUDA内核，采用指令级并行位压缩技术，并为线性算子和注意力算子提供了完整的二值/三值矩阵乘法实现，从而支持在Transformer架构中无缝集成。实验表明，BERT模型在采用BWTA后接近全精度性能，在GLUE基准上平均精度下降仅3.5%，其中五项任务下降小于2%；对于大语言模型，BWTA在困惑度和准确度上均达到可比水平。在效率方面，该方案在NVIDIA GPU上实现了相比FP16的16至24倍内核级加速，对大语言模型端到端预填充速度达到216至330 token/s，且内存占用更低。作为算法-硬件协同设计，BWTA在保证模型质量的同时，实现了实用化的低延迟超低位推理。

TLDR: This paper presents BWTA, an algorithm-hardware co-design for binarized Transformer models, achieving near full-precision accuracy and significant speedups for BERT and LLMs through novel quantization and custom CUDA kernels.

简要说明: 本文提出了BWTA，一种用于二值化Transformer模型的算法-硬件协同设计，通过新颖的量化方法和定制CUDA内核，在BERT和LLM上实现了接近全精度的精度和显著的加速效果。

Authors: Yifu Ding, Xianglong Liu, Shenghao Jin, Jinyang Guo, Jiwen Lu

Which English Do LLMs Prefer? Triangulating Structural Bias Towards American English in Foundation Models

Abstract (English): Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes domains, yet they expose only limited language settings, most notably "English (US)," despite the global diversity and colonial history of English. Through a postcolonial framing to explain the broader significance, we investigate how geopolitical histories of data curation, digital dominance, and linguistic standardization shape the LLM development pipeline. Focusing on two dominant standard varieties, American English (AmE) and British English (BrE), we construct a curated corpus of 1,813 AmE--BrE variants and introduce DiAlign, a dynamic, training-free method for estimating dialectal alignment using distributional evidence. We operationalize structural bias by triangulating evidence across three stages: (i) audits of six major pretraining corpora reveal systematic skew toward AmE, (ii) tokenizer analyses show that BrE forms incur higher segmentation costs, and (iii) generative evaluations show a persistent AmE preference in model outputs. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic and multi-faceted examination of dialectal asymmetries in standard English varieties across the phases of LLM development. We find that contemporary LLMs privilege AmE as the de facto norm, raising concerns about linguistic homogenization, epistemic injustice, and inequity in global AI deployment, while motivating practical steps toward more dialectally inclusive language technologies.

摘要（中文）: 大型语言模型正越来越多地部署于高风险领域，然而尽管英语具有全球多样性和殖民历史背景，这些模型却仅提供有限的语言设置选项，其中最为显著的是“英语（美国）”。本文通过后殖民主义理论框架阐释其深层意义，探究数据策展的地缘政治历史、数字霸权与语言标准化如何塑造大型语言模型的发展路径。聚焦于美国英语和英国英语这两种主流标准变体，我们构建了包含1,813组美英变体的精选语料库，并提出动态无训练对齐方法——通过分布证据评估方言对齐度。我们通过三重证据验证结构性偏见：（1）对六大预训练语料库的审计揭示系统性偏向美国英语；（2）分词器分析显示英国英语形式需要更高分词成本；（3）生成式评估表明模型输出持续偏好美国英语。据我们所知，这是首次对大型语言模型开发全流程中标准英语变体的方言不对称性进行系统化多维度检视。研究发现当代大型语言模型将美国英语默认为实际规范，这引发了关于语言同质化、认知不公以及全球人工智能部署不平等的担忧，同时为开发更具方言包容性的语言技术指明了实践方向。

TLDR: This paper investigates structural bias in LLMs, revealing a systematic preference for American English over British English through corpus audits, tokenizer analysis, and model evaluation, raising concerns about linguistic equity in AI.

简要说明: 本研究调查了大型语言模型中的结构性偏见，通过语料库审计、分词器分析和模型评估，揭示了模型对美国英语的系统性偏好，引发了关于AI部署中语言公平性的担忧。

Authors: Mir Tafseer Nayeem, Davood Rafiei

Graphic-Design-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating AI on Graphic Design Tasks

Abstract (English): We introduce GraphicDesignBench (GDB), the first comprehensive benchmark suite designed specifically to evaluate AI models on the full breadth of professional graphic design tasks. Unlike existing benchmarks that focus on natural-image understanding or generic text-to-image synthesis, GDB targets the unique challenges of professional design work: translating communicative intent into structured layouts, rendering typographically faithful text, manipulating layered compositions, producing valid vector graphics, and reasoning about animation. The suite comprises 50 tasks organized along five axes: layout, typography, infographics, template & design semantics and animation, each evaluated under both understanding and generation settings, and grounded in real-world design templates drawn from the LICA layered-composition dataset. We evaluate a set of frontier closed-source models using a standardized metric taxonomy covering spatial accuracy, perceptual quality, text fidelity, semantic alignment, and structural validity. Our results reveal that current models fall short on the core challenges of professional design: spatial reasoning over complex layouts, faithful vector code generation, fine-grained typographic perception, and temporal decomposition of animations remain largely unsolved. While high-level semantic understanding is within reach, the gap widens sharply as tasks demand precision, structure, and compositional awareness. GDB provides a rigorous, reproducible testbed for tracking progress toward AI systems that can function as capable design collaborators. The full evaluation framework is publicly available.

摘要（中文）: 本文介绍GraphicDesignBench（GDB）——首个专注于评估人工智能模型在专业平面设计全流程任务中表现的综合基准套件。与传统关注自然图像理解或通用文生图任务的基准不同，GDB针对专业设计工作的独特挑战：将传播意图转化为结构化版式、生成字体精准的文本、操控分层构图、生成有效矢量图形，以及理解动画逻辑。该套件包含按五大维度组织的50项任务：版式设计、字体排版、信息图表、模板与设计语义及动画设计，每项任务均在理解与生成两种模式下进行评估，并基于源自LICA分层构图数据集的真实设计模板构建。我们通过涵盖空间准确性、感知质量、文本保真度、语义对齐度和结构有效性等维度的标准化评估体系，对一系列前沿闭源模型进行了测试。实验结果表明，当前模型在专业设计的核心挑战上表现不足：复杂版式的空间推理、精准的矢量代码生成、细粒度字体感知及动画时序解构等任务尚未得到有效解决。尽管高层语义理解已初步实现，但当任务要求精度、结构性和构图意识时，模型能力仍存在显著差距。GDB为追踪AI系统向专业设计协作伙伴方向的发展提供了严谨、可复现的测试平台。完整的评估框架已向公众开放。

Authors: Adrienne Deganutti, Elad Hirsch, Haonan Zhu, Jaejung Seol, Purvanshi Mehta

Schema-Aware Planning and Hybrid Knowledge Toolset for Reliable Knowledge Graph Triple Verification

Abstract (English): Knowledge Graphs (KGs) serve as a critical foundation for AI systems, yet their automated construction inevitably introduces noise, compromising data trustworthiness. Existing triple verification methods, based on graph embeddings or language models, often suffer from single-source bias by relying on either internal structural constraints or external semantic evidence, and usually follow a static inference paradigm. As a result, they struggle with complex or long-tail facts and provide limited interpretability. To address these limitations, we propose SHARP (Schema-Hybrid Agent for Reliable Prediction), a training-free autonomous agent that reformulates triple verification as a dynamic process of strategic planning, active investigation, and evidential reasoning. Specifically, SHARP combines a Memory-Augmented Mechanism with Schema-Aware Strategic Planning to improve reasoning stability, and employs an enhanced ReAct loop with a Hybrid Knowledge Toolset to dynamically integrate internal KG structure and external textual evidence for cross-verification. Experiments on FB15K-237 and Wikidata5M-Ind show that SHARP significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art baselines, achieving accuracy gains of 4.2% and 12.9%, respectively. Moreover, SHARP provides transparent, fact-based evidence chains for each judgment, demonstrating strong interpretability and robustness for complex verification tasks.

摘要（中文）: 知识图谱作为人工智能系统的关键基石，其自动化构建过程不可避免地会引入噪声，从而影响数据可信度。现有的三元组验证方法基于图嵌入或语言模型，通常仅依赖内部结构约束或外部语义证据，存在单源偏差问题，且多遵循静态推理范式。这导致其难以处理复杂或长尾事实，并缺乏可解释性。为突破这些局限，我们提出SHARP（面向可靠预测的模式混合智能体），一种无需训练的自主体，将三元组验证重构为战略规划、主动探查与证据推理的动态过程。具体而言，SHARP通过记忆增强机制与模式感知战略规划提升推理稳定性，并采用增强的ReAct循环与混合知识工具集，动态整合知识图谱内部结构与外部文本证据进行交叉验证。在FB15K-237和Wikidata5M-Ind数据集上的实验表明，SHARP显著优于现有前沿基线模型，准确率分别提升4.2%和12.9%。此外，SHARP能为每个判断提供透明且基于事实的证据链，在复杂验证任务中展现出强大的可解释性与鲁棒性。

TLDR: The paper proposes SHARP, a training-free agent that reformulates knowledge graph triple verification as a dynamic process of planning and reasoning, combining internal graph structure with external semantic evidence to improve accuracy and interpretability.

简要说明: 该论文提出SHARP，一种无需训练的智能体，将知识图谱三元组验证重新定义为动态的规划与推理过程，通过结合内部图结构和外部语义证据来提高准确性和可解释性。

Authors: Xinyan Ma, Xianhao Ou, Weihao Zhang, Shixin Jiang, Runxuan Liu, Dandan Tu, Lei Chen, Ming Liu, Bing Qin

CoALFake: Collaborative Active Learning with Human-LLM Co-Annotation for Cross-Domain Fake News Detection

Abstract (English): The proliferation of fake news across diverse domains highlights critical limitations in current detection systems, which often exhibit narrow domain specificity and poor generalization. Existing cross-domain approaches face two key challenges: (1) reliance on labelled data, which is frequently unavailable and resource intensive to acquire and (2) information loss caused by rigid domain categorization or neglect of domain-specific features. To address these issues, we propose CoALFake, a novel approach for cross-domain fake news detection that integrates Human-Large Language Model (LLM) co-annotation with domain-aware Active Learning (AL). Our method employs LLMs for scalable, low-cost annotation while maintaining human oversight to ensure label reliability. By integrating domain embedding techniques, the CoALFake dynamically captures both domain specific nuances and cross-domain patterns, enabling the training of a domain agnostic model. Furthermore, a domain-aware sampling strategy optimizes sample acquisition by prioritizing diverse domain coverage. Experimental results across multiple datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach consistently outperforms various baselines. Our results emphasize that human-LLM co-annotation is a highly cost-effective approach that delivers excellent performance. Evaluations across several datasets show that CoALFake consistently outperforms a range of existing baselines, even with minimal human oversight.

摘要（中文）: 虚假信息在各领域的泛滥凸显了当前检测系统的关键局限性：其通常表现出狭窄的领域特异性与较差的泛化能力。现有跨领域检测方法面临两大挑战：（1）依赖标注数据，而这类数据往往难以获取且标注成本高昂；（2）僵化的领域划分或对领域特异性特征的忽视导致信息损失。为解决这些问题，我们提出CoALFake——一种融合人机协同标注与领域感知主动学习的跨领域虚假信息检测新方法。该方法利用大语言模型实现可扩展的低成本标注，同时通过人工监督确保标签可靠性。通过集成领域嵌入技术，CoALFake动态捕获领域特异性特征与跨领域模式，从而训练出领域无关的检测模型。此外，领域感知采样策略通过优先选择多样化的领域覆盖来优化样本采集。在多数据集上的实验结果表明，所提方法持续优于各类基线模型。我们的研究证实，人机协同标注是一种具有高成本效益且能实现卓越性能的解决方案。跨数据集评估显示，即使仅需极少人工监督，CoALFake仍能稳定超越现有多种基线方法。

TLDR: Proposes a novel approach for cross-domain fake news detection using human-LLM co-annotation and domain-aware active learning to address data scarcity and information loss.

Authors: Esma Aïmeur, Gilles Brassard, Dorsaf Sallami

Incomplete Multi-View Multi-Label Classification via Shared Codebook and Fused-Teacher Self-Distillation

Abstract (English): Although multi-view multi-label learning has been extensively studied, research on the dual-missing scenario, where both views and labels are incomplete, remains largely unexplored. Existing methods mainly rely on contrastive learning or information bottleneck theory to learn consistent representations under missing-view conditions, but loss-based alignment without explicit structural constraints limits the ability to capture stable and discriminative shared semantics. To address this issue, we introduce a more structured mechanism for consistent representation learning: we learn discrete consistent representations through a multi-view shared codebook and cross-view reconstruction, which naturally align different views within the limited shared codebook embeddings and reduce feature redundancy. At the decision level, we design a weight estimation method that evaluates the ability of each view to preserve label correlation structures, assigning weights accordingly to enhance the quality of the fused prediction. In addition, we introduce a fused-teacher self-distillation framework, where the fused prediction guides the training of view-specific classifiers and feeds the global knowledge back into the single-view branches, thereby enhancing the generalization ability of the model under missing-label conditions. The effectiveness of our proposed method is thoroughly demonstrated through extensive comparative experiments with advanced methods on five benchmark datasets. Code is available at https://github.com/xuy11/SCSD.

摘要（中文）: 尽管多视图多标签学习已得到广泛研究，但针对视图和标签同时缺失的双重缺失场景的研究仍处于探索不足的状态。现有方法主要依赖对比学习或信息瓶颈理论来学习缺失视图条件下的一致性表示，但缺乏显式结构约束的基于损失的对齐方式限制了捕捉稳定且具判别性的共享语义的能力。为解决这一问题，我们引入了一种更具结构化的表示学习机制：通过多视图共享码本和跨视图重构学习离散的一致性表示，从而将不同视图在有限的共享码本嵌入中自然对齐，并减少特征冗余。在决策层面，我们设计了一种权重评估方法，用于衡量各视图保留标签相关结构的能力，并据此分配权重以提升融合预测的质量。此外，我们提出了一种融合教师自蒸馏框架，其中融合预测指导各视图特定分类器的训练，并将全局知识反馈至单视图分支，从而增强模型在标签缺失条件下的泛化能力。通过在五个基准数据集上与先进方法进行广泛对比实验，充分证明了所提方法的有效性。代码发布于https://github.com/xuy11/SCSD。

TLDR: This paper proposes a method for incomplete multi-view multi-label classification, using a shared codebook and fused-teacher self-distillation to handle missing data in both views and labels.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种用于不完整多视图多标签分类的方法，通过共享码本和融合教师自蒸馏来处理视图和标签缺失的问题。

Authors: Xu Yan, Jun Yin, Shiliang Sun, Minghua Wan

Solar-VLM: Multimodal Vision-Language Models for Augmented Solar Power Forecasting

Abstract (English): Photovoltaic (PV) power forecasting plays a critical role in power system dispatch and market participation. Because PV generation is highly sensitive to weather conditions and cloud motion, accurate forecasting requires effective modeling of complex spatiotemporal dependencies across multiple information sources. Although recent studies have advanced AI-based forecasting methods, most fail to fuse temporal observations, satellite imagery, and textual weather information in a unified framework. This paper proposes Solar-VLM, a large-language-model-driven framework for multimodal PV power forecasting. First, modality-specific encoders are developed to extract complementary features from heterogeneous inputs. The time-series encoder adopts a patch-based design to capture temporal patterns from multivariate observations at each site. The visual encoder, built upon a Qwen-based vision backbone, extracts cloud-cover information from satellite images. The text encoder distills historical weather characteristics from textual descriptions. Second, to capture spatial dependencies across geographically distributed PV stations, a cross-site feature fusion mechanism is introduced. Specifically, a Graph Learner models inter-station correlations through a graph attention network constructed over a K-nearest-neighbor (KNN) graph, while a cross-site attention module further facilitates adaptive information exchange among sites. Finally, experiments conducted on data from eight PV stations in a northern province of China demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework. Our proposed model is publicly available at https://github.com/rhp413/Solar-VLM.

摘要（中文）: 光伏发电功率预测在电力系统调度和市场参与中扮演着关键角色。由于光伏发电对天气条件和云层运动高度敏感，精准预测需对多源信息间复杂的时空依赖性进行有效建模。尽管近期研究已推进基于人工智能的预测方法，但大多数未能将时序观测数据、卫星图像和文本气象信息融合至统一框架中。本文提出Solar-VLM——一种基于大语言模型驱动的多模态光伏功率预测框架。首先，我们构建了特定模态编码器以从异构输入中提取互补特征：时序编码器采用分块设计以捕获各站点多元观测数据中的时间模式；基于Qwen架构的视觉编码器从卫星图像中提取云层覆盖信息；文本编码器则从文本描述中提炼历史气象特征。其次，为捕捉地理分布式光伏电站间的空间依赖性，引入了跨站点特征融合机制：通过图学习器在K近邻图上构建图注意力网络以建模站点间关联性，同时跨站点注意力模块进一步促进站点间的自适应信息交互。最后，基于中国北方某省份八个光伏电站数据的实验验证了所提框架的有效性。本模型已公开于https://github.com/rhp413/Solar-VLM。

TLDR: Solar-VLM is a multimodal VLM framework for photovoltaic power forecasting, integrating time-series, satellite imagery, and textual weather data, with a focus on cross-site feature fusion.

简要说明: Solar-VLM 是一个用于光伏功率预测的多模态视觉语言模型框架，融合了时间序列、卫星图像和文本天气数据，重点在于跨站点特征融合。

Authors: Hang Fan, Haoran Pei, Runze Liang, Weican Liu, Long Cheng, Wei Wei

Many Preferences, Few Policies: Towards Scalable Language Model Personalization

Abstract (English): The holy grail of LLM personalization is a single LLM for each user, perfectly aligned with that user's preferences. However, maintaining a separate LLM per user is impractical due to constraints on compute, memory, and system complexity. We address this challenge by developing a principled method for selecting a small portfolio of LLMs that captures representative behaviors across heterogeneous users. We model user preferences across multiple traits (e.g., safety, humor, brevity) through a multi-dimensional weight vector. Given reward functions across these dimensions, our algorithm PALM (Portfolio of Aligned LLMs) generates a small portfolio of LLMs such that, for any weight vector, the portfolio contains a near-optimal LLM for the corresponding scalarized objective. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first result that provides theoretical guarantees on both the size and approximation quality of LLM portfolios for personalization. It characterizes the trade-off between system cost and personalization, as well as the diversity of LLMs required to cover the landscape of user preferences. We provide empirical results that validate these guarantees and demonstrate greater output diversity over common baselines.

摘要（中文）: 大语言模型个性化研究的终极目标是为每位用户配备一个完美契合其偏好的独立模型。然而，受限于计算资源、内存容量及系统复杂度，为每个用户维护独立模型并不现实。我们通过开发一种系统性方法来解决这一挑战：从异构用户群体中筛选出能够代表多样化行为特征的小规模模型组合。我们通过多维权重向量建模用户在多维度特性（如安全性、幽默感、简洁性）上的偏好。给定各维度的奖励函数，我们的PALM算法（对齐大语言模型组合）能够生成一个小规模模型组合，使得针对任意权重向量，该组合中都能找到对应线性加权目标下的近似最优模型。据我们所知，这是首个在个性化模型组合的规模与近似质量方面均提供理论保证的研究成果。该工作揭示了系统成本与个性化程度之间的权衡关系，以及覆盖用户偏好谱系所需的模型多样性边界。实验结果表明，该算法在满足理论保证的同时，相较于常见基线方法能产生更具多样性的输出结果。

TLDR: This paper presents a method for creating a small portfolio of LLMs to efficiently cover diverse user preferences, with theoretical guarantees on portfolio size and approximation quality.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种构建小型LLM组合的方法，以高效覆盖多样化的用户偏好，并在组合规模和近似质量方面提供了理论保证。

Authors: Cheol Woo Kum, Jai Moondra, Roozbeh Nahavandi, Andrew Perrault, Milind Tambe, Swati Gupta

Learning Robust Visual Features in Computed Tomography Enables Efficient Transfer Learning for Clinical Tasks

Abstract (English): There is substantial interest in developing artificial intelligence systems to support radiologists across tasks ranging from segmentation to report generation. Existing computed tomography (CT) foundation models have largely focused on building generalist vision-language systems capable of tasks such as question answering and report generation. However, training reliable vision-language systems requires paired image-text data at a scale that remains unavailable in CT. Moreover, adapting the underlying visual representations to downstream tasks typically requires partial or full backbone fine-tuning, a computationally demanding process inaccessible to many research groups. Instead, foundation models should prioritise learning robust visual representations that enable efficient transfer to new tasks with minimal labelled data and without backbone fine-tuning. We present VoxelFM, a 3D CT foundation model trained with self-distillation using the DINO framework, which learns semantically rich features without language supervision. We evaluated VoxelFM across seven categories of clinically relevant downstream tasks using frozen backbone representations with lightweight probes: classification, regression, survival analysis, instance retrieval, localisation, segmentation, and report generation. VoxelFM matched or outperformed four existing CT foundation models across all task categories. Despite receiving no language supervision during pre-training, VoxelFM surpassed models explicitly trained with language-alignment objectives, including on report generation. Our results indicate that current CT foundation models perform significantly better as feature extractors for lightweight probes rather than as vision encoders for vision-language models. Model weights and training code are publicly available.

摘要（中文）: 当前，开发人工智能系统以支持放射科医生完成从分割到报告生成等一系列任务备受关注。现有的计算机断层扫描（CT）基础模型主要聚焦于构建能够完成问答和报告生成等任务的通用视觉语言系统。然而，训练可靠的视觉语言系统需要大规模配对的图像文本数据，这在CT领域尚未实现。此外，将底层视觉表征适应下游任务通常需要部分或全部的骨干网络微调，这一计算量巨大的过程对许多研究团队而言难以实现。因此，基础模型应优先学习稳健的视觉表征，使其能够以最少的标注数据且无需骨干网络微调即可高效迁移至新任务。我们提出VoxelFM，一种基于DINO框架通过自蒸馏训练的三维CT基础模型，该模型无需语言监督即可学习语义丰富的特征。我们使用轻量级探测头与冻结骨干网络表征，在七类临床相关下游任务中对VoxelFM进行了评估：分类、回归、生存分析、实例检索、定位、分割和报告生成。在所有任务类别中，VoxelFM均达到或超越了现有四种CT基础模型的表现。尽管在预训练阶段未接受任何语言监督，VoxelFM在包括报告生成在内的任务上仍优于那些显式采用语言对齐目标训练的模型。我们的研究结果表明，当前CT基础模型作为轻量级探测头的特征提取器，其表现显著优于作为视觉语言模型的视觉编码器。模型权重与训练代码均已公开。

TLDR: This paper presents VoxelFM, a 3D computed tomography (CT) foundation model trained with self-distillation to learn robust visual features for efficient transfer to clinical tasks like classification and segmentation, without language supervision.

简要说明: 本文提出了VoxelFM，一种通过自蒸馏训练的3D计算机断层扫描（CT）基础模型，旨在学习鲁棒的视觉特征，用于高效迁移到分类、分割等临床任务，无需语言监督。

Authors: Rubén Moreno-Aguado, Alba Magallón, Victor Moreno, Yingying Fang, Guang Yang

InsTraj: Instructing Diffusion Models with Travel Intentions to Generate Real-world Trajectories

Abstract (English): The generation of realistic and controllable GPS trajectories is a fundamental task for applications in urban planning, mobility simulation, and privacy-preserving data sharing. However, existing methods face a two-fold challenge: they lack the deep semantic understanding to interpret complex user travel intent, and struggle to handle complex constraints while maintaining the realistic diversity inherent in human behavior. To resolve this, we introduce InsTraj, a novel framework that instructs diffusion models to generate high-fidelity trajectories directly from natural language descriptions. Specifically, InsTraj first utilizes a powerful large language model to decipher unstructured travel intentions formed in natural language, thereby creating rich semantic blueprints and bridging the representation gap between intentions and trajectories. Subsequently, we proposed a multimodal trajectory diffusion transformer that can integrate semantic guidance to generate high-fidelity and instruction-faithful trajectories that adhere to fine-grained user intent. Comprehensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that InsTraj significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in generating trajectories that are realistic, diverse, and semantically faithful to the input instructions.

摘要（中文）: 生成真实且可控的GPS轨迹是城市规划、移动模拟和隐私保护数据共享应用中的基础任务。然而，现有方法面临双重挑战：它们缺乏对复杂用户出行意图的深层语义理解，且在处理复杂约束的同时难以保持人类行为固有的真实多样性。为解决这一问题，我们提出了InsTraj这一创新框架，通过指令引导扩散模型直接从自然语言描述中生成高保真轨迹。具体而言，InsTraj首先利用强大的大型语言模型解析自然语言形成的非结构化出行意图，从而创建丰富的语义蓝图，并弥合意图与轨迹之间的表示鸿沟。随后，我们提出了一种多模态轨迹扩散变换器，能够融合语义引导，生成符合细粒度用户意图的高保真、高指令遵从度的轨迹。在真实数据集上的综合实验表明，InsTraj在生成真实性、多样性且语义忠实于输入指令的轨迹方面显著优于现有最先进方法。

TLDR: This paper introduces InsTraj, a framework using large language models to interpret travel intent and instruct diffusion models to generate realistic, instruction-faithful GPS trajectories for applications like urban planning and mobility simulation.

简要说明: 本文提出InsTraj框架，利用大型语言模型解析出行意图，并指导扩散模型生成符合自然语言描述、逼真的GPS轨迹，主要应用于城市规划、移动性模拟等领域。

Authors: Yuanshao Zhu, Yuxuan Liang, Xiangyu Zhao, Liang Han, Xinwei Fang, Xuetao Wei, James Jianqiao Yu

Compliance-by-Construction Argument Graphs: Using Generative AI to Produce Evidence-Linked Formal Arguments for Certification-Grade Accountability

Abstract (English): High-stakes decision systems increasingly require structured justification, traceability, and auditability to ensure accountability and regulatory compliance. Formal arguments commonly used in the certification of safety-critical systems provide a mechanism for structuring claims, reasoning, and evidence in a verifiable manner. At the same time, generative artificial intelligence systems are increasingly integrated into decision-support workflows, assisting with drafting explanations, summarizing evidence, and generating recommendations. However, current deployments often rely on language models as loosely constrained assistants, which introduces risks such as hallucinated reasoning, unsupported claims, and weak traceability. This paper proposes a compliance-by-construction architecture that integrates Generative AI (GenAI) with structured formal argument representations. The approach treats each AI-assisted step as a claim that must be supported by verifiable evidence and validated against explicit reasoning constraints before it becomes part of an official decision record. The architecture combines four components: i) a typed Argument Graph representation inspired by assurance-case methods, ii) retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to draft argument fragments grounded in authoritative evidence, iii) a reasoning and validation kernel enforcing completeness and admissibility constraints, and iv) a provenance ledger aligned with the W3C PROV standard to support auditability. We present a system design and an evaluation strategy based on enforceable invariants and worked examples. The analysis suggests that deterministic validation rules can prevent unsupported claims from entering the decision record while allowing GenAI to accelerate argument construction.

摘要（中文）: 高风险决策系统日益要求结构化的论证、可追溯性和可审计性，以确保问责与监管合规。安全关键系统认证中常用的形式化论证方法，为以可验证方式组织主张、推理与证据提供了机制。与此同时，生成式人工智能系统正日益融入决策支持工作流，协助起草解释、总结证据并生成建议。然而，当前部署方案往往依赖语言模型作为松散约束的辅助工具，这引入了诸如虚假推理、无依据主张和弱可追溯性等风险。本文提出一种"构造即合规"架构，将生成式人工智能与结构化形式化论证表示相结合。该方法将每个AI辅助步骤视为必须由可验证证据支持、并经过显式推理约束检验的主张，然后方可纳入正式决策记录。该架构整合了四个核心组件：i) 受保障案例方法启发的类型化论证图表示；ii) 基于检索增强生成的权威证据驱动论证片段草拟机制；iii) 强制执行完备性与可采纳性约束的推理验证内核；iv) 符合W3C PROV标准的溯源账本以支持审计。我们提出了基于可执行不变量的系统设计与评估策略，并通过实际案例进行验证。分析表明，确定性验证规则能够防止无依据主张进入决策记录，同时允许生成式人工智能加速论证构建过程。

TLDR: This paper proposes a compliance-by-construction framework that integrates Generative AI with formal argument graphs to produce structured, evidence-linked justifications for high-stakes decision systems, aiming to improve traceability and auditability.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种合规性构建框架，将生成式AI与形式化论证图相结合，为高风险决策系统生成结构化的、证据关联的论证依据，旨在提高可追溯性和可审计性。

Authors: Mahyar T. Moghaddam

Toward a Sustainable Software Architecture Community: Evaluating ICSA's Environmental Impact

Abstract (English): Generative AI (GenAI) tools are increasingly integrated into software architecture research, yet the environmental impact of their computational usage remains largely undocumented. This study presents the first systematic audit of the carbon footprint of both the digital footprint from GenAI usage in research papers, and the traditional footprint from conference activities within the context of the IEEE International Conference on Software Architecture (ICSA). We report two separate carbon inventories relevant to the software architecture research community: i) an exploratory estimate of the footprint of GenAI inference usage associated with accepted papers within a research-artifact boundary, and ii) the conference attendance and operations footprint of ICSA 2025 (travel, accommodation, catering, venue energy, and materials) within the conference time boundary. These two inventories, with different system boundaries and completeness, support transparency and community reflection. We discuss implications for sustainable software architecture, including recommendations for transparency, greener conference planning, and improved energy efficiency in GenAI operations. Our work supports a more climate-conscious research culture within the ICSA community and beyond

摘要（中文）: 生成式人工智能（GenAI）工具正日益融入软件架构研究领域，但其计算使用对环境的影响却鲜有记录。本研究首次对IEEE国际软件架构大会（ICSA）背景下，研究论文中GenAI使用产生的数字碳足迹以及传统会议活动产生的碳足迹进行了系统性核算。我们报告了与软件架构研究界相关的两项独立碳清单：一是在研究范畴边界内，对已录用论文中GenAI推理使用所产生碳足迹的探索性估算；二是在会议时间边界内，ICSA 2025大会的参会与运营碳足迹（包括差旅、住宿、餐饮、场馆能耗及物料）。这两项具有不同系统边界和完整度的清单，旨在支持透明度与学界反思。我们探讨了其对可持续软件架构的启示，包括透明度建议、绿色会议规划以及提升GenAI运行能效的策略。本工作致力于在ICSA社群及更广泛领域推动更具气候意识的研究文化建设。

TLDR: This paper audits the carbon footprint of Generative AI usage in software architecture research papers and the conference operations of IEEE ICSA, proposing recommendations for sustainability.

简要说明: 本研究审计了软件架构研究论文中生成式AI使用的碳足迹和IEEE ICSA会议运营的碳足迹，并提出了可持续性建议。

Authors: Mahyar T. Moghaddam, Mina Alipour, Torben Worm, Mikkel Baun Kjærgaard

From Paper to Program: A Multi-Stage LLM-Assisted Workflow for Accelerating Quantum Many-Body Algorithm Development

Abstract (English): Translating quantum many-body theory into scalable software traditionally requires months of effort. Zero-shot generation of tensor network algorithms by Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently fails due to spatial reasoning errors and memory bottlenecks. We resolve this using a multi-stage workflow that mimics a physics research group. By generating a mathematically rigorous LaTeX specification as an intermediate blueprint, we constrain the coding LLM to produce exact, matrix-free $\mathcal{O}(D^3)$ operations. We validate this approach by generating a Density-Matrix Renormalization Group (DMRG) engine that accurately captures the critical entanglement scaling of the Spin-$1/2$ Heisenberg model and the symmetry-protected topological (SPT) order of the Spin-$1$ AKLT model. Testing across 16 combinations of leading foundation models yielded a 100\% success rate. By compressing a months-long development cycle into under 24 hours ($\sim 14$ active hours), this framework offers a highly reproducible paradigm for accelerating computational physics research.

摘要（中文）: 将量子多体理论转化为可扩展的软件传统上需要数月时间。大型语言模型在零样本生成张量网络算法时，常因空间推理错误与内存瓶颈而失败。本研究通过模拟物理学研究小组的多阶段工作流程解决了这一问题。我们首先生成数学严谨的LaTeX规范作为中间蓝图，以此约束编码型大语言模型生成精确的、无矩阵的$\mathcal{O}(D^3)$复杂度运算。通过生成能准确捕捉自旋-1/2海森堡模型临界纠缠标度行为与自旋-1 AKLT模型对称保护拓扑序的密度矩阵重整化群计算引擎，我们验证了该方法的有效性。在对16种领先基础模型组合的测试中，该方法实现了100%的成功率。该框架将长达数月的开发周期压缩至24小时内（约14个活跃工时），为加速计算物理研究提供了高度可复现的范式。

TLDR: This paper presents a multi-stage LLM-assisted workflow for generating quantum many-body physics algorithms, achieving 100% success in generating a DMRG engine for specific models, accelerating a months-long development cycle to under 24 hours.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种多阶段LLM辅助工作流，用于生成量子多体物理算法，在生成特定模型的DMRG引擎上实现了100%的成功率，将长达数月的开发周期缩短到24小时以内。

Authors: Yi Zhou

Intelligent Traffic Monitoring with YOLOv11: A Case Study in Real-Time Vehicle Detection

Abstract (English): Recent advancements in computer vision, driven by artificial intelligence, have significantly enhanced monitoring systems. One notable application is traffic monitoring, which leverages computer vision alongside deep learning-based object detection and counting. We present an offline, real-time traffic monitoring system that couples a pre-trained YOLOv11 detector with BoT-SORT/ByteTrack for multi-object tracking, implemented in PyTorch/OpenCV and wrapped in a Qt-based desktop UI. The CNN pipeline enables efficient vehicle detection and counting from video streams without cloud dependencies. Across diverse scenes, the system achieves (66.67-95.83%) counting accuracy. Class-wise detection yields high precision (cars: 0.97-1.00; trucks: 1.00) with strong recall (cars: 0.82-1.00; trucks: 0.70-1.00), resulting in F1 scores of (0.90-1.00 for cars and 0.82-1.00 for trucks). While adverse weather conditions may negatively impact this performance, results remain robust in typical conditions. By integrating lightweight models with an accessible, cloud-independent interface, this paper contributes to the modernization and development of future smart cities by showing the capacity of AI-driven traffic monitoring systems.

摘要（中文）: 人工智能驱动的计算机视觉技术近期取得显著进展，极大地提升了监控系统的性能。其中，交通监控作为一项重要应用，结合了计算机视觉与基于深度学习的物体检测与计数技术。本文提出一种离线实时交通监控系统，该系统将预训练的YOLOv11检测器与BoT-SORT/ByteTrack多目标追踪算法相结合，基于PyTorch/OpenCV框架实现，并封装于Qt桌面界面中。该卷积神经网络处理流程能够从视频流中高效实现车辆检测与计数，且无需依赖云端服务。在多种场景测试中，系统计数准确率达到（66.67-95.83%）。分类型检测表现出高精度（轿车：0.97-1.00；卡车：1.00）与强召回率（轿车：0.82-1.00；卡车：0.70-1.00），由此获得的F1分数分别为（轿车0.90-1.00，卡车0.82-1.00）。尽管恶劣天气条件可能影响系统性能，但在常规环境下其表现保持稳定。通过将轻量化模型与可访问、非云依赖的界面相结合，本文展示了人工智能驱动的交通监控系统在推动智慧城市现代化与未来发展方面的潜力。

Authors: Shkelqim Sherifi

FactReview: Evidence-Grounded Reviews with Literature Positioning and Execution-Based Claim Verification

Abstract (English): Peer review in machine learning is under growing pressure from rising submission volume and limited reviewer time. Most LLM-based reviewing systems read only the manuscript and generate comments from the paper's own narrative. This makes their outputs sensitive to presentation quality and leaves them weak when the evidence needed for review lies in related work or released code. We present FactReview, an evidence-grounded reviewing system that combines claim extraction, literature positioning, and execution-based claim verification. Given a submission, FactReview identifies major claims and reported results, retrieves nearby work to clarify the paper's technical position, and, when code is available, executes the released repository under bounded budgets to test central empirical claims. It then produces a concise review and an evidence report that assigns each major claim one of five labels: Supported, Supported by the paper, Partially supported, In conflict, or Inconclusive. In a case study on CompGCN, FactReview reproduces results that closely match those reported for link prediction and node classification, yet also shows that the paper's broader performance claim across tasks is not fully sustained: on MUTAG graph classification, the reproduced result is 88.4%, whereas the strongest baseline reported in the paper remains 92.6%. The claim is therefore only partially supported. More broadly, this case suggests that AI is most useful in peer review not as a final decision-maker, but as a tool for gathering evidence and helping reviewers produce more evidence-grounded assessments. The code is public at https://github.com/DEFENSE-SEU/Review-Assistant.

摘要（中文）: 机器学习领域的同行评审正面临日益增长的投稿量与有限的审稿时间带来的双重压力。大多数基于大语言模型的评审系统仅阅读稿件并从论文自身叙述中生成评论，这使得其输出结果容易受到论文呈现质量的影响，且当评审所需证据依赖于相关工作或已公开代码时表现薄弱。本文提出FactReview——一个基于证据的评审系统，该系统结合了主张提取、文献定位和基于执行的主张验证。针对给定投稿，FactReview识别其主要主张和报告结果，检索相关文献以明确论文的技术定位，并在代码可用时通过有限资源执行已发布代码库，以检验核心实证主张。系统随后生成简明评审意见及证据报告，为每项主要主张标注五类标签之一：经外部验证支持、仅由论文本身支持、部分支持、存在冲突或证据不足。在针对CompGCN的案例研究中，FactReview复现了与链路预测和节点分类报告中高度吻合的结果，同时指出论文关于跨任务性能的广泛主张未能得到完全支撑：在MUTAG图分类任务中，复现结果为88.4%，而论文报道的最强基线仍为92.6%。因此该主张仅获得部分支持。更广泛而言，本案例表明人工智能在同行评审中最有价值的应用并非作为最终决策者，而是作为证据收集工具，帮助评审人做出更基于证据的评估。代码已公开于https://github.com/DEFENSE-SEU/Review-Assistant。

TLDR: Presents FactReview, an AI system for evidence-grounded peer review in ML, which verifies paper claims via literature positioning and executing released code, but does not address robotics or embodied AI.

简要说明: 提出了FactReview，一种用于机器学习中基于证据的同行评审的AI系统，它通过文献定位和执行发布的代码来验证论文主张，但并未涉及机器人或具身AI。

Authors: Hang Xu, Ling Yue, Chaoqian Ouyang, Libin Zheng, Shaowu Pan, Shimin Di, Min-Ling Zhang

CoopGuard: Stateful Cooperative Agents Safeguarding LLMs Against Evolving Multi-Round Attacks

Abstract (English): As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in complex applications, their vulnerability to adversarial attacks raises urgent safety concerns, especially those evolving over multi-round interactions. Existing defenses are largely reactive and struggle to adapt as adversaries refine strategies across rounds. In this work, we propose CoopGuard , a stateful multi-round LLM defense framework based on cooperative agents that maintains and updates an internal defense state to counter evolving attacks. It employs three specialized agents (Deferring Agent, Tempting Agent, and Forensic Agent) for complementary round-level strategies, coordinated by System Agent, which conditions decisions on the evolving defense state (interaction history) and orchestrates agents over time. To evaluate evolving threats, we introduce the EMRA benchmark with 5,200 adversarial samples across 8 attack types, simulating progressively LLM multi-round attacks. Experiments show that CoopGuard reduces attack success rate by 78.9% over state-of-the-art defenses, while improving deceptive rate by 186% and reducing attack efficiency by 167.9%, offering a more comprehensive assessment of multi-round defense. These results demonstrate that CoopGuard provides robust protection for LLMs in multi-round adversarial scenarios.

摘要（中文）: 随着大型语言模型在复杂应用中的日益广泛部署，其在多轮交互场景下对抗攻击的脆弱性引发了紧迫的安全担忧。现有防御机制多为被动响应，难以在攻击者跨轮次优化策略时有效适应。本研究提出CoopGuard——一种基于协作智能体的多轮防御框架，通过维护并更新内部防御状态来应对持续演变的攻击。该框架部署了三个功能互补的专精智能体（延缓智能体、诱探智能体与溯源智能体），由系统智能体根据动态演化的防御状态（交互历史记录）进行协调决策，实现跨轮次策略调度。为评估动态威胁，我们构建了包含8类攻击类型、总计5,200个对抗样本的EMRA基准测试集，模拟渐进式多轮攻击。实验表明，相比前沿防御方案，CoopGuard将攻击成功率降低78.9%，同时将攻击识别率提升186%，攻击效率降低167.9%，为多轮防御提供了更全面的评估维度。这些结果证明CoopGuard能为多轮对抗场景下的大型语言模型提供鲁棒性保护。

TLDR: CoopGuard is a multi-round defense framework using cooperative agents with state to protect LLMs against evolving adversarial attacks, significantly reducing attack success rates on a new benchmark.

简要说明: CoopGuard是一个基于状态型协作智能体的多轮LLM防御框架，通过维护和更新防御状态来应对演化攻击，在新建基准上显著降低了攻击成功率。

Authors: Siyuan Li, Zehao Liu, Xi Lin, Qinghua Mao, Yuliang Chen, Haoyu Li, Jun Wu, Jianhua Li, Xiu Su

Geometric Limits of Knowledge Distillation: A Minimum-Width Theorem via Superposition Theory

Abstract (English): Knowledge distillation compresses large teachers into smaller students, but performance saturates at a loss floor that persists across training methods and objectives. We argue this floor is geometric: neural networks represent far more features than dimensions through superposition, and a student of width $d_S$ can encode at most $d_S \cdot g(α)$ features, where $g(α) = 1/((1-α)\ln\frac{1}{1-α})$ is a sparsity-dependent capacity function. Features beyond this budget are permanently lost, yielding an importance-weighted loss floor. We validate on a toy model (48 configurations, median accuracy >93%) and on Pythia-410M, where sparse autoencoders measure $F \approx 28{,}700$ features at $α\approx 0.992$ (critical width $d_S^* \approx 1{,}065$). Distillation into five student widths confirms the predicted monotonic floor ordering. The observed floor decomposes into a geometric component and a width-independent architectural baseline ($R^2 = 0.993$). Linear probing shows coarse concepts survive even 88% feature loss, revealing the floor arises from aggregate loss of fine-grained features in the importance distribution's long tail. Our results connect representation geometry to distillation limits and provide a practical tool for predicting distillation performance from SAE measurements alone.

摘要（中文）: 知识蒸馏将大型教师模型压缩成小型学生模型，但其性能会饱和于一个损失下限，该下限在不同训练方法和目标中持续存在。我们认为这一下限具有几何本质：神经网络通过叠加表示的特征数量远超过其维度数，而宽度为$d_S$的学生模型最多只能编码$d_S \cdot g(α)$个特征，其中$g(α) = 1/((1-α)\ln\frac{1}{1-α})$是一个依赖于稀疏性的容量函数。超出此预算的特征将永久丢失，从而产生重要性加权的损失下限。我们在玩具模型（48种配置，中位准确率>93%）和Pythia-410M模型上进行了验证，其中稀疏自编码器测得在$α\approx 0.992$时特征数$F \approx 28{,}700$（临界宽度$d_S^* \approx 1{,}065$）。对五种学生模型宽度的蒸馏实验证实了预测的单调下限排序关系。观测到的损失下限可分解为几何分量与宽度无关的架构基线（$R^2 = 0.993$）。线性探测表明即使损失88%的特征，粗粒度概念仍能保留，这揭示了下限源于重要性分布长尾中细粒度特征的累积损失。我们的研究结果将表示几何与蒸馏极限联系起来，并提供了一种仅通过稀疏自编码器测量即可预测蒸馏性能的实用工具。

TLDR: This paper presents a theoretical and empirical analysis of the inherent performance limits in knowledge distillation, attributing the loss floor to the geometric constraints of feature representation capacity in narrower student networks.

简要说明: 本文从理论和实证角度分析了知识蒸馏的性能极限，将损失下限归因于较窄学生网络中特征表示能力的几何约束。

Authors: Dawar Jyoti Deka, Nilesh Sarkar

Can LLMs Learn to Reason Robustly under Noisy Supervision?

Abstract (English): Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) effectively trains reasoning models that rely on abundant perfect labels, but its vulnerability to unavoidable noisy labels due to expert scarcity remains critically underexplored. In this work, we take the first step toward a systematic analysis of noisy label mechanisms in RLVR. In contrast to supervised classification, most RLVR algorithms incorporate a rollout-based condition: a label's influence on training is contingent on whether the current policy can generate rollouts that realize it, a property that naturally extends to noisy labels. Based on this observation, we distinguish two types of noise: inactive noisy labels, which reduce data efficiency, and active noisy labels, which are reinforced and risk skewing the model toward incorrect distributions. From experiments on training with noisy samples, we identify an Early Correctness Coherence phenomenon: although noisy samples begin to lag behind in later stages, accuracy on both clean and noisy samples increases similarly in early training. Motivated by this dynamic, we propose Online Label Refinement (OLR), which progressively corrects potentially noisy labels with majority-voted answers when two conditions hold: a positive slope in the majority answer's rollout pass rate and stable historical consistency across updates, enabling gradual self-correction as the policy improves. We evaluate OLR on six in-distribution mathematical reasoning benchmarks (AIME24/25, AMC, MATH-500, Minerva, and Olympiad) and three out-of-distribution tasks (ARC-c, GPQA-diamond, and MMLU-pro). Across noise ratios from 0.1 to 0.9, OLR consistently improves robustness under both inactive and active noisy-label settings, achieving average gains of 3.6% to 3.9% on in-distribution benchmarks and 3.3% to 4.6% on out-of-distribution evaluations.

摘要（中文）: 具有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）能够有效训练依赖于大量完美标签的推理模型，但其在专家稀缺条件下对不可避免的噪声标签的脆弱性尚未得到充分研究。本文首次对RLVR中的噪声标签机制进行系统性分析。与监督分类不同，大多数RLVR算法包含基于推演的约束条件：标签对训练的影响取决于当前策略能否生成实现该标签的推演轨迹，这一特性自然延伸至噪声标签。基于此观察，我们区分两类噪声：非活跃噪声标签（降低数据效率）与活跃噪声标签（被强化并可能使模型偏向错误分布）。通过在噪声样本训练中的实验，我们发现了早期正确一致性现象：尽管噪声样本在训练后期逐渐滞后，但在早期训练阶段，干净样本与噪声样本的准确率提升趋势相似。受此动态特性启发，我们提出在线标签优化方法（OLR），该方法在满足两个条件时逐步修正潜在噪声标签：多数答案的推演通过率呈正增长趋势，且历史更新具有稳定一致性，从而在策略改进过程中实现渐进式自我校正。我们在六个分布内数学推理基准测试（AIME24/25、AMC、MATH-500、Minerva和Olympiad）和三个分布外任务（ARC-c、GPQA-diamond和MMLU-pro）上评估OLR。在0.1至0.9的噪声比例范围内，OLR在非活跃与活跃噪声标签设置下均持续提升鲁棒性，在分布内基准测试中平均获得3.6%至3.9%的性能提升，在分布外评估中平均获得3.3%至4.6%的增益。

TLDR: This paper proposes a method (Online Label Refinement) to improve the robustness of reasoning models trained with Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) when faced with noisy supervision labels, evaluated on mathematical reasoning benchmarks.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种方法（在线标签细化），用于在训练带有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）推理模型时，提高其在面对噪声监督标签时的鲁棒性，并在数学推理基准上进行了评估。

Authors: Shenzhi Yang, Guangcheng Zhu, Bowen Song, Sharon Li, Haobo Wang, Xing Zheng, Yingfan Ma, Zhongqi Chen, Weiqiang Wang, Gang Chen

Gram-Anchored Prompt Learning for Vision-Language Models via Second-Order Statistics

Abstract (English): Parameter-efficient prompt learning has become the de facto standard for adapting Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to downstream tasks. Existing approaches predominantly focus on aligning text prompts with first-order visual features (i.e., spatial feature maps). While effective for fine-grained semantic discrimination, we argue that relying solely on first-order information is insufficient for robust adaptation, as these spatially entangled features are highly susceptible to domain shifts and local noise. In this work, we propose \textbf{Gram-Anchored Prompt Learning (GAPL)} for Vision-Language Models via Second-Order Statistics, a framework that synergizes local semantic alignment with global structural consistency. Methodologically, we introduce an additional second-order statistical stream via \textbf{Gram matrices} that augments the standard first-order spatial interaction. By anchoring prompts to these second-order priors, our approach enables language representations to dynamically adapt to statistical distribution shifts across diverse domains. Extensive experiments indicate the effectiveness of the second-order features, and show compelling performances of GAPL on various benchmarks.

摘要（中文）: 参数高效的提示学习已成为将视觉语言模型（VLMs）适配到下游任务的实际标准。现有方法主要关注将文本提示与一阶视觉特征（即空间特征图）对齐。尽管这些方法在细粒度语义区分上表现有效，但我们认为仅依赖一阶信息不足以实现稳健的适配，因为这些空间纠缠特征极易受到域偏移和局部噪声的影响。本研究提出一种基于二阶统计量的视觉语言模型 **Gram矩阵锚定提示学习（GAPL）** 框架，该框架将局部语义对齐与全局结构一致性协同整合。在方法上，我们通过 **Gram矩阵** 引入额外的二阶统计流，以增强标准的一阶空间交互。通过将提示锚定于这些二阶先验信息，我们的方法使语言表征能够动态适应跨域统计分布的变化。大量实验验证了二阶特征的有效性，并表明GAPL在多个基准测试中取得了显著性能。

TLDR: Proposes Gram-Anchored Prompt Learning (GAPL), a method for adapting Vision-Language Models that augments standard first-order spatial feature alignment with a second-order statistical stream using Gram matrices for more robust adaptation to domain shifts.

简要说明: 提出了一种用于视觉语言模型调优的Gram锚定提示学习（GAPL）方法，通过引入Gram矩阵的第二阶统计信息流来增强标准的一阶空间特征对齐，以提升模型面对域迁移时的鲁棒性。

Authors: Minglei Chen, Weilong Wang, Jiang Duan, Ye Deng

Quantifying Trust: Financial Risk Management for Trustworthy AI Agents

Abstract (English): Prior work on trustworthy AI emphasizes model-internal properties such as bias mitigation, adversarial robustness, and interpretability. As AI systems evolve into autonomous agents deployed in open environments and increasingly connected to payments or assets, the operational meaning of trust shifts to end-to-end outcomes: whether an agent completes tasks, follows user intent, and avoids failures that cause material or psychological harm. These risks are fundamentally product-level and cannot be eliminated by technical safeguards alone because agent behavior is inherently stochastic. To address this gap between model-level reliability and user-facing assurance, we propose a complementary framework based on risk management. Drawing inspiration from financial underwriting, we introduce the \textbf{Agentic Risk Standard (ARS)}, a payment settlement standard for AI-mediated transactions. ARS integrates risk assessment, underwriting, and compensation into a single transaction framework that protects users when interacting with agents. Under ARS, users receive predefined and contractually enforceable compensation in cases of execution failure, misalignment, or unintended outcomes. This shifts trust from an implicit expectation about model behavior to an explicit, measurable, and enforceable product guarantee. We also present a simulation study analyzing the social benefits of applying ARS to agentic transactions. ARS's implementation can be found at https://github.com/t54-labs/AgenticRiskStandard.

摘要（中文）: 先前关于可信人工智能的研究主要侧重于模型内部属性，如偏见缓解、对抗鲁棒性和可解释性。随着人工智能系统逐渐发展为部署在开放环境中的自主智能体，并日益与支付或资产连接，信任的操作含义已转向端到端的结果：智能体能否完成任务、遵循用户意图，并避免导致物质或心理损害的故障。这些风险本质上是产品层面的，仅凭技术保障措施无法完全消除，因为智能体行为本质上具有随机性。为了弥合模型级可靠性与面向用户的保障之间的差距，我们提出了一种基于风险管理的补充框架。借鉴金融承保的灵感，我们引入了 **智能体风险标准（ARS）**，这是一个针对人工智能中介交易的支付结算标准。ARS 将风险评估、承保和补偿整合到一个统一的交易框架中，在与智能体交互时保护用户。在 ARS 标准下，当智能体执行失败、行为偏离预期或产生非预期结果时，用户可获得预先定义且具有合同约束力的补偿。这使信任从对模型行为的隐性期望转变为一种明确、可衡量且可强制执行的产品保障。我们还提供了一项模拟研究，分析了将 ARS 应用于智能体交易所带来的社会效益。ARS 的实现可在 https://github.com/t54-labs/AgenticRiskStandard 找到。

TLDR: Proposes a financial risk management framework for trustworthy AI agents, shifting trust from model reliability to enforceable product guarantees.

简要说明: 提出一个用于可信AI代理的金融风险管理框架，将信任从模型可靠性转向可执行的产品保证。
