Authors: Wenyue Hua, Tianyi Peng, Chi Wang, Ian Kaufman, Bryan Lim, Chandler Fang

Diagonal-Tiled Mixed-Precision Attention for Efficient Low-Bit MXFP Inference

Abstract (English): Transformer-based large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance across a wide range of real-world tasks, but their inference cost remains prohibitively high due to the quadratic complexity of attention and the memory bandwidth limitations of high-precision operations. In this work, we present a low-bit mixed-precision attention kernel using the microscaling floating-point (MXFP) data format, utilizing the computing capability on next-generation GPU architectures. Our Diagonal-Tiled Mixed-Precision Attention (DMA) incorporates two kinds of low-bit computation at the tiling-level, and is a delicate fused kernel implemented using Triton, exploiting hardware-level parallelism and memory efficiency to enable fast and efficient inference without compromising model performance. Extensive empirical evaluations on NVIDIA B200 GPUs show that our kernel maintains generation quality with negligible degradation, and meanwhile achieves significant speedup by kernel fusion. We release our code at https://github.com/yifu-ding/MP-Sparse-Attn.

摘要（中文）: 基于Transformer的大型语言模型（LLM）在众多现实任务中展现出卓越性能，但由于注意力机制的二次复杂度及高精度操作的内存带宽限制，其推理成本仍然过高。本文提出一种基于微缩放浮点（MXFP）数据格式的低位混合精度注意力核，利用新一代GPU架构的计算能力。我们设计的对角线分块混合精度注意力（DMA）在分块级别融合了两种低位计算，并通过Triton实现精细化的融合核，充分利用硬件级并行性与内存效率，在不损失模型性能的前提下实现快速高效的推理。在NVIDIA B200 GPU上的大量实验评估表明，该核在保持生成质量仅轻微下降的同时，通过核融合实现了显著的加速效果。代码已发布于https://github.com/yifu-ding/MP-Sparse-Attn。

TLDR: This paper introduces a low-bit mixed-precision attention kernel for efficient LLM inference, focusing on reducing computation and memory costs for next-generation GPUs.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种用于高效LLM推理的低位混合精度注意力内核，重点在于为新一代GPU减少计算和内存成本。

Authors: Yifu Ding, Xinhao Zhang, Jinyang Guo

CODE-GEN: A Human-in-the-Loop RAG-Based Agentic AI System for Multiple-Choice Question Generation

Abstract (English): We present CODE-GEN, a human-in-the-Loop, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG)-based agentic AI system for generating context-aligned multiple-choice questions to develop student code reasoning and comprehension abilities. CODE-GEN employs an agentic AI architecture in which a Generator agent produces multiple-choice coding comprehension questions aligned with course-specific learning objectives, while a Validator agent independently assesses content quality across seven pedagogical dimensions. Both agents are augmented with specialized tools that enhance computational accuracy and verify code outputs. To evaluate the effectiveness of CODE-GEN, we conducted an evaluation study involving six human subject-matter experts (SMEs) who judged 288 AI-generated questions. The SMEs produced a total of 2,016 human-AI rating pairs, indicating agreement or disagreement with the assessments of Validator, along with 131 instances of qualitative feedback. Analyses of SME judgments show strong system performance, with human-validated success rates ranging from 79.9% to 98.6% across the seven pedagogical dimensions. The analysis of qualitative feedback reveals that CODE-GEN achieves high reliability on dimensions well suited to computational verification and explicit criteria matching, including question clarity, code validity, concept alignment, and correct answer validity. In contrast, human expertise remains essential for dimensions requiring deeper instructional judgment, such as designing pedagogically meaningful distractors and providing high-quality feedback that reinforces understanding. These findings inform the strategic allocation of human and AI effort in AI-assisted educational content generation.

摘要（中文）: 本文提出CODE-GEN，一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的人机协同智能体系统，用于生成与课程内容相匹配的选择题，旨在提升学生的代码推理与理解能力。该系统采用智能体架构：生成智能体根据特定课程学习目标创编编程理解选择题，验证智能体则从七个教学维度独立评估内容质量。两大智能体均配备专业工具以提升计算准确性并验证代码输出。为评估CODE-GEN效能，我们开展了一项包含六位领域专家参与的评估研究，专家对288道AI生成题目进行评判。研究共获得2,016组人机评分对（反映专家与验证智能体评估的一致性与分歧）及131条定性反馈。领域专家评判分析表明系统性能卓越，七大教学维度的人工验证成功率介于79.9%至98.6%之间。定性反馈分析显示，CODE-GEN在适于计算验证与明确标准匹配的维度表现高度可靠，包括问题清晰度、代码有效性、概念对齐及正确答案有效性。而在需要深层教学判断的维度——如设计具有教学意义的干扰项、提供促进理解的高质量反馈——人类专业经验仍不可或缺。这些发现为人工智能辅助教育内容生成中人力与AI资源的战略分配提供了实践依据。

TLDR: This paper presents CODE-GEN, a human-in-the-loop, RAG-based agentic AI system for generating multiple-choice coding comprehension questions for educational purposes, with independent validation of content quality.

简要说明: 本文介绍了CODE-GEN，一个用于生成编程理解多选题的教育内容的人机协作、基于检索增强生成（RAG）的智能AI系统，并包含对内容质量的独立验证。

Authors: Xiaojing Duan, Frederick Nwanganga, Chaoli Wang

AdaptFuse: Training-Free Sequential Preference Learning via Externalized Bayesian Inference

Abstract (English): Large language models struggle to accumulate evidence across multiple rounds of user interaction, failing to update their beliefs in a manner consistent with Bayesian inference. Existing solutions require fine-tuning on sensitive user interaction data, limiting their applicability in privacy-conscious settings. We propose AdaptFuse, a training-free framework that externalizes probabilistic computation entirely from the LLM: a symbolic module maintains a Bayesian posterior over a discrete hypothesis set, while a frozen LLM contributes semantic reasoning via multi-sample Dirichlet aggregation. The two signals are combined through entropy-adaptive fusion, which automatically weights each source by its predictive confidence, shifting reliance from the LLM to the symbolic posterior as evidence accumulates. We evaluate across three domains: flight recommendation, hotel recommendation, and web shopping; on Gemma 2 9B, Llama 3 8B, and Qwen 2.5 7B. AdaptFuse consistently outperforms both prompting baselines and fine-tuned Bayesian Teaching models on all tasks, with accuracy improving monotonically over interaction rounds. These results demonstrate that principled inference-time algorithms can substitute for fine-tuning in personalized recommendation, without storing or training on sensitive user data. All the code and materials will be open-sourced.

摘要（中文）: 大型语言模型难以在多次用户交互过程中积累证据，无法以符合贝叶斯推断的方式更新其信念。现有解决方案需在敏感用户交互数据上进行微调，这限制了其在注重隐私场景中的适用性。我们提出AdaptFuse——一种无需训练的框架，将概率计算完全从大语言模型中外部化：符号模块在离散假设集上维护贝叶斯后验分布，而冻结的大语言模型则通过多样本狄利克雷聚合贡献语义推理能力。两种信号通过熵自适应融合进行结合，该机制根据预测置信度自动加权各信号源，随着证据积累逐步将依赖从大语言模型转向符号后验。我们在三个领域进行评估：航班推荐、酒店推荐和网络购物；测试模型包括Gemma 2 9B、Llama 3 8B和Qwen 2.5 7B。在所有任务中，AdaptFuse均持续优于提示基线和微调贝叶斯教学模型，且准确率随交互轮次单调递增。这些结果表明，基于原则的推理时算法可替代微调方法应用于个性化推荐，无需存储或训练敏感用户数据。所有代码与资料将开源发布。

TLDR: A training-free framework that externalizes Bayesian inference from an LLM for sequential decision-making in personalized recommendation, using a symbolic module and a frozen LLM to accumulate user preference evidence without fine-tuning.

简要说明: 一种无需训练的框架，将贝叶斯推理外部化到LLM之外，用于个性化推荐中的顺序决策，利用符号模块和冻结的LLM在不微调的情况下累积用户偏好证据。

Authors: Fangzhou Lin, Peiran Li, Shuo Xing, Siyuan Yang, Qianwen Ge, Kazunori Yamada, Ziming Zhang, Haichong Zhang, Zhengzhong Tu

Uncertainty as a Planning Signal: Multi-Turn Decision Making for Goal-Oriented Conversation

Abstract (English): Goal-oriented conversational systems require making sequential decisions under uncertainty about the user's intent, where the algorithm must balance information acquisition and target commitment over multiple turns. Existing approaches address this challenge from different perspectives: structured methods enable multi-step planning but rely on predefined schemas, while LLM-based approaches support flexible interactions but lack long-horizon decision making, resulting in poor coordination between information acquisition and target commitment. To address this limitation, we formulate goal-oriented conversation as an uncertainty-aware sequential decision problem, where uncertainty serves as a guiding signal for multi-turn decision making. We propose a Conversation Uncertainty-aware Planning framework (CUP) that integrates language models with structured planning: a language model proposes feasible actions, and a planner evaluates their long-term impact on uncertainty reduction. Experiments on multiple conversational benchmarks show that CUP consistently improves success rates while requiring fewer interaction turns. Further analysis demonstrates that uncertainty-aware planning contributes to more efficient information acquisition and earlier confident commitment.

摘要（中文）: 面向目标的对话系统需要在用户意图不确定的情况下做出顺序决策，此时算法必须在多轮对话中平衡信息获取与目标确认。现有方法从不同角度应对这一挑战：结构化方法支持多步规划但依赖预定义框架，而基于大语言模型的方法虽能实现灵活交互却缺乏长期决策能力，导致信息获取与目标确认之间协调不足。为解决这一局限，我们将目标导向对话建模为不确定性感知的顺序决策问题，以不确定性作为多轮决策的引导信号。本文提出一种对话不确定性感知规划框架（CUP），将语言模型与结构化规划相结合：语言模型生成可行动作，规划器则评估这些动作对长期不确定性削减的影响。在多个对话基准测试上的实验表明，CUP在减少交互轮数的同时持续提升任务成功率。进一步分析证明，不确定性感知规划能促成更高效的信息获取与更早的确定性目标确认。

TLDR: The paper proposes a planning framework for goal-oriented conversational AI that uses uncertainty as a signal to coordinate multi-turn information gathering and decision-making, combining language models with structured planners.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种用于目标导向对话AI的规划框架，将不确定性作为信号来协调多轮信息收集和决策，结合了语言模型与结构化规划器。

Authors: Xinyi Ling, Ye Liu, Reza Averly, Xia Ning

LLM-Agent-based Social Simulation for Attitude Diffusion

Abstract (English): This paper introduces discourse_simulator, an open-source framework that combines LLMs with agent-based modelling. It offers a new way to simulate how public attitudes toward immigration change over time in response to salient events like protests, controversies, or policy debates. Large language models (LLMs) are used to generate social media posts, interpret opinions, and model how ideas spread through social networks. Unlike traditional agent-based models that rely on fixed, rule-based opinion updates and cannot generate natural language or consider current events, this approach integrates multidimensional sociological belief structures and real-world event timelines. This framework is wrapped into an open-source Python package that integrates generative agents into a small-world network topology and a live news retrieval system. discourse_sim is purpose-built as a social science research instrument specifically for studying attitude dynamics, polarisation, and belief evolution following real-world critical events. Unlike other LLM Agent Swarm frameworks, which treat the simulations as a prediction black box, discourse_sim treats it as a theory-testing instrument, which is fundamentally a different epistemological stance for studying social science problems. The paper further demonstrates the framework by modelling the Dublin anti-immigration march on April 26, 2025, with N=100 agents over a 15-day simulation. Package link: https://pypi.org/project/discourse-sim/

摘要（中文）: 本文介绍了 discourse_simulator，一个将大型语言模型（LLMs）与基于代理的建模相结合的开源框架。该框架提供了一种新的方法来模拟公众对移民的态度如何随时间变化，特别是在抗议、争议或政策辩论等显著事件发生时。大型语言模型被用于生成社交媒体帖子、解读观点，并模拟思想如何通过社交网络传播。与传统的基于代理的模型不同——这些模型依赖固定、基于规则的意见更新，无法生成自然语言或考虑当前事件——discourse_simulator 整合了多维社会学信念结构和真实世界的事件时间线。该框架被封装成一个开源 Python 包，将生成式智能体集成到小世界网络拓扑结构和实时新闻检索系统中。discourse_simulator 是专门为社会科学研究设计的工具，特别用于研究真实世界关键事件后的态度动态、两极分化和信念演化。不同于其他将模拟视为预测黑盒的 LLM 智能体群框架，discourse_simulator 将其视为理论检验工具，这代表了一种研究社会科学问题的根本不同的认识论立场。本文进一步通过模拟 2025 年 4 月 26 日都柏林反移民游行，在一个为期 15 天的模拟中使用 100 个代理，展示了该框架的应用。 包链接：https://pypi.org/project/discourse-sim/

Authors: Deepak John Reji

FeynmanBench: Benchmarking Multimodal LLMs on Diagrammatic Physics Reasoning

Abstract (English): Breakthroughs in frontier theory often depend on the combination of concrete diagrammatic notations with rigorous logic. While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) show promise in general scientific tasks, current benchmarks often focus on local information extraction rather than the global structural logic inherent in formal scientific notations. In this work, we introduce FeynmanBench, the first benchmark centered on Feynman diagram tasks. It is designed to evaluate AI's capacity for multistep diagrammatic reasoning, which requires satisfying conservation laws and symmetry constraints, identifying graph topology, converting between diagrammatic and algebraic representations, and constructing scattering amplitudes under specific conventions and gauges. To support large-scale and reproducible evaluation, we developed an automated pipeline producing diverse Feynman diagrams along with verifiable topological annotations and amplitude results. Our database spans the electromagnetic, weak, and strong interactions of the Standard Model, encompasses over 100 distinct types and includes more than 2000 tasks. Experiments on state-of-the-art MLLMs reveal systematic failure modes, including unstable enforcement of physical constraints and violations of global topological conditions, highlighting the need for physics-grounded benchmarks for visual reasoning over scientific notation. FeynmanBench provides a logically rigorous test of whether AI can effectively engage in scientific discovery, particularly within theoretical physics.

摘要（中文）: 前沿理论的突破往往依赖于具体图式记号与严谨逻辑的结合。虽然多模态大语言模型在通用科学任务中展现出潜力，但现有基准大多聚焦于局部信息提取，而未能关注形式化科学记号中固有的全局结构逻辑。本研究提出了首个以费曼图任务为核心的基准测试FeynmanBench，旨在评估人工智能进行多步图式推理的能力。该测试要求模型满足守恒律与对称性约束、识别图结构拓扑、实现图式与代数表达间的转换，并在特定规范与约定下构建散射振幅。为支持大规模可复现的评估，我们开发了自动化流程来生成多样化的费曼图，并提供可验证的拓扑标注与振幅计算结果。我们的数据库涵盖标准模型的电磁、弱与强相互作用，包含超过100种类型共2000余项任务。对当前先进多模态大语言模型的实验揭示了系统性的失效模式，包括物理约束执行的不稳定性和全局拓扑条件的违反，凸显了针对科学符号视觉推理建立基于物理原理的基准测试的必要性。FeynmanBench为检验人工智能能否有效参与科学发现——特别是理论物理领域的探索——提供了逻辑严谨的测试平台。

TLDR: This paper introduces FeynmanBench, a benchmark for evaluating multimodal large language models on diagrammatic reasoning tasks in theoretical physics, such as analyzing Feynman diagrams.

Authors: Zeyu Wang, Xiaogang Li, Peiyao Xiao, Qinhao Kong, Ben Wang, Chengliang Xu, Zichao Chen, Bing Zhao, Hu Wei

Regime-Calibrated Demand Priors for Ride-Hailing Fleet Dispatch and Repositioning

Abstract (English): Effective ride-hailing dispatch requires anticipating demand patterns that vary substantially across time-of-day, day-of-week, season, and special events. We propose a regime-calibrated approach that (i) segments historical trip data into demand regimes, (ii) matches the current operating period to the most similar historical analogues via a similarity ensemble combining Kolmogorov-Smirnov distance, Wasserstein-1 distance, feature distance, variance ratio, event pattern similarity, and temporal proximity, and (iii) uses the resulting calibrated demand prior to drive both an LP-based fleet repositioning policy and batch dispatch with Hungarian matching. In ablation, a distributional-only metric subset achieves the strongest mean-wait reduction, while the full ensemble is retained as a robustness-oriented default that preserves calendar and event context. Evaluated on 5.2 million NYC TLC trips across 8 diverse scenarios (winter/summer, weekday/weekend/holiday, morning/evening/night) with 5 random seeds each, our method reduces mean rider wait times by 31.1% (bootstrap 95% CI: [26.5, 36.6]; Friedman chi-squared = 80.0, p = 4.25e-18; Cohen's d = 7.5-29.9). P95 wait drops 37.6% and the Gini coefficient of wait times improves from 0.441 to 0.409. The two contributions compose multiplicatively: calibration provides 16.9% reduction relative to the replay baseline; LP repositioning adds a further 15.5%. The approach requires no training, is deterministic and explainable, generalizes to Chicago (23.3% wait reduction using the NYC-built regime library without retraining), and is robust across fleet sizes (32-47% improvement for 0.5x-2.0x fleet scaling). Code is available at https://github.com/IndarKarhana/regime-calibrated-dispatch.

摘要（中文）: 高效的网约车调度需要准确预测随时间（一天内、一周内、季节间）和特殊事件大幅波动的需求模式。本文提出一种体制校准方法：首先将历史行程数据划分为需求体制；其次通过结合柯尔莫哥洛夫-斯米尔诺夫距离、瓦瑟斯坦-1距离、特征距离、方差比、事件模式相似度与时间邻近度的综合相似性度量，将当前运营时段与最相似的历史类比时段进行匹配；最后利用校准后的需求先验驱动基于线性规划的车辆重定位策略和采用匈牙利匹配的批量派单机制。在消融实验中，纯分布度量子集实现了最强的平均等待时间降低效果，而完整集成方案作为面向鲁棒性的默认设置被保留，以维持日历与事件语境的一致性。 在纽约出租车委员会520万条行程数据构成的8种多元场景（冬季/夏季、工作日/周末/节假日、清晨/傍晚/夜间）中，每种场景采用5个随机种子进行测试。本方法使乘客平均等待时间降低31.1%（自助法95%置信区间：[26.5, 36.6]；弗里德曼卡方检验值=80.0，p=4.25e-18；科恩d值范围7.5-29.9），第95百分位等待时间下降37.6%，等待时间基尼系数从0.441改善至0.409。两项贡献呈乘数效应：相较于回放基线，校准环节贡献16.9%的降幅，线性规划重定位策略进一步带来15.5%的优化。该方法无需训练过程，具有确定性和可解释性，可推广至芝加哥市场（直接使用纽约构建的体制库即实现23.3%的等待时间降低），并在不同车队规模下保持鲁棒性（0.5-2.0倍车队规模缩放中实现32-47%的改进）。代码开源地址：https://github.com/IndarKarhana/regime-calibrated-dispatch。

TLDR: A method for ride-hailing dispatch that uses historical data segmentation and similarity matching to create calibrated demand priors, improving fleet repositioning and reducing rider wait times significantly.

简要说明: 一种针对网约车调度的新方法，通过历史数据分割和相似度匹配来创建校准的需求先验，从而优化车队调度并显著降低乘客等待时间。

Authors: Indar Kumar, Akanksha Tiwari

Enhancing behavioral nudges with large language model-based iterative personalization: A field experiment on electricity and hot-water conservation

Abstract (English): Nudging is widely used to promote behavioral change, but its effectiveness is often limited when recipients must repeatedly translate feedback into workable next steps under changing circumstances. Large language models (LLMs) may help reduce part of this cognitive work by generating personalized guidance and updating it iteratively across intervention rounds. We developed an LLM agent for iterative personalization and tested it in a three-arm randomized experiment among 233 university residents in China, using daily electricity and shower hot-water conservation as objectively measured cases differing in friction. LLM-personalized nudges (T2) produced the largest conservation effects, while image-enhanced conventional nudges (T1) and text-based conventional nudges (C) showed similar outcomes (omnibus p = 0.009). Relative to C, T2 reduced electricity consumption by 0.56 kWh per room-day (p = 0.014), corresponding to an 18.3 percentage-point higher adjusted saving rate. This advantage emerged within the first two intervention rounds, alongside iterative updating of personalized guidance, and persisted thereafter. Hot-water outcomes followed the same direction but were smaller, less precisely estimated, and attenuated over time, consistent with stronger friction in this domain. LLM-personalized nudges emphasized prospective and context-specific guidance and were associated with higher participant engagement. This study provides field evidence that LLM-based iterative personalization can enhance behavioral nudging, with behavioral friction as a potential boundary condition. Larger trials and extension to more behaviors are warranted.

摘要（中文）: 助推技术被广泛用于促进行为改变，但当接收者需要在不断变化的环境中反复将反馈转化为可行的后续步骤时，其效果往往有限。大型语言模型（LLM）可通过生成个性化指导并在多轮干预中持续迭代更新，从而帮助减少部分认知负担。我们开发了一种用于迭代个性化推送的LLM智能体，并在一项涉及中国233名高校住宿生的三臂随机实验中进行了测试，研究选取了客观可测量的每日节电与淋浴热水节约两个案例，二者在行为阻力方面存在差异。LLM个性化助推（T2）产生了最大的节能效果，而图像增强型传统助推（T1）与文本型传统助推（C）的效果相近（整体检验p=0.009）。相对于C组，T2组使每间宿舍日均用电量降低0.56千瓦时（p=0.014），相当于调整后的节电率提升了18.3个百分点。这一优势在干预前两轮中即随个性化指导的迭代更新而显现，并在此后持续保持。热水节约效果呈相同趋势，但幅度较小、估计精度较低，且随时间推移有所衰减，这与该领域更强的行为阻力特征相符。LLM个性化助推注重前瞻性和情境特异性指导，并与更高的参与者参与度相关。本研究为基于LLM的迭代个性化技术能够增强行为助推提供了实地证据，同时指出行为阻力可能构成其效果边界。未来有必要开展更大规模的试验，并将研究延伸至更多行为领域。

TLDR: This paper uses an LLM agent to iteratively generate personalized behavioral nudges, testing them in a field experiment on electricity and water conservation. The LLM-personalized nudges outperformed conventional nudges in reducing energy consumption, with effectiveness varying based on the level of friction in the task.

Authors: Zonghan Li, Yi Liu, Chunyan Wang, Song Tong, Kaiping Peng, Feng Ji

When Models Know More Than They Say: Probing Analogical Reasoning in LLMs

Abstract (English): Analogical reasoning is a core cognitive faculty essential for narrative understanding. While LLMs perform well when surface and structural cues align, they struggle in cases where an analogy is not apparent on the surface but requires latent information, suggesting limitations in abstraction and generalisation. In this paper we compare a model's probed representations with its prompted performance at detecting narrative analogies, revealing an asymmetry: for rhetorical analogies, probing significantly outperforms prompting in open-source models, while for narrative analogies, they achieve a similar (low) performance. This suggests that the relationship between internal representations and prompted behavior is task-dependent and may reflect limitations in how prompting accesses available information.

摘要（中文）: 类比推理是叙事理解中至关重要的核心认知能力。当表面特征与结构线索一致时，大语言模型表现良好，但在类比关系并非表面显性而需依赖潜在信息时，模型则表现出明显困难，这揭示了其在抽象与泛化能力上的局限。本文通过对比模型内部表征的探测结果与其在提示下识别叙事类比的表现，发现了一种不对称现象：在开源模型中，对于修辞性类比，探测方法显著优于提示方法；而对于叙事性类比，两种方法均表现较差且水平相近。这表明内部表征与提示行为之间的关系具有任务依赖性，可能反映了提示机制在调用可用信息时存在固有局限。

TLDR: This paper investigates analogical reasoning in LLMs, comparing probed internal representations to prompted performance on detecting narrative analogies, finding a task-dependent asymmetry in how models access information.

简要说明: 本文研究大语言模型中的类比推理能力，通过对比模型内部探测表征与提示性能，发现在检测叙事类比时存在任务依赖的信息访问不对称性。

Authors: Hope McGovern, Caroline Craig, Thomas Lippincott, Hale Sirin

Affording Process Auditability with QualAnalyzer: An Atomistic LLM Analysis Tool for Qualitative Research

Abstract (English): Large language models are increasingly used for qualitative data analysis, but many workflows obscure how analytic conclusions are produced. We present QualAnalyzer, an open-source Chrome extension for Google Workspace that supports atomistic LLM analysis by processing each data segment independently and preserving the prompt, input, and output for every unit. Through two case studies -- holistic essay scoring and deductive thematic coding of interview transcripts -- we show that this approach creates a legible audit trail and helps researchers investigate systematic differences between LLM and human judgments. We argue that process auditability is essential for making LLM-assisted qualitative research more transparent and methodologically robust.

摘要（中文）: 大型语言模型正日益被用于定性数据分析，但许多工作流程掩盖了分析结论的产生过程。本文介绍QualAnalyzer——一款为Google Workspace开发的开源Chrome扩展程序，该工具通过独立处理每个数据单元并完整保留每项分析的提示词、输入与输出，实现了原子化的大型语言模型分析。我们通过两个案例研究——整体性论文评分与访谈转录本的演绎式主题编码——证明该方法能生成可追溯的审计轨迹，帮助研究者系统探查大型语言模型与人类判断之间的差异。我们认为，过程可审计性对于提升大型语言模型辅助的定性研究的透明度与方法论严谨性具有至关重要的作用。

TLDR: This paper introduces QualAnalyzer, a tool for making LLM-assisted qualitative research more transparent by providing an audit trail for each data segment analysis.

Authors: Max Hao Lu, Ryan Ellegood, Rony Rodriguez-Ramirez, Sophia Blumert

AURA: Always-On Understanding and Real-Time Assistance via Video Streams

Abstract (English): Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs) have achieved strong performance on many video understanding tasks, but most existing systems remain offline and are not well-suited for live video streams that require continuous observation and timely response. Recent streaming VideoLLMs have made progress, yet current approaches often rely on decoupled trigger-response pipelines or are limited to captioning-style narration, reducing their effectiveness for open-ended question answering and long-horizon interaction. We propose AURA (Always-On Understanding and Real-Time Assistance), an end-to-end streaming visual interaction framework that enables a unified VideoLLM to continuously process video streams and support both real-time question answering and proactive responses. AURA integrates context management, data construction, training objectives, and deployment optimization for stable long-horizon streaming interaction. It achieves state-of-the-art performance on streaming benchmarks and supports a real-time demo system with ASR and TTS running at 2 FPS on two 80G accelerators. We release the AURA model together with a real-time inference framework to facilitate future research.

摘要（中文）: 视频大语言模型（VideoLLMs）在众多视频理解任务中展现出强大性能，但现有系统大多为离线模式，难以适配需要持续观察与实时响应的直播视频流。近期出现的流式视频大语言模型虽取得进展，但当前方法往往依赖解耦的触发-响应流程，或局限于字幕式描述叙述，限制了其在开放域问答和长程交互中的有效性。本文提出AURA（持续感知与实时辅助系统），这是一种端到端的流式视觉交互框架，使统一视频大语言模型能够持续处理视频流，同时支持实时问答与主动响应。AURA通过整合上下文管理、数据构建、训练目标和部署优化，实现了稳定的长程流式交互。该框架在流式基准测试中达到最先进性能，并支持集成自动语音识别与文本转语音的实时演示系统，在双80G加速器上以2帧/秒的速度稳定运行。我们将公开AURA模型及其实时推理框架，以推动未来研究。

TLDR: Proposes an end-to-end streaming VideoLLM framework for real-time video understanding and interaction, enabling continuous processing and response in live video streams.

简要说明: 提出一个端到端的流式视频大语言模型框架，用于实时视频理解和交互，支持对实时视频流进行连续处理和响应。

Authors: Xudong Lu, Yang Bo, Jinpeng Chen, Shuhan Li, Xintong Guo, Huankang Guan, Fang Liu, Dunyuan Xu, Peiwen Sun, Heyang Sun, Rui Liu, Hongsheng Li

Hierarchical Co-Embedding of Font Shapes and Impression Tags

Abstract (English): Font shapes can evoke a wide range of impressions, but the correspondence between fonts and impression descriptions is not one-to-one: some impressions are broadly compatible with diverse styles, whereas others strongly constrain the set of plausible fonts. We refer to this graded constraint strength as style specificity. In this paper, we propose a hyperbolic co-embedding framework that models font--impression correspondence through entailment rather than simple paired alignment. Font images and impression descriptions, represented as single tags or tag sets, are embedded in a shared hyperbolic space with two complementary entailment constraints: impression-to-font entailment and low-to-high style-specificity entailment among impressions. This formulation induces a radial structure in which low style-specificity impressions lie near the origin and high style-specificity impressions lie farther away, yielding an interpretable geometric measure of how strongly an impression constrains font style. Experiments on the MyFonts dataset demonstrate improved bidirectional retrieval over strong one-to-one baselines. In addition, traversal and tag-level analyses show that the learned space captures a coherent progression from ambiguous to more style-specific impressions and provides a meaningful, data-driven quantification of style specificity.

摘要（中文）: 字体形态能够唤起广泛的感官印象，但字体与印象描述之间的对应关系并非一一对应：某些印象可兼容多种字体风格，而另一些印象则对可能匹配的字体集合产生强烈限制。我们将这种梯度化的约束强度称为风格特异性。本文提出一种双曲协同嵌入框架，通过蕴含关系而非简单的配对对齐来建模字体与印象的对应关系。字体图像和印象描述（以单一标签或标签集形式表示）被嵌入到共享的双曲空间中，并受到两种互补的蕴含约束：印象到字体的蕴含关系，以及印象间从低到高的风格特异性蕴含关系。该框架构建出辐射状空间结构：低风格特异性印象靠近空间原点，高风格特异性印象则分布于更远端，从而形成可解释的几何度量，用以量化特定印象对字体风格的约束强度。在MyFonts数据集上的实验表明，该方法在双向检索任务中优于强一对一基线模型。此外，通过空间遍历和标签层级分析发现，所学空间能捕捉从模糊印象到高风格特异性印象的连贯递进关系，并为风格特异性提供了有意义的数据驱动量化标准。

TLDR: The paper proposes a hyperbolic co-embedding framework to model the correspondence between font images and descriptive impression tags, introducing the concept of 'style specificity' to measure how strongly an impression constrains visual style.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种双曲共同嵌入框架，用于建模字体图像与描述性印象标签之间的对应关系，引入了'风格特异性'概念来衡量印象对视觉风格的限制强度。

Authors: Yugo Kubota, Kaito Shiku, Seiichi Uchida

OP-GRPO: Efficient Off-Policy GRPO for Flow-Matching Models

Abstract (English): Post training via GRPO has demonstrated remarkable effectiveness in improving the generation quality of flow-matching models. However, GRPO suffers from inherently low sample efficiency due to its on-policy training paradigm. To address this limitation, we present OP-GRPO, the first Off-Policy GRPO framework tailored for flow-matching models. First, we actively select high-quality trajectories and adaptively incorporate them into a replay buffer for reuse in subsequent training iterations. Second, to mitigate the distribution shift introduced by off-policy samples, we propose a sequence-level importance sampling correction that preserves the integrity of GRPO's clipping mechanism while ensuring stable policy updates. Third, we theoretically and empirically show that late denoising steps yield ill-conditioned off-policy ratios, and mitigate this by truncating trajectories at late steps. Across image and video generation benchmarks, OP-GRPO achieves comparable or superior performance to Flow-GRPO with only 34.2% of the training steps on average, yielding substantial gains in training efficiency while maintaining generation quality.

摘要（中文）: 通过GRPO进行的后训练已证明在提升流匹配模型的生成质量方面效果显著。然而，由于GRPO采用在线策略训练范式，其样本效率存在固有缺陷。为克服这一局限，我们提出了OP-GRPO——首个专为流匹配模型设计的离线策略GRPO框架。首先，我们主动筛选高质量轨迹，并自适应地将其纳入回放缓冲区，以供后续训练迭代重复使用。其次，为缓解离线策略样本引入的分布偏移问题，我们提出了一种序列级重要性采样校正方法，在保持GRPO裁剪机制完整性的同时确保策略更新的稳定性。第三，我们从理论和实验上证明，后期去噪步骤会产生病态的离线策略比率，并通过在后期步骤截断轨迹来缓解这一问题。在图像与视频生成基准测试中，OP-GRPO仅需平均34.2%的训练步数即可达到与Flow-GRPO相当或更优的性能，在保持生成质量的同时实现了训练效率的大幅提升。

TLDR: OP-GRPO is an efficient off-policy training framework for flow-matching models that improves sample efficiency for image and video generation by leveraging a replay buffer and importance sampling.

简要说明: OP-GRPO是一种用于流匹配模型的高效离策略训练框架，通过利用回放缓冲区和重要性采样，提高了图像和视频生成的样本效率。

Authors: Liyu Zhang, Kehan Li, Tingrui Han, Tao Zhao, Yuxuan Sheng, Shibo He, Chao Li

Rethinking Exposure Correction for Spatially Non-uniform Degradation

Abstract (English): Real-world exposure correction is fundamentally challenged by spatially non-uniform degradations, where diverse exposure errors frequently coexist within a single image. However, existing exposure correction methods are still largely developed under a predominantly uniform assumption. Architecturally, they typically rely on globally aggregated modulation signals that capture only the overall exposure trend. From the optimization perspective, conventional reconstruction losses are usually derived under a shared global scale, thus overlooking the spatially varying correction demands across regions. To address these limitations, we propose a new exposure correction paradigm explicitly designed for spatial non-uniformity. Specifically, we introduce a Spatial Signal Encoder to predict spatially adaptive modulation weights, which are used to guide multiple look-up tables for image transformation, together with an HSL-based compensation module for improved color fidelity. Beyond the architectural design, we propose an uncertainty-inspired non-uniform loss that dynamically allocates the optimization focus based on local restoration uncertainties, better matching the heterogeneous nature of real-world exposure errors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior qualitative and quantitative performance compared with state-of-the-art methods. Code is available at https://github.com/FALALAS/rethinkingEC.

摘要（中文）: 真实世界的曝光校正面临着空间非均匀性退化的根本挑战，即单张图像中常同时存在多种类型的曝光误差。然而，现有曝光校正方法仍主要基于均匀性假设进行开发。在架构层面，这些方法通常依赖仅能捕捉整体曝光趋势的全局聚合调制信号；从优化角度看，传统的重建损失函数通常在全局共享尺度下构建，因而忽视了不同区域对校正需求的时空差异性。为突破这些局限，我们提出一种专为空间非均匀性设计的新型曝光校正范式。具体而言，我们引入空间信号编码器来预测空间自适应的调制权重，这些权重通过引导多个查找表实现图像变换，并结合基于HSL色彩空间的补偿模块以提升色彩保真度。在架构设计之外，我们提出一种基于不确定性的非均匀损失函数，该函数能够根据局部修复的不确定性动态分配优化重点，从而更好地匹配真实世界曝光误差的异构特性。大量实验表明，相较于现有先进方法，我们的方法在定性与定量评估中均取得了更优的性能。代码已公开于：https://github.com/FALALAS/rethinkingEC。

TLDR: This paper proposes a novel exposure correction method designed to handle spatially non-uniform degradation in images, using a Spatial Signal Encoder and an uncertainty-inspired loss for local adaptive correction.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种新的曝光校正方法，旨在处理图像中的空间非均匀退化问题，使用空间信号编码器和不确定性引导的损失函数进行局部自适应校正。

Authors: Ao Li, Jiawei Sun, Le Dong, Zhenyu Wang, Weisheng Dong

SARES-DEIM: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts Meets DETR for Robust SAR Ship Detection

Abstract (English): Ship detection in Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) imagery is fundamentally challenged by inherent coherent speckle noise, complex coastal clutter, and the prevalence of small-scale targets. Conventional detectors, primarily designed for optical imagery, often exhibit limited robustness against SAR-specific degradation and suffer from the loss of fine-grained ship signatures during spatial downsampling. To address these limitations, we propose SARES-DEIM, a domain-aware detection framework grounded in the DEtection TRansformer (DETR) paradigm. Central to our approach is SARESMoE (SAR-aware Expert Selection Mixture-of-Experts), a module leveraging a sparse gating mechanism to selectively route features toward specialized frequency and wavelet experts. This sparsely-activated architecture effectively filters speckle noise and semantic clutter while maintaining high computational efficiency. Furthermore, we introduce the Space-to-Depth Enhancement Pyramid (SDEP) neck to preserve high-resolution spatial cues from shallow stages, significantly improving the localization of small targets. Extensive experiments on two benchmark datasets demonstrate the superiority of SARES-DEIM. Notably, on the challenging HRSID dataset, our model achieves a mAP50:95 of 76.4% and a mAP50 of 93.8%, outperforming state-of-the-art YOLO-series and specialized SAR detectors.

摘要（中文）: 合成孔径雷达（SAR）图像中的舰船检测面临固有相干斑点噪声、复杂海岸杂波以及小尺度目标普遍存在的根本性挑战。传统检测器主要针对光学图像设计，在应对SAR特有退化时鲁棒性有限，且空间下采样过程中易丢失细粒度舰船特征。为克服这些局限，我们提出了SARES-DEIM——一种基于检测变换器（DETR）范式的领域感知检测框架。该方法的核心理念是SARESMoE（SAR感知专家选择混合专家）模块，其利用稀疏门控机制将特征选择性地路由至专用频率与小波专家。这种稀疏激活架构能有效滤除斑点噪声和语义杂波，同时保持高计算效率。此外，我们引入空间到深度增强金字塔（SDEP）颈部结构，以保留浅层阶段的高分辨率空间线索，显著提升小目标定位能力。在两个基准数据集上的大量实验验证了SARES-DEIM的优越性。特别是在具有挑战性的HRSID数据集上，我们的模型实现了76.4%的mAP50:95和93.8%的mAP50，性能优于当前最先进的YOLO系列及专用SAR检测器。

TLDR: The paper introduces SARES-DEIM, a novel ship detection method for Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) imagery, combining a DETR-based detector with a sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) module and a custom feature pyramid to address speckle noise and small targets, achieving state-of-the-art results on benchmark datasets.

简要说明: 该论文提出SARES-DEIM，一种用于合成孔径雷达（SAR）图像的新型船舶检测方法，将基于DETR的检测器与稀疏专家混合模块及定制特征金字塔相结合，以解决散斑噪声和小目标问题，在基准数据集上取得了最先进的性能。

Authors: Fenghao Song, Shaojing Yang, Xi Zhou

Detecting Media Clones in Cultural Repositories Using a Positive Unlabeled Learning Approach

Abstract (English): We formulate curator-in-the-loop duplicate discovery in the AtticPOT repository as a Positive-Unlabeled (PU) learning problem. Given a single anchor per artefact, we train a lightweight per-query Clone Encoder on augmented views of the anchor and score the unlabeled repository with an interpretable threshold on the latent l_2 norm. The system proposes candidates for curator verification, uncovering cross-record duplicates that were not verified a priori. On CIFAR-10 we obtain F1=96.37 (AUROC=97.97); on AtticPOT we reach F1=90.79 (AUROC=98.99), improving F1 by +7.70 points over the best baseline (SVDD) under the same lightweight backbone. Qualitative "find-similar" panels show stable neighbourhoods across viewpoint and condition. The method avoids explicit negatives, offers a transparent operating point, and fits de-duplication, record linkage, and curator-in-the-loop workflows.

摘要（中文）: 在AtticPOT数字仓储中，我们将策展人参与循环的重复项发现问题形式化为一个正例-未标记（PU）学习问题。通过为每个文物设定单一锚点，我们基于锚点的增强视图训练轻量化的单查询克隆编码器，并采用潜在l_2范数的可解释阈值对未标记仓储进行评分。该系统向策展人提供候选对象以供验证，从而发现先前未经核验的跨记录重复项。在CIFAR-10数据集上我们获得F1=96.37（AUROC=97.97）；在AtticPOT数据集上达到F1=90.79（AUROC=98.99），在相同轻量化骨干网络下较最佳基线方法（SVDD）的F1值提升7.70个百分点。定性的"查找相似"面板显示出跨视角与条件稳定的邻域分布。该方法避免了显式负例采样，提供透明的操作节点，适用于去重处理、记录关联及策展人参与循环的工作流程。

TLDR: This paper introduces a Positive-Unlabeled learning approach for detecting duplicate media items in cultural repositories, focusing on the AtticPOT database and using a per-query Clone Encoder to identify cross-record duplicates.

简要说明: 本研究提出一种基于正无标记学习的方法，用于检测文化资料库中的媒体克隆项目，聚焦于AtticPOT数据库，利用每查询克隆编码器来识别跨记录的重复项。

Authors: V. Sevetlidis, V. Arampatzakis, M. Karta, I. Mourthos, D. Tsiafaki, G. Pavlidis

ATSS: Detecting AI-Generated Videos via Anomalous Temporal Self-Similarity

Abstract (English): AI-generated videos (AIGVs) have achieved unprecedented photorealism, posing severe threats to digital forensics. Existing AIGV detectors focus mainly on localized artifacts or short-term temporal inconsistencies, thus often fail to capture the underlying generative logic governing global temporal evolution, limiting AIGV detection performance. In this paper, we identify a distinctive fingerprint in AIGVs, termed anomalous temporal self-similarity (ATSS). Unlike real videos that exhibit stochastic natural dynamics, AIGVs follow deterministic anchor-driven trajectories (e.g., text or image prompts), inducing unnaturally repetitive correlations across visual and semantic domains. To exploit this, we propose the ATSS method, a multimodal detection framework that exploits this insight via a triple-similarity representation and a cross-attentive fusion mechanism. Specifically, ATSS reconstructs semantic trajectories by leveraging frame-wise descriptions to construct visual, textual, and cross-modal similarity matrices, which jointly quantify the inherent temporal anomalies. These matrices are encoded by dedicated Transformer encoders and integrated via a bidirectional cross-attentive fusion module to effectively model intra- and inter-modal dynamics. Extensive experiments on four large-scale benchmarks, including GenVideo, EvalCrafter, VideoPhy, and VidProM, demonstrate that ATSS significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in terms of AP, AUC, and ACC metrics, exhibiting superior generalization across diverse video generation models. Code and models of ATSS will be released at https://github.com/hwang-cs-ime/ATSS.

摘要（中文）: AI生成视频（AIGV）已实现前所未有的真实感，对数字取证构成严重威胁。现有AIGV检测方法主要聚焦于局部伪影或短期时序不一致性，往往难以捕捉控制全局时序演化的深层生成逻辑，限制了检测性能。本文在AIGV中发现了一种独特指纹特征，称为异常时序自相似性（ATSS）。与展现随机自然动态的真实视频不同，AIGV遵循确定性锚点驱动轨迹（如文本或图像提示），导致视觉与语义域中出现非自然的重复关联性。为利用此特征，我们提出ATSS检测方法——一种通过三重相似性表征与跨模态注意力融合机制实现的多模态检测框架。该方法通过逐帧描述重构语义轨迹，构建视觉、文本及跨模态相似度矩阵，共同量化固有的时序异常。这些矩阵由专用Transformer编码器处理，并通过双向跨模态注意力融合模块进行整合，以有效建模模态内与模态间动态关系。在GenVideo、EvalCrafter、VideoPhy和VidProM四个大规模基准数据集上的实验表明，ATSS在AP、AUC和ACC指标上显著优于现有最优方法，并在多样化的视频生成模型中展现出卓越的泛化能力。ATSS的代码与模型将在https://github.com/hwang-cs-ime/ATSS开源。

TLDR: This paper proposes a method for detecting AI-generated videos by identifying anomalous temporal self-similarity, using a multimodal framework that analyzes visual, textual, and cross-modal similarity matrices.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种通过识别异常时间自相似性来检测AI生成视频的方法，使用一个分析视觉、文本和跨模态相似性矩阵的多模态框架。

Authors: Hang Wang, Chao Shen, Lei Zhang, Zhi-Qi Cheng

High-Fidelity Mural Restoration via a Unified Hybrid Mask-Aware Transformer

Abstract (English): Ancient murals are valuable cultural artifacts, but many have suffered severe degradation due to environmental exposure, material aging, and human activity. Restoring these artworks is challenging because it requires both reconstructing large missing structures and strictly preserving authentic, undamaged regions. This paper presents the Hybrid Mask-Aware Transformer (HMAT), a unified framework for high-fidelity mural restoration. HMAT integrates Mask-Aware Dynamic Filtering for robust local texture modeling with a Transformer bottleneck for long-range structural inference. To further address the diverse morphology of degradation, we introduce a mask-conditional style fusion module that dynamically guides the generative process. In addition, a Teacher-Forcing Decoder with hard-gated skip connections is designed to enforce fidelity in valid regions and focus reconstruction on missing areas. We evaluate HMAT on the DHMural dataset and a curated Nine-Colored Deer dataset under varying degradation levels. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves competitive performance compared to state-of-the-art approaches, while producing more structurally coherent and visually faithful restorations. These findings suggest that HMAT provides an effective solution for the digital restoration of cultural heritage murals.

摘要（中文）: 古代壁画是珍贵的文化遗产，但由于环境暴露、材料老化和人类活动等因素，许多壁画已遭受严重退化。修复这些艺术品具有挑战性，因为它既需要重建大面积缺失的结构，又必须严格保护完好无损的真实区域。本文提出了一种混合掩码感知变压器（HMAT）框架，用于实现高保真壁画修复的统一方案。HMAT集成了掩码感知动态滤波技术以增强局部纹理建模能力，同时结合变压器瓶颈结构进行长距离结构推断。为进一步应对退化的多样形态，我们引入了掩码条件风格融合模块，动态指导生成过程。此外，设计了带有硬门控跳跃连接的教师强制解码器，以强制保持有效区域的保真度，并将重建重点集中在缺失区域。 我们在DHMural数据集和精心构建的九色鹿数据集上，对不同退化程度进行了评估。实验结果表明，与现有先进方法相比，所提出的方法取得了具有竞争力的性能，同时能够生成结构更连贯、视觉更真实的修复结果。这些发现表明，HMAT为文化遗产壁画的数字化修复提供了有效的解决方案。

TLDR: A Transformer-based method for high-fidelity restoration of damaged cultural heritage murals, focusing on local texture modeling and long-range structure inference.

简要说明: 一种基于Transformer的壁画高保真修复方法，专注于局部纹理建模和长距离结构推断。

Authors: Jincheng Jiang, Qianhao Han, Chi Zhang, Zheng Zheng

Multimodal Structure Learning: Disentangling Shared and Specific Topology via Cross-Modal Graphical Lasso

Abstract (English): Learning interpretable multimodal representations inherently relies on uncovering the conditional dependencies between heterogeneous features. However, sparse graph estimation techniques, such as Graphical Lasso (GLasso), to visual-linguistic domains is severely bottlenecked by high-dimensional noise, modality misalignment, and the confounding of shared versus category-specific topologies. In this paper, we propose Cross-Modal Graphical Lasso (CM-GLasso) that overcomes these fundamental limitations. By coupling a novel text-visualization strategy with a unified vision-language encoder, we strictly align multimodal features into a shared latent space. We introduce a cross-attention distillation mechanism that condenses high-dimensional patches into explicit semantic nodes, naturally extracting spatial-aware cross-modal priors. Furthermore, we unify tailored GLasso estimation and Common-Specific Structure Learning (CSSL) into a joint objective optimized via the Alternating Direction Method of Multiplier (ADMM). This formulation guarantees the simultaneous disentanglement of invariant and class-specific precision matrices without multi-step error accumulation. Extensive experiments across eight benchmarks covering both natural and medical domains demonstrate that CM-GLasso establishes a new state-of-the-art in generative classification and dense semantic segmentation tasks.

摘要（中文）: 学习可解释的多模态表示本质上依赖于揭示异构特征之间的条件依赖关系。然而，在视觉-语言领域应用稀疏图估计技术（如图形套索）时，往往会受到高维噪声、模态不对齐以及共享与类别特定拓扑结构混淆等问题的严重制约。本文提出的跨模态图形套索方法从根本上克服了这些限制。通过将新颖的文本可视化策略与统一的视觉-语言编码器相结合，我们将多模态特征严格对齐到共享的潜在空间中。引入的跨注意力蒸馏机制将高维图像块压缩为显式语义节点，自然提取出具有空间感知的跨模态先验信息。此外，我们将定制的图形套索估计与共享-特定结构学习方法整合到基于交替方向乘子法优化的联合目标函数中，这种设计保证了不变性精度矩阵和类别特定精度矩阵的同步解耦，避免了多步误差累积。在涵盖自然领域和医学领域的八个基准测试上的大量实验表明，跨模态图形套索在生成式分类和密集语义分割任务中实现了最先进的性能。

TLDR: This paper proposes a novel method, Cross-Modal Graphical Lasso, for learning disentangled multimodal representations to uncover shared and category-specific conditional dependencies between visual and linguistic features, with applications in image classification and segmentation.

Authors: Fei Wang, Yutong Zhang, Xiong Wang

Task-Guided Multi-Annotation Triplet Learning for Remote Sensing Representations

Abstract (English): Prior multi-task triplet loss methods relied on static weights to balance supervision between various types of annotation. However, static weighting requires tuning and does not account for how tasks interact when shaping a shared representation. To address this, the proposed task-guided multi-annotation triplet loss removes this dependency by selecting triplets through a mutual-information criteria that identifies triplets most informative across tasks. This strategy modifies which samples influence the representation rather than adjusting loss magnitudes. Experiments on an aerial wildlife dataset compare the proposed task-guided selection against several triplet loss setups for shaping a representation in an effective multi-task manner. The results show improved classification and regression performance and demonstrate that task-aware triplet selection produces a more effective shared representation for downstream tasks.

摘要（中文）: 先前多任务三元组损失方法依赖于静态权重来平衡不同类型标注之间的监督关系。然而，静态加权策略既需要人工调参，也无法在构建共享表征时兼顾任务间的相互作用。为解决这一问题，本文提出的任务导向多标注三元组损失通过互信息准则选择三元组样本，消除了对静态权重的依赖。该准则能够识别出对跨任务信息贡献最大的三元组样本，从而改变影响表征构建的样本选择方式，而非简单地调整损失权重。通过航空野生动物数据集上的实验，我们将所提出的任务导向选择策略与多种三元组损失设置进行比较，以评估其在有效多任务表征构建中的性能。实验结果表明，该方法在分类和回归任务中均取得了更优的性能，证明任务感知的三元组选择能够为下游任务生成更有效的共享表征。

TLDR: This paper proposes a task-guided multi-annotation triplet loss that uses a mutual-information criterion for triplet selection to learn a shared representation, showing improved performance on classification and regression tasks for an aerial wildlife dataset.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种任务引导的多标注三元组损失方法，通过互信息准则进行三元组选择来学习共享表示，在航空野生动物数据集上展示了分类和回归任务的性能提升。

Authors: Meilun Zhou, Alina Zare

SPARK-IL: Spectral Retrieval-Augmented RAG for Knowledge-driven Deepfake Detection via Incremental Learning

Abstract (English): Detecting AI-generated images remains a significant challenge because detectors trained on specific generators often fail to generalize to unseen models; however, while pixel-level artifacts vary across models, frequency-domain signatures exhibit greater consistency, providing a promising foundation for cross-generator detection. To address this, we propose SPARK-IL, a retrieval-augmented framework that combines dual-path spectral analysis with incremental learning by utilizing a partially frozen ViT-L/14 encoder for semantic representations alongside a parallel path for raw RGB pixel embeddings. Both paths undergo multi-band Fourier decomposition into four frequency bands, which are individually processed by Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN) with mixture-of-experts for band-specific transformations before the resulting spectral embeddings are fused via cross-attention with residual connections. During inference, this fused embedding retrieves the $k$ nearest labeled signatures from a Milvus database using cosine similarity to facilitate predictions via majority voting, while an incremental learning strategy expands the database and employs elastic weight consolidation to preserve previously learned transformations. Evaluated on the UniversalFakeDetect benchmark across 19 generative models -- including GANs, face-swapping, and diffusion methods -- SPARK-IL achieves a 94.6\% mean accuracy, with the code to be publicly released at https://github.com/HessenUPHF/SPARK-IL.

摘要（中文）: 摘要： 检测人工智能生成的图像仍是一项重大挑战，因为基于特定生成器训练出的检测器往往无法泛化到未见过的模型；然而，尽管不同模型在像素层面的伪影存在差异，频域特征却表现出更高的一致性，这为跨生成器检测提供了可行基础。为此，我们提出SPARK-IL框架，该框架结合了双路径频谱分析与增量学习机制：通过部分冻结的ViT-L/14编码器提取语义表示，同时构建并行路径处理原始RGB像素嵌入。两条路径均经多频带傅里叶分解为四个频段，各频段由采用专家混合机制的Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）进行独立变换，最终通过残差连接与交叉注意力机制融合频谱嵌入。在推理阶段，融合后的嵌入通过余弦相似度从Milvus数据库中检索k个最接近的标记特征，并采用多数投票机制进行预测；同时，增量学习策略将动态扩展数据库，并运用弹性权重固化技术保留已学习的变换特征。在涵盖19种生成模型（包括GAN、人脸替换及扩散模型）的UniversalFakeDetect基准测试中，SPARK-IL实现了94.6%的平均准确率，相关代码将在https://github.com/HessenUPHF/SPARK-IL公开。

TLDR: This paper proposes SPARK-IL, a framework for detecting AI-generated images using a dual-path spectral analysis and retrieval-augmented incremental learning, achieving high accuracy on a cross-generator detection benchmark.

简要说明: 本文提出了SPARK-IL，一种基于双路径频谱分析和检索增强增量学习的框架，用于检测AI生成图像，在跨生成器检测基准上取得了高准确率。

Authors: Hessen Bougueffa Eutamene, Abdellah Zakaria Sellam, Abdelmalik Taleb-Ahmed, Abdenour Hadid

Which Leakage Types Matter?

Abstract (English): Twenty-eight within-subject counterfactual experiments across 2,047 tabular datasets, plus a boundary experiment on 129 temporal datasets, measuring the severity of four data leakage classes in machine learning. Class I (estimation - fitting scalers on full data) is negligible: all nine conditions produce $|Δ\text{AUC}| \leq 0.005$. Class II (selection - peeking, seed cherry-picking) is substantial: ~90% of the measured effect is noise exploitation that inflates reported scores. Class III (memorization) scales with model capacity: d_z = 0.37 (Naive Bayes) to 1.11 (Decision Tree). Class IV (boundary) is invisible under random CV. The textbook emphasis is inverted: normalization leakage matters least; selection leakage at practical dataset sizes matters most.

摘要（中文）: 在2,047个表格数据集上进行了28项受试者内反事实实验，并结合129个时间数据集的边界实验，用以测量机器学习中四类数据泄漏问题的严重程度。第一类泄漏（估计环节——在全数据上拟合缩放器）可忽略不计：所有九种实验条件下的曲线下面积绝对值变化均不超过0.005。第二类泄漏（选择环节——数据窥探、种子择优）影响显著：约90%的测量效应源于噪声利用，导致报告分数虚高。第三类泄漏（记忆效应）随模型容量递增：标准化效应量从朴素贝叶斯算法的0.37升至决策树算法的1.11。第四类泄漏（边界效应）在随机交叉验证中不可见。传统教材的侧重点需要反转：归一化泄漏影响最小，而在实际数据集规模下选择泄漏问题最为关键。

TLDR: Empirical study quantifying the severity of different types of data leakage in machine learning, finding that common preprocessing practices have negligible impact while selection-based leaks severely inflate reported performance.

简要说明: 一项量化机器学习中不同类型数据泄漏严重性的实证研究，发现常见的预处理实践影响可忽略不计，而基于选择的数据泄漏会严重夸大报告性能。

Authors: Simon Roth

Non-Equilibrium Stochastic Dynamics as a Unified Framework for Insight and Repetitive Learning: A Kramers Escape Approach to Continual Learning

Abstract (English): Continual learning in artificial neural networks is fundamentally limited by the stability--plasticity dilemma: systems that retain prior knowledge tend to resist acquiring new knowledge, and vice versa. Existing approaches, most notably elastic weight consolidation~(EWC), address this empirically without a physical account of why plasticity eventually collapses as tasks accumulate. Separately, the distinction between sudden insight and gradual skill acquisition through repetitive practice has lacked a unified theoretical description. Here, we show that both problems admit a common resolution within non-equilibrium statistical physics. We model the state of a learning system as a particle evolving under Langevin dynamics on a double-well energy landscape, with the noise amplitude governed by a time-dependent effective temperature $T(t)$. The probability density obeys a Fokker--Planck equation, and transitions between metastable states are governed by the Kramers escape rate $k = (ω_0ω_b/2π)\,e^{-ΔE/T}$. We make two contributions. First, we identify the EWC penalty term as an energy barrier whose height grows linearly with the number of accumulated tasks, yielding an exponential collapse of the transition rate predicted analytically and confirmed numerically. Second, we show that insight and repetitive learning correspond to two qualitatively distinct temperature protocols within the same Fokker--Planck equation: insight events produce transient spikes in $T(t)$ that drive rapid barrier crossing, whereas repetitive practice operates at a modestly elevated but fixed temperature, achieving transitions through sustained stochastic diffusion. These results establish a physically grounded framework for understanding plasticity and its failure in continual learning systems, and suggest principled design criteria for adaptive noise schedules in artificial intelligence.

摘要（中文）: 人工神经网络中的持续学习从根本上受到稳定性-可塑性困境的限制：保留先前知识的系统倾向于抗拒获取新知识，反之亦然。现有方法（最显著的是弹性权重固化EWC）通过经验性方式解决这一问题，但并未从物理层面解释为何随着任务积累可塑性最终会崩溃。与此同时，顿悟式学习与通过重复练习实现的渐进式技能习得之间的差异，也缺乏统一的理论描述。本研究证明这两个问题可在非平衡态统计物理框架内获得共同解决方案。我们将学习系统状态建模为在双势阱能量景观上遵循朗之万动力学演化的粒子，其噪声振幅由随时间变化的等效温度$T(t)$控制。概率密度服从福克-普朗克方程，亚稳态之间的跃迁由克拉默斯逃逸率$k = (ω_0ω_b/2π)\,e^{-ΔE/T}$决定。我们做出两项贡献：首先，我们将EWC惩罚项识别为能量势垒，其高度随累积任务数量线性增长，由此解析预测并数值验证了跃迁率的指数级衰减。其次，我们证明顿悟与重复学习对应于同一福克-普朗克方程中两种性质不同的温度调控机制：顿悟事件通过$T(t)$的瞬态尖峰驱动快速跨越势垒，而重复练习则在适度升高但恒定的温度下运作，通过持续随机扩散实现状态跃迁。这些结果建立了理解持续学习系统中可塑性及其失效的物理基础框架，并为人工智能中自适应噪声调节机制提供了原则性设计准则。

TLDR: The paper proposes a theoretical physics framework, using non-equilibrium stochastic dynamics and the Kramers escape rate, to model the stability-plasticity dilemma in continual learning and to explain insight vs. repetitive practice as distinct temperature protocols.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种理论物理学框架，利用非平衡随机动力学和克莱默斯逃逸率来模拟持续学习中的稳定性-可塑性困境，并将顿悟与重复性练习解释为两种不同的温度协议。

Authors: Gunn Kim

Primal-Dual Methods for Nonsmooth Nonconvex Optimization with Orthogonality Constraints

Abstract (English): Recent advancements in data science have significantly elevated the importance of orthogonally constrained optimization problems. The Riemannian approach has become a popular technique for addressing these problems due to the advantageous computational and analytical properties of the Stiefel manifold. Nonetheless, the interplay of nonsmoothness alongside orthogonality constraints introduces substantial challenges to current Riemannian methods, including scalability, parallelizability, complicated subproblems, and cumulative numerical errors that threaten feasibility. In this paper, we take a retraction-free primal-dual approach and propose a linearized smoothing augmented Lagrangian method specifically designed for nonsmooth and nonconvex optimization with orthogonality constraints. Our proposed method is single-loop and free of subproblem solving. We establish its iteration complexity of $O(ε^{-3})$ for finding $ε$-KKT points, matching the best-known results in the Riemannian optimization literature. Additionally, by invoking the standard Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) property, we demonstrate asymptotic sequential convergence of the proposed algorithm. Numerical experiments on both smooth and nonsmooth orthogonal constrained problems demonstrate the superior computational efficiency and scalability of the proposed method compared with state-of-the-art algorithms.

摘要（中文）: 近期数据科学领域的进展显著提升了正交约束优化问题的重要性。借助Stiefel流形优越的计算与分析特性，黎曼优化方法已成为处理此类问题的常用技术。然而，非光滑性与正交约束的交织为现有黎曼方法带来了严峻挑战，包括可扩展性、可并行性、复杂子问题求解以及危及可行性的累积数值误差。本文采用无需回撤映射的原对偶方法，提出了一种专为具有正交约束的非光滑非凸优化问题设计的线性化平滑增广拉格朗日算法。该方法采用单循环结构，无需子问题求解过程。我们证明了该方法在寻找$ε$-KKT点时的迭代复杂度为$O(ε^{-3})$，与黎曼优化文献中已知的最优结果相匹配。此外，通过引入标准的Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz（KL）性质，我们证明了所提算法具有渐近序列收敛性。在光滑与非光滑正交约束问题上的数值实验表明，相较于当前最先进算法，本方法具有更优越的计算效率与可扩展性。

TLDR: A novel primal-dual method for nonsmooth, nonconvex optimization with orthogonality constraints, offering single-loop efficiency and proven iteration complexity, though no direct connection to robotics is mentioned.

简要说明: 提出了一种用于解决带正交约束的非光滑、非凸优化问题的新原对偶方法，具有单循环效率和可证明的迭代复杂度，但未提及与机器人学的直接联系。

Authors: Linglingzhi Zhu, Wentao Ding, Shangyuan Liu, Anthony Man-Cho So

Measuring Robustness of Speech Recognition from MEG Signals Under Distribution Shift

Abstract (English): This study investigates robust speech-related decoding from non-invasive MEG signals using the LibriBrain phoneme-classification benchmark from the 2025 PNPL competition. We compare residual convolutional neural networks (CNNs), an STFT-based CNN, and a CNN--Transformer hybrid, while also examining the effects of group averaging, label balancing, repeated grouping, normalization strategies, and data augmentation. Across our in-house implementations, preprocessing and data-configuration choices matter more than additional architectural complexity, among which instance normalization emerges as the most influential modification for generalization. The strongest of our own models, a CNN with group averaging, label balancing, repeated grouping, and instance normalization, achieves 60.95% F1-macro on the test split, compared with 39.53% for the plain CNN baseline. However, most of our models, without instance normalization, show substantial validation-to-test degradation, indicating that distribution shift induced by different normalization statistics is a major obstacle to generalization in our experiments. By contrast, MEGConformer maintains 64.09% F1-macro on both validation and test, and saliency-map analysis is qualitatively consistent with this contrast: weaker models exhibit more concentrated or repetitive phoneme-sensitive patterns across splits, whereas MEGConformer appears more distributed. Overall, the results suggest that improving the reliability of non-invasive phoneme decoding will likely require better handling of normalization-related distribution shift while also addressing the challenge of single-trial decoding.

摘要（中文）: 本研究基于2025年PNPL竞赛的LibriBrain音素分类基准，探究了从非侵入式脑磁图信号中实现鲁棒语音相关解码的方法。我们比较了残差卷积神经网络、基于短时傅里叶变换的卷积神经网络以及卷积神经网络-Transformer混合模型，同时考察了组平均、标签平衡、重复分组、归一化策略及数据增强的影响。在我们自主实现的模型中，预处理与数据配置的选择比增加架构复杂度更为关键，其中实例归一化成为提升泛化能力最有效的改进方法。我们最优的模型（采用组平均、标签平衡、重复分组和实例归一化的卷积神经网络）在测试集上取得了60.95%的宏观F1分数，而基础卷积神经网络基准仅为39.53%。然而，大多数未经实例归一化处理的模型在验证集到测试集上均出现显著性能下降，表明由不同归一化统计量引起的分布偏移是本实验中泛化的主要障碍。相比之下，MEGConformer模型在验证集和测试集上均保持64.09%的宏观F1分数，显著性图谱分析也与此形成定性对应：较弱模型在不同数据划分中表现出更集中或重复的音素敏感模式，而MEGConformer的敏感模式分布更为广泛。总体而言，研究结果表明提升非侵入式音素解码的可靠性需要更好地处理归一化相关的分布偏移，同时应对单试次解码的挑战。

TLDR: This paper investigates methods to improve the robustness of phoneme classification from MEG signals under distribution shift, finding that preprocessing choices like instance normalization are more critical for generalization than model architecture.

简要说明: 本研究探讨了在分布偏移下提高MEG信号音素分类鲁棒性的方法，发现预处理选择（如实例归一化）比模型架构对泛化能力更为关键。

Authors: Sheng-You Chien, Bo-Yi Mao, Yi-Ning Chang, Po-Chih Kuo

Restless Bandits with Individual Penalty Constraints: A New Near-Optimal Index Policy and How to Learn It

Abstract (English): This paper investigates the Restless Multi-Armed Bandit (RMAB) framework under individual penalty constraints to address resource allocation challenges in dynamic wireless networked environments. Unlike conventional RMAB models, our model allows each user (arm) to have distinct and stringent performance constraints, such as energy limits, activation limits, or age of information minimums, enabling the capture of diverse objectives including fairness and efficiency. To find the optimal resource allocation policy, we propose a new Penalty-Optimal Whittle (POW) index policy. The POW index of an user only depends on the user's transition kernel and penalty constraints, and remains invariable to system-wide features such as the number of users present and the amount of resource available. This makes it computationally tractable to calculate the POW Indices offline without any need for online adaptation. Moreover, we theoretically prove that the POW index policy is asymptotically optimal while satisfying all individual penalty constraints. We also introduce a deep reinforcement learning algorithm to efficiently learn the POW index on the fly. Simulation results across various applications and system configurations further demonstrate that the POW index policy not only has near-optimal performance but also significantly outperforms other existing policies.

摘要（中文）: 本文研究了具有个体惩罚约束的"不安定多臂老虎机"框架，以解决动态无线网络环境中的资源分配难题。与传统RMAB模型不同，我们的模型允许每个用户（臂）具有独立且严格的性能约束，如能量限制、激活限制或信息年龄阈值，从而能够兼顾公平性、效率等多重目标。为寻求最优资源分配策略，我们提出了一种新型的"惩罚最优威特勒指数"策略。该策略中用户的POW指数仅取决于其状态转移核与惩罚约束，而与系统级特征（如用户总数和可用资源量）无关，这使得离线计算POW指数具有计算可行性，无需在线适配。理论上我们证明了POW指数策略在满足所有个体惩罚约束的同时具有渐近最优性。此外，我们还引入了一种深度强化学习算法来动态高效地学习POW指数。通过多应用场景和系统配置的仿真实验表明，POW指数策略不仅具备接近最优的性能表现，且显著优于其他现有策略。

TLDR: This paper introduces a new Penalty-Optimal Whittle (POW) index policy and a learning algorithm for Restless Multi-Armed Bandits with individual penalty constraints, focusing on resource allocation in wireless networks.

Authors: Nida Zamir, I-Hong Hou

Spectral Path Regression: Directional Chebyshev Harmonics for Interpretable Tabular Learning

Abstract (English): Classical approximation bases such as Chebyshev polynomials provide principled and interpretable representations, but their multivariate tensor-product constructions scale exponentially with dimension and impose axis-aligned structure that is poorly matched to real tabular data. We address this by replacing tensorised oscillations with directional harmonic modes of the form $\cos(\mathbf{m}^{\top}\arccos(\mathbf{x}))$, which organise multivariate structure by direction in angular space rather than by coordinate index. This representation yields a discrete spectral regression model in which complexity is controlled by selecting a small number of structured frequency vectors (spectral paths), and training reduces to a single closed-form ridge solve with no iterative optimisation. Experiments on standard continuous-feature tabular regression benchmarks show that the resulting models achieve accuracy competitive with strong nonlinear baselines while remaining compact, computationally efficient, and explicitly interpretable through analytic expressions of learned feature interactions.

摘要（中文）: 经典逼近基如切比雪夫多项式提供了原理性强且可解释的表示方法，但其多元张量积结构会随着维度增加呈指数级扩展，并强加了与真实表格数据匹配度较差的轴对齐结构。我们通过用$\cos(\mathbf{m}^{\top}\arccos(\mathbf{x}))$形式的方向谐波模态替代张量化振荡来解决这一问题，该模态在角空间中按方向而非坐标索引来组织多元结构。这种表示产生了一个离散谱回归模型，其中通过选择少量结构化频率向量（谱路径）来控制复杂度，训练过程简化为无需迭代优化的单次封闭形式岭回归求解。在标准连续特征表格回归基准测试上的实验表明，所得模型在保持紧凑性、计算效率及通过解析表达式实现学习特征交互显式可解释性的同时，达到了与强非线性基准相竞争的精度水平。

TLDR: The paper introduces Spectral Path Regression, a method using directional Chebyshev harmonics for interpretable, efficient tabular learning, achieving competitive accuracy on standard regression benchmarks.

简要说明: 本文提出谱路径回归方法，利用方向性切比雪夫谐波进行可解释、高效的表格学习，在标准回归基准测试中取得了有竞争力的准确率。

Authors: Milo Coombs

ArrowFlow: Hierarchical Machine Learning in the Space of Permutations

Abstract (English): We introduce ArrowFlow, a machine learning architecture that operates entirely in the space of permutations. Its computational units are ranking filters, learned orderings that compare inputs via Spearman's footrule distance and update through permutation-matrix accumulation, a non-gradient rule rooted in displacement evidence. Layers compose hierarchically: each layer's output ranking becomes the next layer's input, enabling deep ordinal representation learning without any floating-point parameters in the core computation. We connect the architecture to Arrow's impossibility theorem, showing that violations of social-choice fairness axioms (context dependence, specialization, symmetry breaking) serve as inductive biases for nonlinearity, sparsity, and stability. Experiments span UCI tabular benchmarks, MNIST, gene expression cancer classification (TCGA), and preference data, all against GridSearchCV-tuned baselines. ArrowFlow beats all baselines on Iris (2.7% vs. 3.3%) and is competitive on most UCI datasets. A single parameter, polynomial degree, acts as a master switch: degree 1 yields noise robustness (8-28% less degradation), privacy preservation (+0.5pp cost), and missing-feature resilience; higher degrees trade these for improved clean accuracy. ArrowFlow is not designed to surpass gradient-based methods. It is an existence proof that competitive classification is possible in a fundamentally different computational paradigm, one that elevates ordinal structure to a first-class citizen, with natural alignment to integer-only and neuromorphic hardware.

摘要（中文）: 本文提出ArrowFlow——一种完全在排列空间内运行的机器学习架构。其计算单元为排序过滤器，即通过斯皮尔曼步程距离比较输入的学习排序机制，并采用基于置换证据的非梯度规则（置换矩阵累积法）进行更新。该架构采用层级组合结构：每一层的输出排序直接作为下一层的输入，实现了无需核心计算中包含任何浮点参数的深度序数表示学习。 我们将该架构与阿罗不可能性定理建立联系，证明违反社会选择公平公理（情境依赖性、专业化、对称性破缺）的现象可转化为非线性、稀疏性与稳定性的归纳偏置。 实验涵盖UCI表格基准测试、MNIST手写数字识别、基因表达癌症分类（TCGA）以及偏好数据，所有实验均采用网格搜索交叉验证调参的基线模型进行对比。ArrowFlow在Iris数据集上以2.7%错误率优于所有基线模型（基线为3.3%），在多数UCI数据集上表现具有竞争力。其单一参数——多项式次数——发挥总控开关作用：一次项配置可实现噪声鲁棒性（性能衰减降低8-28%）、隐私保护（成本增加0.5个百分点）与缺失特征适应性；更高次项配置则会以牺牲这些特性为代价换取更高清洁数据准确率。 ArrowFlow并非旨在超越基于梯度的方法，而是作为一项存在性证明：在将序数结构提升为一等公民、天然适配纯整数与神经形态硬件的全新计算范式下，完全可能实现具有竞争力的分类性能。

TLDR: Introduces ArrowFlow, a novel ML architecture operating solely in the permutation space using ranking filters, demonstrating competitive performance on tabular data and image classification, and presenting an alternative computational paradigm.

简要说明: 介绍了ArrowFlow，一种在排列空间中运行的ML架构，使用排序过滤器，在表格数据和图像分类上展示了竞争力，并提出了一种替代计算范式。

Authors: Ozgur Yilmaz

Unmasking Hallucinations: A Causal Graph-Attention Perspective on Factual Reliability in Large Language Models

Abstract (English): This paper primarily focuses on the hallucinations caused due to AI language models(LLMs).LLMs have shown extraordinary Language understanding and generation capabilities .Still it has major a disadvantage hallucinations which give outputs which are factually incorrect ,misleading or unsupported by input data . These hallucinations cause serious problems in scenarios like medical diagnosis or legal reasoning.Through this work,we propose causal graph attention network (GCAN) framework that reduces hallucinations through interpretation of internal attention flow within a transformer architecture with the help of constructing token level graphs that combine self attention weights and gradient based influence scores.our method quantifies each tokens factual dependency using a new metric called the Causal Contribution Score (CCS). We further introduce a fact-anchored graph reweighting layer that dynamically reduces the influence of hallucination prone nodes during generation. Experiments on standard benchmarks such as TruthfulQA and HotpotQA show a 27.8 percent reduction in hallucination rate and 16.4 percent improvement in factual accuracy over baseline retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) models. This work contributes to the interpretability,robustness, and factual reliability of future LLM architectures.

摘要（中文）: 本文主要研究人工智能语言模型（LLM）产生的幻觉现象。尽管LLM展现出卓越的语言理解与生成能力，但其仍存在一个重大缺陷——幻觉，即输出内容存在事实性错误、具有误导性或缺乏输入数据支持。此类幻觉在医疗诊断、法律推理等场景中可能引发严重问题。本研究提出一种因果图注意力网络（GCAN）框架，通过构建融合自注意力权重与梯度影响分数的词元级图，解释Transformer架构内部的注意力流动机制，从而降低幻觉产生。我们采用一种名为因果贡献分数（CCS）的新度量指标来量化各词元的事实依赖度，并进一步引入事实锚定图重加权层，在生成过程中动态抑制易产生幻觉的节点影响力。在TruthfulQA和HotpotQA等基准测试上的实验表明，该方法相比基线检索增强生成（RAG）模型将幻觉率降低了27.8%，事实准确性提升了16.4%。这项工作为未来LLM架构的可解释性、鲁棒性及事实可靠性提供了有益参考。

TLDR: This paper proposes a causal graph attention network (GCAN) framework to reduce hallucinations in Large Language Models (LLMs) by interpreting attention flow and using a new metric to quantify token-level factual dependency, showing significant improvements on standard benchmarks.

Authors: Sailesh kiran kurra, Shiek Ruksana, Vishal Borusu

Autoencoder-Based Parameter Estimation for Superposed Multi-Component Damped Sinusoidal Signals

Abstract (English): Damped sinusoidal oscillations are widely observed in many physical systems, and their analysis provides access to underlying physical properties. However, parameter estimation becomes difficult when the signal decays rapidly, multiple components are superposed, and observational noise is present. In this study, we develop an autoencoder-based method that uses the latent space to estimate the frequency, phase, decay time, and amplitude of each component in noisy multi-component damped sinusoidal signals. We investigate multi-component cases under Gaussian-distribution training and further examine the effect of the training-data distribution through comparisons between Gaussian and uniform training. The performance is evaluated through waveform reconstruction and parameter-estimation accuracy. We find that the proposed method can estimate the parameters with high accuracy even in challenging setups, such as those involving a subdominant component or nearly opposite-phase components, while remaining reasonably robust when the training distribution is less informative. This demonstrates its potential as a tool for analyzing short-duration, noisy signals.

摘要（中文）: 阻尼正弦振荡广泛存在于诸多物理系统中，对其进行分析有助于揭示底层物理特性。然而，当信号快速衰减、多分量叠加且存在观测噪声时，参数估计变得尤为困难。本研究提出一种基于自编码器的方法，利用潜在空间对含噪声多分量阻尼正弦信号中各分量的频率、相位、衰减时间和振幅进行估计。我们探究了高斯分布训练下的多分量情况，并通过对比高斯分布与均匀分布训练，进一步检验了训练数据分布的影响。通过波形重建和参数估计精度对方法性能进行评估。结果表明，即使在包含次主导分量或近似反相分量的挑战性场景中，该方法仍能实现高精度参数估计；当训练数据分布信息量较少时，该方法仍保持较好的鲁棒性。这证明了其作为短时含噪声信号分析工具的应用潜力。

TLDR: The paper introduces an autoencoder-based method for accurately estimating parameters of noisy, multi-component damped sinusoidal signals, investigating the impact of different training-data distributions.

Authors: Momoka Iida, Hayato Motohashi, Hirotaka Takahashi

ACES: Who Tests the Tests? Leave-One-Out AUC Consistency for Code Generation

Abstract (English): Selecting LLM-generated code candidates using LLM-generated tests is challenging because the tests themselves may be incorrect. Existing methods either treat all tests equally or rely on ad-hoc heuristics to filter unreliable tests. Yet determining test correctness requires knowing which codes are correct, creating a \emph{circular dependency}. Our key insight is that we need not determine test correctness at all: \emph{test votes should rank, not merely count}. What matters is not how many codes pass a test, but whether the test can \emph{distinguish} correct from incorrect code. We break the circular dependency via leave-one-out evaluation: hold out one test, rank codes by their aggregate scores on all remaining tests, and measure whether the held-out test's pass/fail pattern agrees with this ranking. We formalize this agreement as the leave-one-out AUC~(LOO-AUC) and prove that the expected LOO-AUC is proportional to each test's ability to separate correct code from incorrect code. Building on this, we propose \textbf{ACES}~(\textbf{A}UC \textbf{C}onsist\textbf{E}ncy \textbf{S}coring) with two complementary variants: ACES-C provides closed-form weights that provably approximate the oracle in expectation under a mild assumption on average test quality; ACES-O drops this assumption and iteratively optimizes a differentiable LOO-AUC objective. Both operate solely on the binary pass matrix with negligible overhead, and achieve state-of-the-art Pass@$k$ on multiple code generation benchmarks.

摘要（中文）: 使用LLM生成的测试来筛选LLM生成的代码候选面临挑战，因为测试本身可能不正确。现有方法要么平等对待所有测试，要么依赖临时启发式规则过滤不可靠测试。然而，判定测试正确性需要预先知道哪些代码是正确的，这形成了\emph{循环依赖关系}。我们的核心发现是：无需判定测试正确性——\emph{测试应当通过投票排序，而非简单计数}。关键不在于有多少代码通过测试，而在于测试能否\emph{区分}正确与错误代码。我们通过留一评估打破循环依赖：暂存单个测试，根据其余所有测试的聚合分数对代码排序，并检验暂存测试的通过/失败模式是否与排序一致。我们将这种一致性形式化为留一法AUC（LOO-AUC），并证明其期望值与每个测试区分正确/错误代码的能力成正比。基于此，我们提出\emph{ACES}（\textbf{A}UC \textbf{C}onsist\textbf{E}ncy \textbf{S}coring）方法，包含两个互补变体：ACES-C在平均测试质量满足温和假设时，提供可证明近似理想权重的闭式解；ACES-O摒弃该假设，通过迭代优化可微分的LOO-AUC目标函数。两种方法仅需二元通过矩阵即可运行，计算开销可忽略，并在多个代码生成基准测试中实现了最优的Pass@$k$指标。

TLDR: Proposes ACES, a method using leave-one-out AUC consistency to rank and weight LLM-generated tests for evaluating and selecting LLM-generated code, without requiring ground-truth test correctness.

简要说明: 提出了ACES方法，利用留一法AUC一致性来对LLM生成的测试进行排名和加权，用于评估和选择LLM生成的代码，无需依赖真实测试的正确性。

Authors: Hui Sun, Yun-Ji Zhang, Zheng Xie, Ren-Biao Liu, Yali Du, Xin-Ye Li, Ming Li

Align Your Structures: Generating Trajectories with Structure Pretraining for Molecular Dynamics

Abstract (English): Generating molecular dynamics (MD) trajectories using deep generative models has attracted increasing attention, yet remains inherently challenging due to the limited availability of MD data and the complexities involved in modeling high-dimensional MD distributions. To overcome these challenges, we propose a novel framework that leverages structure pretraining for MD trajectory generation. Specifically, we first train a diffusion-based structure generation model on a large-scale conformer dataset, on top of which we introduce an interpolator module trained on MD trajectory data, designed to enforce temporal consistency among generated structures. Our approach effectively harnesses abundant structural data to mitigate the scarcity of MD trajectory data and effectively decomposes the intricate MD modeling task into two manageable subproblems: structural generation and temporal alignment. We comprehensively evaluate our method on the QM9 and DRUGS small-molecule datasets across unconditional generation, forward simulation, and interpolation tasks, and further extend our framework and analysis to tetrapeptide and protein monomer systems. Experimental results confirm that our approach excels in generating chemically realistic MD trajectories, as evidenced by remarkable improvements of accuracy in geometric, dynamical, and energetic measurements.

摘要（中文）: 利用深度生成模型生成分子动力学轨迹已受到越来越多的关注，但由于分子动力学数据的可用性有限以及高维分子动力学分布建模的复杂性，这一任务仍面临内在挑战。为克服这些困难，我们提出一种新颖框架，该框架利用结构预训练来实现分子动力学轨迹的生成。具体而言，我们首先在大规模构象数据集上训练一个基于扩散的结构生成模型，在此基础上引入一个在分子动力学轨迹数据上训练的插值模块，该模块旨在增强生成结构间的时间一致性。我们的方法有效利用丰富的结构数据缓解了分子动力学轨迹数据的稀缺性问题，并将复杂的分子动力学建模任务分解为两个可处理的子问题：结构生成与时间对齐。我们在QM9和DRUGS小分子数据集上，对无条件生成、正向模拟和插值任务进行了全面评估，并将框架与分析进一步拓展至四肽和蛋白质单体系统。实验结果证实，我们的方法在生成化学意义上真实的分子动力学轨迹方面表现优异，几何、动力学及能量测量指标的显著提升有力地证明了这一点。

TLDR: This paper proposes a diffusion-based framework for generating molecular dynamics trajectories by first pretraining on structural data and then aligning structures temporally, applied to molecules and proteins.

Authors: Aniketh Iyengar, Jiaqi Han, Pengwei Sun, Mingjian Jiang, Jianwen Xie, Stefano Ermon

Improving Model Performance by Adapting the KGE Metric to Account for System Non-Stationarity

Abstract (English): Geoscientific systems tend to be characterized by pronounced temporal non-stationarity, arising from seasonal and climatic variability in hydrometeorological drivers, and from natural and anthropogenic changes to land use and cover. As has been pointed out, such variability renders "the assumption of statistical stationarity obsolete in water management", and requires us to "account for, rather than ignore, non-stationary trends" in the data. However, metrics used for model development are typically based on the implicit and unjustifiable assumption that the data generating process is time-stationary. Here, we introduce the JKGE_ss metric (adapted from KGE_ss) that detects and accounts for dynamical non-stationarity in the statistical properties of the data and thereby improves information extraction and model performance. Unlike NSE and KGE_ss, which use the long-term mean as a benchmark against which to evaluate model efficiency, JKGE_ss emphasizes reproduction of temporal variations in system storage. We tested the robustness of the new metric by training physical-conceptual and data-based catchment-scale models of varying complexity across a wide range of hydroclimatic conditions, from recent-precipitation-dominated to snow-dominated to strongly arid. In all cases, the result was improved reproduction of system temporal dynamics at all time scales, across wet to dry years, and over the full range of flow levels (especially recession periods). Since traditional metrics fail to adequately account for temporal shifts in system dynamics, potentially resulting in misleading assessments of model performance under changing conditions, we recommend the adoption of JKGE_ss for geoscientific model development.

摘要（中文）: 地球科学系统往往具有显著的时间非平稳性，这源于水文气象驱动因素中季节和气候的变异性，以及自然和人为因素导致的土地利用与覆被变化。正如已指出的那样，这种变异性使得“水管理中的统计平稳性假设已过时”，并要求我们在数据中“考虑而非忽略非平稳趋势”。然而，用于模型开发的指标通常基于一个隐含且不合理的假设，即数据生成过程是时间平稳的。 在此，我们提出JKGE_ss指标（由KGE_ss调整而来），该指标能够检测并处理数据统计特性中的动态非平稳性，从而提高信息提取和模型性能。与使用长期均值作为模型效率评估基准的NSE和KGE_ss不同，JKGE_ss强调对系统蓄水时间变化的复现能力。 我们通过训练物理概念性和基于数据的、不同复杂度的流域尺度模型，检验了新指标的鲁棒性，测试范围涵盖了从近期降水主导型、积雪主导型到极端干旱型的广泛水文气候条件。在所有案例中，新指标均能改善所有时间尺度上系统时间动态的复现效果，适应从湿润到干旱年份的各类情况，并覆盖全流量范围（尤其是退水期）。 由于传统指标无法充分考量系统动态的时间偏移，可能导致在变化条件下对模型性能的误导性评估，我们建议在地球科学模型开发中采用JKGE_ss指标。

TLDR: This paper proposes a new metric, JKGE_ss, for evaluating geoscientific models by accounting for temporal non-stationarity in hydrological data, which improves the reproduction of system dynamics across varying conditions.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种新的度量指标JKGE_ss，用于评估地球科学模型，通过考虑水文数据中的时间非平稳性，改进了在不同条件下系统动态的复现。

Authors: M Jawad, HV Gupta, YH Wang, MA Farmani, A Behrangi, GY Niu

Improving ML Attacks on LWE with Data Repetition and Stepwise Regression

Abstract (English): The Learning with Errors (LWE) problem is a hard math problem in lattice-based cryptography. In the simplest case of binary secrets, it is the subset sum problem, with error. Effective ML attacks on LWE were demonstrated in the case of binary, ternary, and small secrets, succeeding on fairly sparse secrets. The ML attacks recover secrets with up to 3 active bits in the "cruel region" (Nolte et al., 2024) on samples pre-processed with BKZ. We show that using larger training sets and repeated examples enables recovery of denser secrets. Empirically, we observe a power-law relationship between model-based attempts to recover the secrets, dataset size, and repeated examples. We introduce a stepwise regression technique to recover the "cool bits" of the secret.

摘要（中文）: 带误差学习问题是一种基于格密码的困难数学问题。在最简单的二进制密钥情形下，它实质上是带误差的子集和问题。针对二进制、三进制及小数值密钥的机器学习攻击已被证明有效，能够在相当稀疏的密钥情况下成功实施。通过BKZ预处理的样本，在"残酷区域"内，机器学习攻击可恢复至多含有3个活跃比特的密钥（Nolte等人，2024）。本研究表明，通过使用更大的训练集和重复样本，能够恢复更密集的密钥。实验观察到，基于模型的密钥恢复尝试次数、数据集规模与重复样本数量之间存在幂律关系。我们提出了一种逐步回归技术，用以恢复密钥中的"冷却比特"。

TLDR: This paper introduces a machine learning technique to attack the Learning with Errors (LWE) lattice-based cryptography problem by using larger datasets and data repetition, improving secret recovery for denser secrets.

简要说明: 本文提出一种机器学习技术，通过使用更大数据集和数据重复来攻击基于格的LWE密码学问题，从而提高对更密集私钥的恢复能力。

Authors: Alberto Alfarano, Eshika Saxena, Emily Wenger, François Charton, Kristin Lauter

PhaseFlow4D: Physically Constrained 4D Beam Reconstruction via Feedback-Guided Latent Diffusion

Abstract (English): We address the problem of recovering a time-varying 4D distribution from a sparse sequence of 2D projections - analogous to novel-view synthesis from sparse cameras, but applied to the 4D transverse phase space density $ρ(x,p_x,y,p_y)$ of charged particle beams. Direct single shot measurement of this high-dimensional distribution is physically impossible in real particle accelerator systems; only limited 1D or 2D projections are accessible. We propose PhaseFlow4D, a feedback-guided latent diffusion model that reconstructs and tracks the full 4D phase space from incomplete 2D observations alone, with built-in hard physics constraints. Our core technical contribution is a 4D VAE whose decoder generates the full 4D phase space tensor, from which 2D projections are analytically computed and compared against 2D beam measurements. This projection-consistency constraint guarantees physical correctness by construction - not as a soft penalty, but as an architectural prior. An adaptive feedback loop then continuously tunes the conditioning vector of the latent diffusion model to track time-varying distributions online without retraining. We validate on multi-particle simulations of heavy-ion beams at the Facility for Rare Isotope Beams (FRIB), where full physics simulations require $\sim$6 hours on a 100-core HPC system. PhaseFlow4D achieves accurate 4D reconstructions 11000$\times$ faster while faithfully tracking distribution shifts under time-varying source conditions - demonstrating that principled generative reconstruction under incomplete observations transfers robustly beyond visual domains.

摘要（中文）: 本研究旨在解决从稀疏二维投影序列中重建时变四维分布的问题——类似于稀疏相机视角下的新视角合成，但应用于带电粒子束的四维横向相空间密度$ρ(x,p_x,y,p_y)$。在真实粒子加速器系统中，直接单次测量该高维分布具有物理上的不可能性；仅能获取有限的一维或二维投影数据。我们提出了PhaseFlow4D模型，这是一种基于反馈引导的潜在扩散模型，仅通过不完整的二维观测即可重建并跟踪完整四维相空间，同时内置严格的物理约束。我们的核心技术贡献是构建了一个四维变分自编码器（VAE），其解码器生成完整四维相空间张量，并基于此解析计算出二维投影，再与二维束流测量数据进行对比。这种投影一致性约束通过架构先验（而非软惩罚项）保证了物理正确性。随后，自适应反馈回路持续调整潜在扩散模型的条件向量，实现在线跟踪时变分布而无需重新训练。我们在稀有同位素束流装置（FRIB）的重离子束多粒子模拟中验证了该模型：完整物理模拟在100核高性能计算系统上需耗时约6小时，而PhaseFlow4D在实现精确四维重建的同时，速度提升11000倍，并能在时变源条件下准确跟踪分布变化。这表明基于不完整观测的生成式重建方法具有鲁棒性，其适用范围可有效拓展至视觉领域之外。

TLDR: This paper introduces PhaseFlow4D, a feedback-guided latent diffusion model for reconstructing and tracking the 4D phase space density of charged particle beams from sparse 2D projections, with a focus on particle accelerator physics.

简要说明: 本文提出了PhaseFlow4D，一种反馈引导的隐扩散模型，用于从稀疏的2D投影中重建和跟踪带电粒子束的4D相空间密度，重点应用于粒子加速器物理。
