Authors: Alexander Scheinker, Alexander Plastun, Peter Ostroumov

SODA: Semi On-Policy Black-Box Distillation for Large Language Models

Abstract (English): Black-box knowledge distillation for large language models presents a strict trade-off. Simple off-policy methods (e.g., sequence-level knowledge distillation) struggle to correct the student's inherent errors. Fully on-policy methods (e.g., Generative Adversarial Distillation) solve this via adversarial training but introduce well-known training instability and crippling computational overhead. To address this dilemma, we propose SODA (Semi On-policy Distillation with Alignment), a highly efficient alternative motivated by the inherent capability gap between frontier teachers and much smaller base models. Because a compact student model's natural, zero-shot responses are almost strictly inferior to the powerful teacher's targets, we can construct a highly effective contrastive signal simply by pairing the teacher's optimal response with a one-time static snapshot of the student's outputs. This demonstrates that exposing the small student to its own static inferior behaviors is sufficient for high-quality distribution alignment, eliminating the need for costly dynamic rollouts and fragile adversarial balancing. Extensive evaluations across four compact Qwen2.5 and Llama-3 models validate this semi on-policy paradigm. SODA matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art methods on 15 out of 16 benchmark results. More importantly, it achieves this superior distillation quality while training 10 times faster, consuming 27% less peak GPU memory, and completely eliminating adversarial instability.

摘要（中文）: 大型语言模型的黑盒知识蒸馏面临严格的权衡取舍。简单的离线策略方法（如序列级知识蒸馏）难以纠正学生模型固有的错误。完全在线策略方法（如生成对抗蒸馏）通过对抗训练解决了这一问题，但引入了众所周知的训练不稳定性和巨大的计算开销。为应对这一困境，我们提出SODA（基于对齐的半在线策略蒸馏方法），该方法受前沿教师模型与更小型基础模型之间固有能力差距的启发，提供了一种高效替代方案。由于紧凑型学生模型在零样本条件下的自然响应几乎严格劣于强大教师模型的预期输出，我们仅需将教师模型的最优响应与学生模型静态输出的单次快照配对，即可构建高效的对比信号。这表明，让小型学生模型接触其自身静态的次优行为已足以实现高质量分布对齐，从而无需昂贵的动态展开过程和脆弱的对抗平衡机制。通过对四个紧凑型Qwen2.5和Llama-3模型的广泛评估，验证了这种半在线策略范式的有效性。在16项基准测试结果中，SODA在15项上达到或超越了现有最优方法。更重要的是，该方法在实现更优蒸馏质量的同时，训练速度提升10倍，峰值GPU内存消耗降低27%，并完全消除了对抗训练的不稳定性。

TLDR: The paper proposes SODA, a semi-on-policy black-box knowledge distillation method for efficiently training smaller LLMs using a contrastive signal from a more powerful teacher model, claiming superior performance and 10x faster training.

简要说明: 本文提出了SODA，一种用于高效训练较小大语言模型的半在线黑盒知识蒸馏方法，通过使用更强大教师模型的对比信号，声称实现了更优的性能和10倍的训练速度。

Authors: Xiwen Chen, Jingjing Wang, Wenhui Zhu, Peijie Qiu, Xuanzhao Dong, Hejian Sang, Zhipeng Wang, Alborz Geramifard, Feng Luo

Where to Steer: Input-Dependent Layer Selection for Steering Improves LLM Alignment

Abstract (English): Steering vectors have emerged as a lightweight and effective approach for aligning large language models (LLMs) at inference time, enabling modulation over model behaviors by shifting LLM representations towards a target behavior. However, existing methods typically apply steering vectors at a globally fixed layer, implicitly assuming that the optimal intervention layer is invariant across inputs. We argue that this assumption is fundamentally limited, as representations relevant to a target behavior can be encoded at different layers depending on the input. Theoretically, we show that different inputs can require steering at different layers to achieve alignment with a desirable model behavior. We also provide empirical evidence that the optimal steering layer varies substantially across inputs in practice. Motivated by these observations, we introduce Where to Steer (W2S), a framework that adaptively selects the intervention layer conditioned on the input, by learning a mapping from input embeddings to optimal steering layers. Across multiple LLMs and alignment behaviors, W2S consistently outperforms fixed-layer baselines, with improvements in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings. Our findings highlight the importance of input-dependent control in LLM alignment and demonstrate that adaptive layer selection is a key design dimension missing in the current methodology of steering vectors.

摘要（中文）: 转向向量已成为一种在推理时对齐大型语言模型（LLM）的轻量级有效方法，它通过将LLM的表征向目标行为偏移来实现对模型行为的调节。然而，现有方法通常在一个全局固定的层上应用转向向量，这隐含地假设了最佳干预层在不同输入间是不变的。我们认为这一假设存在根本性局限，因为与目标行为相关的表征可能根据输入的不同编码在不同层中。从理论上，我们证明了不同输入为实现与理想模型行为的对齐，可能需要在不同层进行干预。我们还提供了实证证据，表明在实际应用中最佳转向层在不同输入间存在显著差异。基于这些观察，我们提出了“何处转向”（W2S）框架，该框架通过学习从输入嵌入到最佳转向层的映射，根据输入自适应地选择干预层。在多个LLM和对齐行为上的实验表明，W2S始终优于固定层基线方法，在分布内和分布外设置中均取得了提升。我们的研究结果突显了输入依赖性控制在LLM对齐中的重要性，并证明自适应层选择是当前转向向量方法中缺失的关键设计维度。

TLDR: The paper proposes a method for adaptively selecting the optimal layer to apply steering vectors in large language models for improved alignment, based on input embeddings.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种方法，根据输入嵌入自适应地选择应用转向向量的最佳层，以提高大型语言模型的对齐性能。

Authors: Soham Gadgil, Chris Lin, Su-In Lee

A Bayesian Information-Theoretic Approach to Data Attribution

Abstract (English): Training Data Attribution (TDA) seeks to trace model predictions back to influential training examples, enhancing interpretability and safety. We formulate TDA as a Bayesian information-theoretic problem: subsets are scored by the information loss they induce - the entropy increase at a query when removed. This criterion credits examples for resolving predictive uncertainty rather than label noise. To scale to modern networks, we approximate information loss using a Gaussian Process surrogate built from tangent features. We show this aligns with classical influence scores for single-example attribution while promoting diversity for subsets. For even larger-scale retrieval, we relax to an information-gain objective and add a variance correction for scalable attribution in vector databases. Experiments show competitive performance on counterfactual sensitivity, ground-truth retrieval and coreset selection, showing that our method scales to modern architectures while bridging principled measures with practice.

摘要（中文）: 训练数据归因（TDA）旨在将模型预测追溯到具有影响力的训练样本，以增强可解释性与安全性。我们将TDA构建为一个贝叶斯信息论问题：通过计算子集移除时在查询点引起的熵增——即信息损失——来评估子集的重要性。这一标准强调样本在消解预测不确定性方面的贡献，而非对标签噪声的修正。为适应现代网络规模，我们利用基于切向特征构建的高斯过程替代模型来近似信息损失。研究表明，该方法在单样本归因任务中与经典影响力评分一致，同时能在子集归因中促进多样性。针对更大规模的检索需求，我们放宽为信息增益目标，并引入方差校正以实现向量数据库中的可扩展归因。实验结果表明，本方法在反事实敏感性、真实数据检索与核心集选择任务中具有竞争力，能够在衔接理论度量与实践应用的同时，有效扩展到现代网络架构。

TLDR: This paper introduces a Bayesian information-theoretic framework for training data attribution, which attributes model predictions to influential training examples by scoring them based on the predictive uncertainty they resolve, and proposes scalable approximations for modern neural networks.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯信息论框架用于训练数据归因，通过基于样本解决预测不确定性的能力对其进行评分，将模型预测归因于有影响力的训练数据，并提出了适用于现代神经网络的、可扩展的近似方法。

Authors: Dharmesh Tailor, Nicolò Felicioni, Kamil Ciosek

Understanding When Poisson Log-Normal Models Outperform Penalized Poisson Regression for Microbiome Count Data

Abstract (English): Multivariate count models are often justified by their ability to capture latent dependence, but researchers receive little guidance on when this added structure improves on simpler penalized marginal Poisson regression. We study this question using real microbiome data under a unified held-out evaluation framework. For count prediction, we compare PLN and GLMNet(Poisson) on 20 datasets spanning 32 to 18,270 samples and 24 to 257 taxa, using held-out Poisson deviance under leave-one-taxon-out prediction with 3-fold sample cross-validation rather than synthetic or in-sample criteria. For network inference, we compare PLNNetwork and GLMNet(Poisson) neighborhood selection on five publicly available datasets with experimentally validated microbial interaction truth. PLN outperforms GLMNet(Poisson) on most count-prediction datasets, with gains up to 38 percent. The primary predictor of the winner is the sample-to-taxon ratio, with mean absolute correlation as the strongest secondary signal and overdispersion as an additional predictor. PLNNetwork performs best on broad undirected interaction benchmarks, whereas GLMNet(Poisson) is better aligned with local or directional effects. Taken together, these results provide guidance for choosing between latent multivariate count models and penalized Poisson regression in biological count prediction and interaction recovery.

摘要（中文）: 多元计数模型常因其能捕捉潜在依赖关系而受青睐，但研究人员很少获得关于这种额外结构何时优于简单惩罚边际泊松回归的指导。我们在统一的留出评估框架下利用真实微生物组数据探讨此问题。针对计数预测，我们在20个数据集（样本量范围32至18,270，分类单元数24至257）上比较了PLN与GLMNet（泊松）模型，采用留一分类单元预测结合三折样本交叉验证的留出泊松偏差作为评估标准，而非合成或样本内准则。针对网络推断，我们在五个具有实验验证的微生物相互作用真实数据集的公开数据集上比较了PLNNetwork与GLMNet（泊松）邻域选择方法。在大多数计数预测数据集中，PLN的表现优于GLMNet（泊松），提升幅度最高达38%。决定模型优劣的主要预测因素是样本与分类单元数量的比值，平均绝对相关性是最强的次要信号，而过离散度可作为额外预测指标。在广泛的非定向相互作用基准测试中，PLNNetwork表现最佳，而GLMNet（泊松）更适用于局部或定向效应分析。综合而言，这些结果为生物计数预测和相互作用恢复中潜在多元计数模型与惩罚泊松回归之间的选择提供了依据。

TLDR: This paper compares Poisson log-normal (PLN) models and penalized Poisson regression (GLMNet) for predicting microbiome count data and inferring microbial interaction networks, finding PLN generally better for count prediction and GLMNet better for some specific interaction types.

简要说明: 本研究比较了泊松对数正态（PLN）模型和惩罚性泊松回归（GLMNet）在预测微生物组计数数据和推断微生物相互作用网络方面的表现，发现PLN在计数预测上通常更优，而GLMNet在某些特定类型的相互作用推断上更好。

Authors: Daniel Agyapong, Julien Chiquet, Jane Marks, Toby Dylan Hocking

Collapse-Free Prototype Readout Layer for Transformer Encoders

Abstract (English): DDCL-Attention is a prototype-based readout layer for transformer encoders that replaces simple pooling methods, such as mean pooling or class tokens, with a learned compression mechanism. It uses a small set of global prototype vectors and assigns tokens to them through soft probabilistic matching, producing compact token summaries at linear complexity in sequence length. The method offers three main advantages. First, it avoids prototype collapse through an exact decomposition of the training loss into a reconstruction term and a diversity term, ensuring that prototypes remain distinct. Second, its joint training with the encoder is shown to be stable under a practical timescale condition, using Tikhonov's singular perturbation theory and explicit learning-rate constraints. Third, the same framework supports three uses: a final readout layer, a differentiable codebook extending VQ-VAE, and a hierarchical document compressor. Experiments on four datasets confirm the theoretical predictions: the loss decomposition holds exactly, prototype separation grows as expected when the stability condition is met, and the codebook reaches full utilization, outperforming standard hard vector quantization. An additional study on orbital debris classification shows that the method also applies beyond standard NLP and vision tasks, including scientific tabular data.

摘要（中文）: DDCL-Attention是一种基于原型的变压器编码器读出层，它通过学习压缩机制取代了传统的简单池化方法（如均值池化或类别标记）。该方法采用一组小规模的全局原型向量，并通过软概率匹配将标记分配给这些原型，从而以序列长度的线性复杂度生成紧凑的标记摘要。 该方法具有三个主要优势。首先，通过将训练损失精确分解为重构项与多样性项，有效避免了原型坍缩，确保原型保持差异性。其次，在时间尺度条件下，结合Tikhonov奇异摄动理论及显式学习率约束，该方法与编码器的联合训练被证明具有稳定性。第三，同一框架支持三种应用场景：作为最终读出层、作为扩展VQ-VAE的可微分码书，以及作为分层文档压缩器。 在四个数据集上的实验验证了理论预测：损失分解严格成立；在满足稳定性条件时原型分离度按预期增长；码书达到完全利用率，性能优于标准硬向量量化。此外，在轨道碎片分类任务上的研究表明，该方法同样适用于标准自然语言处理和视觉任务之外的科学表格数据领域。

TLDR: This paper introduces a prototype-based readout layer for transformers to improve sequence summarization, with theoretical guarantees against prototype collapse and experimental validation on NLP, vision, and tabular data tasks.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种基于原型的Transformer读取层，用于改善序列摘要，具有防止原型坍缩的理论保证，并在NLP、视觉和表格数据任务上进行了实验验证。

Authors: Giansalvo Cirrincione, Rahul Ranjeev Kumar

Explainability-Guided Adversarial Attacks on Transformer-Based Malware Detectors Using Control Flow Graphs

Abstract (English): Transformer-based malware detection systems operating on graph modalities such as control flow graphs (CFGs) achieve strong performance by modeling structural relationships in program behavior. However, their robustness to adversarial evasion attacks remains underexplored. This paper examines the vulnerability of a RoBERTa-based malware detector that linearizes CFGs into sequences of function calls, a design choice that enables transformer modeling but may introduce token-level sensitivities and ordering artifacts exploitable by adversaries. By evaluating evasion strategies within this graph-to-sequence framework, we provide insight into the practical robustness of transformer-based malware detectors beyond aggregate detection accuracy. This paper proposes a white-box adversarial evasion attack that leverages explainability mechanisms to identify and perturb most influential graph components. Using token- and word-level attributions derived from integrated gradients, the attack iteratively replaces positively attributed function calls with synthetic external imports, producing adversarial CFG representations without altering overall program structure. Experimental evaluation on small- and large-scale Windows Portable Executable (PE) datasets demonstrates that the proposed method can reliably induce misclassification, even against models trained to high accuracy. Our results highlight that explainability tools, while valuable for interpretability, can also expose critical attack surfaces in transformer-based malware detectors.

摘要（中文）: 基于Transformer的恶意软件检测系统在控制流图（CFG）等图结构模态上运行，通过建模程序行为的结构关系实现了强劲性能。然而，其对对抗性逃逸攻击的鲁棒性仍缺乏深入研究。本文探讨了一种基于RoBERTa的恶意软件检测器的脆弱性，该检测器将CFG线性化为函数调用序列——这种设计选择虽实现了Transformer建模，但可能引入令牌级敏感性和可被攻击者利用的序列人工痕迹。通过在图到序列框架内评估逃逸策略，本研究揭示了超越聚合检测精度的、基于Transformer的恶意软件检测器的实际鲁棒性。 本文提出了一种白盒对抗性逃逸攻击，该攻击利用可解释性机制识别并扰动最具影响力的图结构组件。通过融合梯度方法获取的令牌级和词级归因分析，该攻击迭代地将正向归因的函数调用替换为合成的外部导入，从而在不改变整体程序结构的前提下生成对抗性CFG表示。在小型和大型Windows可移植可执行文件（PE）数据集上的实验评估表明，即使针对高精度训练的模型，所提方法仍能可靠地诱导误分类。我们的研究结果强调，可解释性工具虽然有助于提升模型透明度，但也可能暴露基于Transformer的恶意软件检测器中的关键攻击面。

TLDR: This paper proposes an explainability-guided white-box adversarial attack to evade a transformer-based malware detector that uses control flow graphs, demonstrating that interpretability tools can expose vulnerabilities in such models.

简要说明: 本文提出一种利用可解释性方法指导的白盒对抗攻击，用于规避基于控制流图的Transformer恶意软件检测器，表明可解释性工具可能暴露此类模型的关键攻击面。

Authors: Andrew Wheeler, Kshitiz Aryal, Maanak Gupta

A Model of Understanding in Deep Learning Systems

Abstract (English): I propose a model of systematic understanding, suitable for machine learning systems. On this account, an agent understands a property of a target system when it contains an adequate internal model that tracks real regularities, is coupled to the target by stable bridge principles, and supports reliable prediction. I argue that contemporary deep learning systems often can and do achieve such understanding. However they generally fall short of the ideal of scientific understanding: the understanding is symbolically misaligned with the target system, not explicitly reductive, and only weakly unifying. I label this the Fractured Understanding Hypothesis.

摘要（中文）: 本文提出一种适用于机器学习系统的系统性理解模型。该模型认为，当智能体具备能够追踪真实规律性的充分内部模型、通过稳定的桥接原则与目标系统耦合、并能支持可靠预测时，即可理解目标系统的特定属性。我认为当代深度学习系统常常能够且已经实现这种理解。然而，它们通常未能达到科学理解的理想标准：其理解与目标系统存在符号表征错位，缺乏显式的还原性解释，且统一性较弱。我将此现象称为"碎片化理解假说"。

TLDR: This paper proposes a philosophical model of 'systematic understanding' for deep learning systems, arguing they achieve a form of understanding but fall short of scientific ideals.

简要说明: 本文为深度学习系统提出了一个‘系统性理解’的哲学模型，认为其能实现某种理解，但未达到科学理解的标准。

Authors: David Peter Wallis Freeborn

Uncertainty-Aware Test-Time Adaptation for Cross-Region Spatio-Temporal Fusion of Land Surface Temperature

Abstract (English): Deep learning models have shown great promise in diverse remote sensing applications. However, they often struggle to generalize across geographic regions unseen during training due to domain shifts. Domain shifts occur when data distributions differ between the training region and new target regions, due to variations in land cover, climate, and environmental conditions. Test-time adaptation (TTA) has emerged as a solution to such shifts, but existing methods are primarily designed for classification and are not directly applicable to regression tasks. In this work, we address the regression task of spatio-temporal fusion (STF) for land surface temperature estimation. We propose an uncertainty-aware TTA framework that updates only the fusion module of a pre-trained STF model, guided by epistemic uncertainty, land use and land cover consistency, and bias correction, without requiring source data or labeled target samples. Experiments on four target regions with diverse climates, namely Rome in Italy, Cairo in Egypt, Madrid in Spain, and Montpellier in France, show consistent improvements in RMSE and MAE for a pre-trained model in Orléans, France. The average gains are 24.2% and 27.9%, respectively, even with limited unlabeled target data and only 10 TTA epochs.

摘要（中文）: 深度学习模型在多种遥感应用中展现出巨大潜力。然而，由于领域偏移的存在，这些模型往往难以泛化到训练时未见过的地理区域。当训练区域与目标区域在土地覆盖、气候和环境条件等方面存在差异，导致数据分布不一致时，就会发生领域偏移。针对此类问题，测试时自适应方法应运而生，但现有方法主要针对分类任务设计，无法直接适用于回归任务。本研究聚焦于地表温度估算的时空融合回归任务，提出了一种不确定性感知的测试时自适应框架。该框架在不依赖源数据或目标区域标记样本的前提下，基于认知不确定性、土地利用与土地覆盖一致性以及偏差校正的指导，仅更新预训练时空融合模型中的融合模块。通过在意大利罗马、埃及开罗、西班牙马德里和法国蒙彼利尔四个不同气候的目标区域进行实验，结果显示：针对在法国奥尔良预训练的模型，即使仅使用少量无标记目标数据且仅进行10个测试时自适应周期，其均方根误差和平均绝对误差均获得持续改善，平均提升幅度分别达到24.2%和27.9%。

TLDR: The paper proposes an uncertainty-aware test-time adaptation method to improve the generalization of a pre-trained land surface temperature fusion model across different geographic regions, addressing domain shift in a remote sensing regression task.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种不确定性感知的测试时适应方法，用于改进预训练的地表温度融合模型在不同地理区域间的泛化能力，解决了遥感回归任务中的域偏移问题。

Authors: Sofiane Bouaziz, Adel Hafiane, Raphael Canals, Rachid Nedjai

NetSecBed: A Container-Native Testbed for Reproducible Cybersecurity Experimentation

Abstract (English): Cybersecurity research increasingly depends on reproducible evidence, such as traffic traces, logs, and labeled datasets, yet most public datasets remain static and offer limited support for controlled re-execution and traceability, especially in heterogeneous multi-protocol environments. This paper presents NetSecBed, a container-native, scenario-oriented testbed for reproducible generation of network traffic evidence and execution artifacts under controlled conditions, particularly suitable for IoT, IIoT, and pervasive multi-protocol environments. The framework integrates 60 attack scenarios, 9 target services, and benign traffic generators as single-purpose containers, enabling plug-and-play extensibility and traceability through declarative specifications. Its pipeline automates parametrized execution, packet capture, log collection, service probing, feature extraction, and dataset consolidation. The main contribution is a repeatable, auditable, and extensible framework for cybersecurity experimentation that reduces operational bias and supports continuous dataset generation.

摘要（中文）: 网络安全研究日益依赖可重现证据，如流量轨迹、日志和标注数据集，然而当前大多数公开数据集仍为静态数据，在受控复现与可追溯性方面支持有限，尤其面对异构多协议环境时更为突出。本文提出NetSecBed——一种面向容器化、场景导向的实验平台，可在受控条件下生成可复现的网络流量证据与执行痕迹，特别适用于物联网、工业物联网及泛在多协议环境。该框架集成了60种攻击场景、9类目标服务及良性流量生成器，均以单一功能容器形式实现，通过声明式配置实现即插即用扩展与全链路追溯。其自动化流程支持参数化执行、数据包捕获、日志收集、服务探测、特征提取与数据集整合。本研究的主要贡献在于构建了一个可重复、可审计、可扩展的网络安全实验框架，能够有效降低实验操作偏差，并为持续生成数据集提供支持。

Authors: Leonardo Bitzki, Diego Kreutz, Tiago Heinrich, Douglas Fideles, Leandro Bertholdo, Silvio Quincozes, Angelo Diniz

Fine-grained Analysis of Stability and Generalization for Stochastic Bilevel Optimization

Abstract (English): Stochastic bilevel optimization (SBO) has been integrated into many machine learning paradigms recently, including hyperparameter optimization, meta learning, and reinforcement learning. Along with the wide range of applications, there have been numerous studies on the computational behavior of SBO. However, the generalization guarantees of SBO methods are far less understood from the lens of statistical learning theory. In this paper, we provide a systematic generalization analysis of the first-order gradient-based bilevel optimization methods. Firstly, we establish the quantitative connections between the on-average argument stability and the generalization gap of SBO methods. Then, we derive the upper bounds of on-average argument stability for single-timescale stochastic gradient descent (SGD) and two-timescale SGD, where three settings (nonconvex-nonconvex (NC-NC), convex-convex (C-C), and strongly-convex-strongly-convex (SC-SC)) are considered respectively. Experimental analysis validates our theoretical findings. Compared with the previous algorithmic stability analysis, our results do not require reinitializing the inner-level parameters at each iteration and are applicable to more general objective functions.

摘要（中文）: 随机双层优化（SBO）近年来已广泛应用于机器学习范式，包括超参数优化、元学习及强化学习。随着应用领域的不断拓展，针对SBO计算行为的研究已取得丰硕成果。然而，从统计学习理论视角审视，现有研究对SBO方法泛化性能的理论保障仍缺乏系统认知。本文针对基于一阶梯度的双层优化方法展开系统性泛化分析。首先，建立了SBO方法的平均参数稳定性与泛化误差之间的量化关联。随后，分别在非凸-非凸（NC-NC）、凸-凸（C-C）及强凸-强凸（SC-SC）三种设定下，推导了单时间尺度随机梯度下降（SGD）与双时间尺度SGD的平均参数稳定性上界。实验分析验证了理论结果的可靠性。相较于既有算法稳定性研究，本文成果无需在每次迭代时重新初始化内层参数，且适用于更广泛的目标函数类别。

TLDR: This paper provides a theoretical generalization analysis for first-order stochastic bilevel optimization methods, establishing connections between argument stability and generalization gap, and deriving stability bounds for several settings (nonconvex-nonconvex, convex-convex, strongly-convex-strongly-convex).

简要说明: 本文对一阶随机双层优化方法进行了理论泛化性分析，建立了参数稳定性与泛化误差之间的定量联系，并推导了非凸-非凸、凸-凸、强凸-强凸等几种设定下的稳定性上界。

Authors: Xuelin Zhang, Hong Chen, Bin Gu, Tieliang Gong, Feng Zheng

Embedding Enhancement via Fine-Tuned Language Models for Learner-Item Cognitive Modeling

Abstract (English): Learner-item cognitive modeling plays a central role in the web-based online intelligent education system by enabling cognitive diagnosis (CD) across diverse online educational scenarios. Although ID embedding remains the mainstream approach in cognitive modeling due to its effectiveness and flexibility, recent advances in language models (LMs) have introduced new possibilities for incorporating rich semantic representations to enhance CD performance. This highlights the need for a comprehensive analysis of how LMs enhance embeddings through semantic integration across mainstream CD tasks. This paper identifies two key challenges in fully leveraging LMs in existing work: Misalignment between the training objectives of LMs and CD models creates a distribution gap in feature spaces; A unified framework is essential for integrating textual embeddings across varied CD tasks while preserving the strengths of existing cognitive modeling paradigms to ensure the robustness of embedding enhancement. To address these challenges, this paper introduces EduEmbed, a unified embedding enhancement framework that leverages fine-tuned LMs to enrich learner-item cognitive modeling across diverse CD tasks. EduEmbed operates in two stages. In the first stage, we fine-tune LMs based on role-specific representations and an interaction diagnoser to bridge the semantic gap of CD models. In the second stage, we employ a textual adapter to extract task-relevant semantics and integrate them with existing modeling paradigms to improve generalization. We evaluate the proposed framework on four CD tasks and computerized adaptive testing (CAT) task, achieving robust performance. Further analysis reveals the impact of semantic information across diverse tasks, offering key insights for future research on the application of LMs in CD for online intelligent education systems.

摘要（中文）: 学习者-项目认知建模通过网络在线智能教育系统实现在多样化在线教育场景中的认知诊断，因而扮演着核心角色。尽管ID嵌入因其有效性和灵活性仍然是认知建模的主流方法，但语言模型的最新进展为融入丰富的语义表示以提升认知诊断性能提供了新的可能性。这凸显了深入分析语言模型如何通过语义整合在主流认知诊断任务中增强嵌入表示的必要性。本文指出当前研究中充分利用语言模型面临的两个关键挑战：语言模型与认知诊断模型的训练目标不一致导致特征空间存在分布差异；需要一个统一的框架来整合各类认知诊断任务中的文本嵌入，同时保留现有认知建模范式的优势以确保嵌入增强的鲁棒性。为应对这些挑战，本文提出EduEmbed——一个统一的嵌入增强框架，该框架利用微调的语言模型来丰富跨多种认知诊断任务的学习者-项目认知建模。EduEmbed分为两个阶段运行：第一阶段，我们基于角色特定表示和交互诊断器对语言模型进行微调，以弥合认知诊断模型的语义鸿沟；第二阶段，我们采用文本适配器提取任务相关语义，并将其与现有建模范式相融合以提升泛化能力。我们在四项认知诊断任务及计算机化自适应测试任务上对所提框架进行评估，均取得了稳健的性能表现。进一步分析揭示了语义信息在不同任务中的影响，为未来语言模型在在线智能教育系统认知诊断中的应用研究提供了关键见解。

TLDR: This paper introduces EduEmbed, a framework that fine-tunes language models to improve cognitive diagnosis models for educational assessment, with no connection to robotics or embodied AI.

Authors: Yuanhao Liu, Zihan Zhou, Kaiying Wu, Shuo Liu, Yiyang Huang, Jiajun Guo, Aimin Zhou, Hong Qian

Parent Selection Mechanisms in Elitist Crossover-Based Algorithms

Abstract (English): Parent selection methods are widely used in evolutionary computation to accelerate the optimization process, yet their theoretical benefits are still poorly understood. In this paper, we address this gap by incorporating different parent selection strategies into the $(μ+1)$ genetic algorithm (GA). We show that, with an appropriately chosen population size and a parent selection strategy that selects a pair of maximally distant parents with probability $Ω(1)$ for crossover, the resulting algorithm solves the Jump$_k$ problem in $O(k4^kn\log(n))$ expected time. This bound is significantly smaller than the best known bound of $O(nμ\log(μ)+n\log(n)+n^{k-1})$ for any $(μ+1)$~GA using no explicit diversity-preserving mechanism and a constant crossover probability. To establish this result, we introduce a novel diversity metric that captures both the maximum distance between pairs of individuals in the population and the number of pairs achieving this distance. The crucial point of our analysis is that it relies on crossover as a mechanism for creating and maintaining diversity throughout the run, rather than using crossover only in the final step to combine already diversified individuals, as it has been done in many previous works. The insights provided by our analysis contribute to a deeper theoretical understanding of the role of crossover in the population dynamics of genetic algorithms.

摘要（中文）: 在进化计算中，父代选择方法被广泛用于加速优化过程，但其理论优势仍未得到充分理解。本文通过将不同的父代选择策略融入$(μ+1)$遗传算法中，填补了这一研究空白。研究表明，当选择适当的种群规模，并采用一种以概率$Ω(1)$选择一对最大距离父代进行交叉的父代选择策略时，所得算法能够以$O(k4^kn\log(n))$的期望时间解决Jump$_k$问题。这一界限显著优于已知的最优界限$O(nμ\log(μ)+n\log(n)+n^{k-1})$，后者针对的是不使用显式多样性保持机制且具有恒定交叉概率的任何$(μ+1)$~GA。 为证明这一结果，我们引入了一种新的多样性度量指标，该指标同时捕捉了种群中个体对之间的最大距离以及达到该距离的对数。我们分析的关键之处在于，整个运行过程中将交叉作为创建和维持多样性的机制，而非如以往许多研究那样仅在最后一步将已经多样化的个体进行组合。我们的分析所揭示的见解有助于从理论上更深入地理解遗传算法种群动态中交叉的作用。

TLDR: Theoretical analysis showing that a genetic algorithm using a specific parent selection strategy to maximize diversity solves the Jump_k benchmark problem faster than previous methods, emphasizing crossover's role in maintaining population diversity.

简要说明: 理论分析表明，采用特定最大化多样性亲本选择策略的遗传算法，其求解 Jump_k 基准问题的速度超过先前方法，强调了交叉操作在维持种群多样性中的作用。

Authors: Andre Opris, Denis Antipov

BAAI Cardiac Agent: An intelligent multimodal agent for automated reasoning and diagnosis of cardiovascular diseases from cardiac magnetic resonance imaging

Abstract (English): Cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) is a cornerstone for diagnosing cardiovascular disease. However, it remains underutilized due to complex, time-consuming interpretation across multi-sequences, phases, quantitative measures that heavily reliant on specialized expertise. Here, we present BAAI Cardiac Agent, a multimodal intelligent system designed for end-to-end CMR interpretation. The agent integrates specialized cardiac expert models to perform automated segmentation of cardiac structures, functional quantification, tissue characterization and disease diagnosis, and generates structured clinical reports within a unified workflow. Evaluated on CMR datasets from two hospitals (2413 patients) spanning 7-types of major cardiovascular diseases, the agent achieved an area under the receiver-operating-characteristic curve exceeding 0.93 internally and 0.81 externally. In the task of estimating left ventricular function indices, the results generated by this system for core parameters such as ejection fraction, stroke volume, and left ventricular mass are highly consistent with clinical reports, with Pearson correlation coefficients all exceeding 0.90. The agent outperformed state-of-the-art models in segmentation and diagnostic tasks, and generated clinical reports showing high concordance with expert radiologists (six readers across three experience levels). By dynamically orchestrating expert models for coordinated multimodal analysis, this agent framework enables accurate, efficient CMR interpretation and highlights its potentials for complex clinical imaging workflows. Code is available at https://github.com/plantain-herb/Cardiac-Agent.

摘要（中文）: 心脏磁共振（CMR）是诊断心血管疾病的基石技术。然而，由于需对多序列、多时相及定量测量进行复杂耗时的解读，且高度依赖专业经验，其临床应用仍不充分。本文提出BAAI心脏智能体，这是一个专为端到端CMR解读设计的多模态智能系统。该智能体通过整合专业心脏模型，在统一工作流中实现心脏结构自动分割、功能量化、组织表征与疾病诊断，并生成结构化临床报告。基于两家医院涵盖7类主要心血管疾病的2413例患者CMR数据集评估显示，该智能体接收者操作特征曲线下面积内部验证超过0.93，外部验证达0.81。在左心室功能指标评估任务中，系统对射血分数、每搏输出量及左心室质量等核心参数的计算结果与临床报告高度一致，皮尔逊相关系数均超过0.90。该智能体在分割与诊断任务中表现优于现有先进模型，其生成的临床报告与不同经验水平（三位阅片医师）的放射科专家诊断具有高度一致性。通过动态协调专家模型进行多模态协同分析，该智能体框架实现了精准高效的CMR解读，彰显了其在复杂临床影像工作流程中的应用潜力。代码开源地址：https://github.com/plantain-herb/Cardiac-Agent。

Authors: Taiping Qu, Hongkai Zhang, Lantian Zhang, Can Zhao, Nan Zhang, Hui Wang, Zhen Zhou, Mingye Zou, Kairui Bo, Pengfei Zhao, Xingxing Jin, Zixian Su, Kun Jiang, Huan Liu, Yu Du, Maozhou Wang, Ruifang Yan, Zhongyuan Wang, Tiejun Huang, Lei Xu, Henggui Zhang

Supervised Dimensionality Reduction Revisited: Why LDA on Frozen CNN Features Deserves a Second Look

Abstract (English): Effective ride-hailing dispatch requires anticipating demand patterns that vary substantially across time-of-day, day-of-week, season, and special events. We propose a regime-calibrated approach that (i) segments historical trip data into demand regimes, (ii) matches the current operating period to the most similar historical analogues via a six-metric similarity ensemble (Kolmogorov-Smirnov, Wasserstein-1, feature distance, variance ratio, event pattern, temporal proximity), and (iii) uses the resulting calibrated demand prior to drive both an LP-based fleet repositioning policy and batch dispatch with Hungarian matching. In ablation, a distributional-only subset is strongest on mean wait, while the full ensemble is retained as a robustness-oriented default. Evaluated on 5.2 million NYC TLC trips across 8 diverse scenarios (winter/summer, weekday/weekend/holiday, morning/evening/night) with 5 random seeds each, our method reduces mean rider wait times by 31.1% (bootstrap 95% CI: [26.5, 36.6]%; Friedman chi-sq = 80.0, p = 4.25e-18; Cohen's d = 7.5-29.9 across scenarios). The improvement extends to the tail: P95 wait drops 37.6% and the Gini coefficient of wait times improves from 0.441 to 0.409 (7.3% relative). The two contributions compose multiplicatively and are independently validated: calibration provides 16.9% reduction; LP repositioning adds a further 15.5%. The approach requires no training, is deterministic and explainable, generalizes to Chicago (23.3% wait reduction via NYC-built regime library), and is robust across fleet sizes (32-47% improvement for 0.5-2x fleet scaling). We provide comprehensive ablation studies, formal statistical tests, and routing-fidelity validation with OSRM.

摘要（中文）: 高效的网约车调度需要准确预测随一天中的时段、星期几、季节及特殊事件而大幅波动的需求模式。本文提出一种基于状态校准的方法，该方法首先将历史出行数据划分为不同需求状态；其次通过包含六项指标的相似度集成模型（科尔莫戈罗夫-斯米尔诺夫距离、沃瑟斯坦-1距离、特征距离、方差比、事件模式及时序邻近度）将当前运营时段与最相似的历史时段进行匹配；最后利用所得校准后的需求先验信息，同时驱动基于线性规划的车辆重定位策略和基于匈牙利匹配的批量派单系统。在消融实验中，仅使用分布特征的子集在降低平均等待时间方面表现最优，而完整集成模型因其稳健性被保留作为默认配置。 该方法在涵盖八种不同场景（冬季/夏季、工作日/周末/节假日、清晨/傍晚/夜间）的520万条纽约出租车委员会行程数据上进行评估，每种场景采用5个随机种子。实验表明：乘客平均等待时间降低31.1%（自助法95%置信区间：[26.5, 36.6]%；弗里德曼卡方检验值=80.0，p=4.25e-18；各场景科恩效应值d=7.5-29.9）。改进效果延伸至长尾分布：P95等待时间下降37.6%，等待时间基尼系数从0.441改善至0.409（相对改善7.3%）。两项技术贡献呈乘数效应且独立验证：校准环节贡献16.9%的降幅，线性规划重定位策略额外贡献15.5%。该方法无需训练过程，具有确定性和可解释性，可推广至芝加哥市场（通过纽约构建的状态库实现等待时间降低23.3%），并在不同车队规模下保持稳健（车队规模在0.5-2倍缩放时获得32-47%的改进）。我们提供了全面的消融实验、正式统计检验以及基于OSRM的路径保真度验证。

TLDR: This paper proposes a regime-calibrated demand prediction and dispatch system for ride-hailing, which uses historical trip data segmentation and a multi-metric similarity ensemble to improve vehicle repositioning and matching. The method is evaluated on large-scale real-world trip data and significantly reduces rider wait times.

简要说明: 本研究提出了一种用于网约车调度的体制校准方法，通过对历史出行数据分段和使用多指标相似度集成来优化车辆调度和匹配，在大规模真实数据评估中显著减少了乘客等待时间。

Authors: Indar Kumar, Girish Karhana, Sai Krishna Jasti, Ankit Hemant Lade

Automating Cloud Security and Forensics Through a Secure-by-Design Generative AI Framework

Abstract (English): As cloud environments become increasingly complex, cybersecurity and forensic investigations must evolve to meet emerging threats. Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise in automating log analysis and reasoning tasks, yet they remain vulnerable to prompt injection attacks and lack forensic rigor. To address these dual challenges, we propose a unified, secure-by-design GenAI framework that integrates PromptShield and the Cloud Investigation Automation Framework (CIAF). PromptShield proactively defends LLMs against adversarial prompts using ontology-driven validation that standardizes user inputs and mitigates manipulation. CIAF streamlines cloud forensic investigations through structured, ontology-based reasoning across all six phases of the forensic process. We evaluate our system on real-world datasets from AWS and Microsoft Azure, demonstrating substantial improvements in both LLM security and forensic accuracy. Experimental results show PromptShield boosts classification performance under attack conditions, achieving precision, recall, and F1 scores above 93%, while CIAF enhances ransomware detection accuracy in cloud logs using Likert-transformed performance features. Our integrated framework advances the automation, interpretability, and trustworthiness of cloud forensics and LLM-based systems, offering a scalable foundation for real-time, AI-driven incident response across diverse cloud infrastructures.

摘要（中文）: 随着云环境日益复杂化，网络安全与取证分析必须不断发展以应对新兴威胁。大语言模型在自动化日志分析与推理任务中展现出潜力，但其仍易受提示词注入攻击且缺乏取证严谨性。为应对这两重挑战，我们提出一种统一的安全设计生成式人工智能框架，该框架整合了PromptShield与云调查自动化框架。PromptShield采用本体驱动验证机制，通过标准化用户输入和抵御恶意操控，主动防御大语言模型免受对抗性提示词攻击；云调查自动化框架则基于本体化结构推理，在取证全流程六阶段中实现云取证调查的标准化。我们在真实场景的AWS与微软Azure数据集上评估系统性能，证明其在大语言模型安全性和取证准确性方面均取得显著提升。实验结果显示，PromptShield在攻击条件下将分类性能提升至精确率、召回率与F1分数均超93%，而云调查自动化框架通过李克特量表转化的性能特征，有效增强了云日志中勒索软件检测的准确度。这一集成框架推动了云取证与大语言模型系统在自动化、可解释性与可信度方面的进步，为跨多类云基础设施的实时人工智能驱动事件响应提供了可扩展基础。

Authors: Dalal Alharthi, Ivan Roberto Kawaminami Garcia

I-CALM: Incentivizing Confidence-Aware Abstention for LLM Hallucination Mitigation

Abstract (English): Large language models (LLMs) frequently produce confident but incorrect answers, partly because common binary scoring conventions reward answering over honestly expressing uncertainty. We study whether prompt-only interventions -- explicitly announcing reward schemes for answer-versus-abstain decisions plus humility-oriented normative principles -- can reduce hallucination risk without modifying the model. Our focus is epistemic abstention on factual questions with a verifiable answer, where current LLMs often fail to abstain despite being uncertain about their answers. We first assess self-reported verbal confidence as a usable uncertainty signal, showing stability under prompt paraphrasing and reasonable calibration against a token-probability baseline. We then study I-CALM, a prompt-based framework that (i) elicits verbal confidence, (ii) partially rewards abstention through explicit reward schemes, and (iii) adds lightweight normative principles emphasizing truthfulness, humility, and responsibility. Using GPT-5 mini on PopQA as the main setting, we find that confidence-eliciting, abstention-rewarding prompts, especially with norms, reduce the false-answer rate on answered cases mainly by identifying and shifting error-prone cases to abstention and re-calibrating their confidence. This trades coverage for reliability while leaving forced-answer performance largely unchanged. Varying the abstention reward yields a clear abstention-hallucination frontier. Overall, results show the framework can improve selective answering on factual questions without retraining, with the magnitude of effect varying across models and datasets. Code is available at the following https://github.com/binzeli/hallucinationControl.

摘要（中文）: 大型语言模型（LLM）常常会生成看似自信但实则错误的答案，部分原因在于常见的二元评分机制倾向于奖励回答问题而非诚实表达不确定性。本研究探讨是否仅通过提示干预——即明确宣布对回答与弃答的奖励机制，并引入谦逊导向的规范原则——即可在不修改模型的情况下降低幻觉风险。我们重点关注在可验证事实性问题上的认知性弃答行为，当前LLM在面对此类问题时往往即使在不确定答案的情况下仍不选择弃答。我们首先评估了自我报告的语言置信度作为可用不确定性信号的可靠性，证明其在提示重述下的稳定性，并与基于词元概率的基线进行了合理的校准对比。随后，我们研究了I-CALM这一基于提示的框架，该框架（i）激发语言置信度表达，（ii）通过明确的奖励机制对弃答行为给予部分奖励，（iii）加入强调真实性、谦逊和责任的轻量级规范原则。以PopQA数据集上使用GPT-5 mini为主要实验场景，研究发现：激发置信度表达并奖励弃答的提示策略（尤其是结合规范原则时）能显著降低已回答案例中的错误答案率，主要通过识别易错案例并将其转向弃答，同时重新校准其置信度。这一机制在保持强制回答性能基本不变的情况下，以覆盖率为代价提升了可靠性。调整弃答奖励值可清晰呈现弃答-幻觉边界曲线。总体而言，结果表明该框架无需重新训练即可改进模型在事实性问题上的选择性回答能力，其效果程度因模型和数据集而异。代码可在以下网址获取：https://github.com/binzeli/hallucinationControl。

TLDR: This paper introduces I-CALM, a prompt-based framework to reduce LLM hallucinations by eliciting verbal confidence and rewarding abstention on uncertain factual questions, trading coverage for reliability.

简要说明: 本文提出了I-CALM框架，通过提示引导LLM自我报告置信度，并奖励其对不确定的事实问题选择弃答，以降低幻觉，在可靠性和覆盖率之间进行权衡。

Authors: Haotian Zong, Binze Li, Yufei Long, Sinyin Chang, Jialong Wu, Gillian K. Hadfield

Latency-Aware Resource Allocation over Heterogeneous Networks: A Lorentz-Invariant Market Mechanism

Abstract (English): We present a telecom-native auction mechanism for allocating bandwidth and time slots across heterogeneous-delay networks, ranging from low-Earth-orbit (LEO) satellite constellations to delay-tolerant deep-space relays. The Lorentz-Invariant Auction (LIA) treats bids as spacetime events and reweights reported values based on the \emph{horizon slack}, a causal quantity derived from the earliest-arrival times relative to a public clearing horizon. Unlike other delay-equalization rules, LIA combines a causal-ordering formulation, a uniquely exponential slack correction implied by a semigroup-style invariance axiom, and a critical-value implementation that ensures truthful reported values once slacks are fixed by trusted infrastructure. We analyze the incentive result in the exogenous-slack regime and separately examine bounded slack-estimation error and endogenous-delay limitations. Under fixed feasible slacks, LIA is individually rational and achieves welfare at least \(e^{-λΔ}\) relative to the optimal feasible allocation, where \(Δ\) is the slack spread. We evaluate LIA on STARLINK-200, INTERNET-100, and DSN-30 across 52,500 baseline instances with market sizes \(n\in\{10,20,30,40,50\}\) and conduct additional robustness sweeps. On Starlink and Internet, LIA maintains near-efficiency while eliminating measured timing rents. However, on DSN, welfare is lower in thin markets but improves with depth. We also distinguish winner-determination time from the background cost of maintaining slack estimates and study robustness beyond independent and identically distributed (iid) noise through error-spread bounds and structured (distance-biased and subnetwork-correlated) noise models. These results suggest that causal-consistent mechanism design offers a practical non-buffering alternative to synchronized delay equalization in heterogeneous telecom infrastructures.

摘要（中文）: 本文提出了一种电信原生拍卖机制，用于在从低地球轨道（LEO）卫星星座到容忍延迟的深空中继等异构延迟网络中分配带宽和时隙。洛伦兹不变性拍卖（LIA）将投标视为时空事件，并根据“视界松弛”（一种基于相对于公共清算视界的最早到达时间推导出的因果性量值）对申报价值进行重新加权。与其他延迟均衡规则不同，LIA结合了因果排序框架、由半群式不变性公理所隐含的独特指数型松弛校正，以及一种临界值实现机制——该机制确保一旦松弛值由可信基础设施确定，所申报的价值即为真实值。我们分析了外生松弛机制下的激励效果，并分别考察了有界松弛估计误差和内生延迟限制。在固定可行松弛条件下，LIA满足个体理性，并实现相对于最优可行分配至少 \(e^{-λΔ}\) 的福利水平，其中 \(Δ\) 为松弛跨度。我们在包含市场规模 \(n\in\{10,20,30,40,50\}\) 的52,500个基准实例上，对STARLINK-200、INTERNET-100和DSN-30网络评估了LIA的性能，并进行了额外的鲁棒性扫描。在Starlink和Internet网络上，LIA在保持接近最优效率的同时消除了实测的时序租金。然而在DSN网络中，稀疏市场下的福利水平较低，但随市场深度增加而改善。我们还区分了获胜者确定时间与维持松弛估计的背景成本，并通过误差跨度边界和结构化（距离偏置及子网相关）噪声模型研究了独立同分布噪声之外的鲁棒性。这些结果表明，因果一致性机制设计为异构电信基础设施中的同步延迟均衡提供了一种实用的非缓冲替代方案。

TLDR: This paper proposes a novel auction mechanism for allocating bandwidth and time slots in heterogeneous-delay telecommunication networks, focusing on latency-aware resource allocation, not on robotics or embodied AI.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种用于异构延迟电信网络中带宽和时隙分配的新型拍卖机制，专注于延迟感知的资源分配，与机器人或具身AI无关。

Authors: Saad Alqithami

LAA-X: Unified Localized Artifact Attention for Quality-Agnostic and Generalizable Face Forgery Detection

Abstract (English): In this paper, we propose Localized Artifact Attention X (LAA-X), a novel deepfake detection framework that is both robust to high-quality forgeries and capable of generalizing to unseen manipulations. Existing approaches typically rely on binary classifiers coupled with implicit attention mechanisms, which often fail to generalize beyond known manipulations. In contrast, LAA-X introduces an explicit attention strategy based on a multi-task learning framework combined with blending-based data synthesis. Auxiliary tasks are designed to guide the model toward localized, artifact-prone (i.e., vulnerable) regions. The proposed framework is compatible with both CNN and transformer backbones, resulting in two different versions, namely, LAA-Net and LAA-Former, respectively. Despite being trained only on real and pseudo-fake samples, LAA-X competes with state-of-the-art methods across multiple benchmarks. Code and pre-trained weights for LAA-Net\footnote{https://github.com/10Ring/LAA-Net} and LAA-Former\footnote{https://github.com/10Ring/LAA-Former} are publicly available.

摘要（中文）: 本文提出了一种新颖的深度伪造检测框架——局部伪影注意力X（LAA-X），该框架不仅对高质量伪造内容具有鲁棒性，且能泛化至未见过的篡改类型。现有方法通常依赖二元分类器与隐式注意力机制的结合，这类方法往往难以泛化至已知篡改类型之外。与此不同，LAA-X基于多任务学习框架并结合基于混合的数据合成技术，引入了一种显式注意力策略。辅助任务被设计用于引导模型关注局部化、易产生伪影（即脆弱）的区域。所提框架兼容CNN与Transformer骨干网络，相应衍生出两个版本：LAA-Net与LAA-Former。尽管仅使用真实样本与伪伪造样本进行训练，LAA-X在多个基准测试中均与当前最先进方法取得可比性能。LAA-Net与LAA-Former的代码及预训练权重均已公开。

TLDR: Proposes LAA-X, a framework using explicit attention and multi-task learning to improve generalization and robustness of deepfake detection.

简要说明: 提出LAA-X框架，利用显式注意力和多任务学习来提高深度伪造检测的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Authors: Dat Nguyen, Enjie Ghorbel, Anis Kacem, Marcella Astrid, Djamila Aouada

TORA: Topological Representation Alignment for 3D Shape Assembly

Abstract (English): Flow-matching methods for 3D shape assembly learn point-wise velocity fields that transport parts toward assembled configurations, yet they receive no explicit guidance about which cross-part interactions should drive the motion. We introduce TORA, a topology-first representation alignment framework that distills relational structure from a frozen pretrained 3D encoder into the flow-matching backbone during training. We first realize this via simple instantiation, token-wise cosine matching, which injects the learned geometric descriptors from the teacher representation. We then extend to employ a Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) loss to match the similarity structure between student and teacher representations for enhanced topological alignment. Through systematic probing of diverse 3D encoders, we show that geometry- and contact-centric teacher properties, not semantic classification ability, govern alignment effectiveness, and that alignment is most beneficial at later transformer layers where spatial structure naturally emerges. TORA introduces zero inference overhead while yielding two consistent benefits: faster convergence (up to 6.9$\times$) and improved accuracy in-distribution, along with greater robustness under domain shift. Experiments on five benchmarks spanning geometric, semantic, and inter-object assembly demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, with particularly pronounced gains in zero-shot transfer to unseen real-world and synthetic datasets. Project page: https://nahyuklee.github.io/tora.

摘要（中文）: 面向三维形状装配的流匹配方法通过学习点对点的速度场，将零件输送到装配构型，但该方法并未获得关于何种跨零件交互应驱动运动的显式指导。本文提出TORA——一种拓扑优先的表征对齐框架，该框架在训练过程中从冻结的预训练三维编码器中提取关系结构，并将其注入流匹配主干网络。我们首先通过简单的实例化实现这一目标，即基于词元的余弦匹配，从而将教师表征中学习到的几何描述符注入学生模型。随后，我们进一步引入中心核对齐损失，通过匹配学生与教师表征间的相似性结构来增强拓扑对齐效果。通过对多种三维编码器的系统化探究，我们发现决定对齐效果的关键在于教师模型以几何和接触关系为核心的特性，而非语义分类能力；同时，在Transformer网络的后层中——空间结构自然形成之处——对齐带来的收益最为显著。TORA方法不引入任何额外推理开销，同时带来两项稳定优势：更快的收敛速度（最高达6.9倍）以及在分布内精度的提升，且在领域偏移下展现出更强的鲁棒性。在涵盖几何、语义及跨对象装配的五个基准测试中，TORA均实现了最先进的性能表现，特别是在向未见过的真实世界和合成数据集进行零样本迁移时，其性能提升尤为显著。项目页面：https://nahyuklee.github.io/tora。

TLDR: Proposes a topology-first representation alignment framework for 3D shape assembly, using a pretrained 3D encoder to guide a flow-matching model, resulting in faster convergence and improved performance on assembly benchmarks.

简要说明: 提出了一个用于三维形状装配的拓扑优先表示对齐框架，利用预训练的三维编码器来指导流匹配模型，从而实现了更快的收敛速度和更好的装配基准性能。

Authors: Nahyuk Lee, Zhiang Chen, Marc Pollefeys, Sunghwan Hong

Stable and Privacy-Preserving Synthetic Educational Data with Empirical Marginals: A Copula-Based Approach

Abstract (English): To advance Educational Data Mining (EDM) within strict privacy-protecting regulatory frameworks, researchers must develop methods that enable data-driven analysis while protecting sensitive student information. Synthetic data generation is one such approach, enabling the release of statistically generated samples instead of real student records; however, existing deep learning and parametric generators often distort marginal distributions and degrade under iterative regeneration, leading to distribution drift and progressive loss of distributional support that compromise reliability. In response, we introduce the Non-Parametric Gaussian Copula (NPGC), a plug-and-play synthesis method that replaces deep learning and parametric optimization with empirical statistical anchoring to preserve the observed marginal distributions while modeling dependencies through a copula framework. NPGC integrates Differential Privacy (DP) at both the marginal and correlation levels, supports heterogeneous variable types, and treats missing data as an explicit state to retain informative absence patterns. We evaluate NPGC against deep learning and parametric baselines on five benchmark datasets and demonstrate that it remains stable across multiple regeneration cycles and achieves competitive downstream performance at substantially lower computational cost. We further validate NPGC through deployment in a real-world online learning platform, demonstrating its practicality for privacy-preserving research.

摘要（中文）: 为在严格隐私保护法规框架下推进教育数据挖掘，研究人员必须开发既能实现数据驱动分析又能保护敏感学生信息的方法。合成数据生成便是其中一种途径，它通过发布统计生成的样本来替代真实学生记录；然而，现有深度学习和参数化生成器常会扭曲边缘分布，且在迭代再生过程中性能下降，导致分布漂移和分布支撑的持续损失，从而损害数据可靠性。对此，我们提出非参数高斯耦合（NPGC）方法——一种即插即用的合成技术，它通过经验统计锚定替代深度学习和参数化优化，在利用耦合框架建模依赖关系的同时保持观测到的边缘分布。NPGC在边缘分布和相关性层面均集成了差分隐私保护机制，支持异构变量类型，并将缺失数据视为显式状态以保留信息性的缺失模式。我们在五个基准数据集上对NPGC与深度学习和参数化基线方法进行对比评估，证明该方法在多轮再生循环中保持稳定，并以显著更低的计算成本实现了具有竞争力的下游任务性能。我们进一步通过在真实在线学习平台中的部署验证了NPGC的实用性，展现了其在隐私保护研究领域的应用价值。

Authors: Gabriel Diaz Ramos, Lorenzo Luzi, Debshila Basu Mallick, Richard Baraniuk

Uncertainty-Aware Foundation Models for Clinical Data

Abstract (English): Healthcare foundation models have largely followed paradigms from natural language processing and computer vision, emphasizing large scale pretraining and deterministic representations over heterogeneous clinical data. However, clinical observations are inherently incomplete, reflecting sparse, irregular, and modality dependent measurements of an underlying physiologic state. In this work, we propose a framework for uncertainty aware foundation modeling that represents each patient not as a point embedding, but as a distribution over plausible latent states. By learning set valued representations and enforcing consistency across partial views of the same patient, the model captures what is invariantly inferable while explicitly encoding epistemic uncertainty. We integrate this formulation with multimodal encoders and scalable self supervised objectives, combining reconstruction, contrastive alignment, and distributional regularization. Across diverse clinical tasks, our approach improves predictive performance, robustness under missing data, and uncertainty calibration relative to strong baselines. These results suggest that modeling what is not observed rather than only what is constitutes a critical inductive bias for healthcare foundation models.

摘要（中文）: 医疗基础模型主要遵循了自然语言处理和计算机视觉领域的范式，强调在海量异构临床数据上进行大规模预训练并提取确定性表征。然而，临床观察本质上具有不完整性，其反映的是对潜在生理状态稀疏、非规则且依赖于特定模态的测量结果。本研究提出一种不确定性感知的基础建模框架，将每位患者表示为可能潜在状态的概率分布，而非单一的点嵌入表征。通过学习集合值表示并确保同一患者局部视图间的一致性，该模型既能捕获具有不变性的可推断信息，又能显式编码认知不确定性。我们将此框架与多模态编码器及可扩展的自监督目标相结合，整合了重构损失、对比对齐和分布正则化方法。在多样化的临床任务中，相较于强基线模型，本方法在预测性能、数据缺失下的鲁棒性以及不确定性校准方面均有显著提升。这些结果表明，对未观测信息的建模而不仅限于已观测信息，构成了医疗基础模型的关键归纳偏置。

TLDR: This paper proposes a framework for uncertainty-aware foundation models in healthcare, representing patients as distributions over latent states to handle incomplete clinical data, and demonstrates improved performance and robustness.

Authors: Qian Zhou, Yuanyun Zhang, Shi Li

Physical Sensitivity Kernels Can Emerge in Data-Driven Forward Models: Evidence From Surface-Wave Dispersion

Abstract (English): Data-driven neural networks are increasingly used as surrogate forward models in geophysics, but it remains unclear whether they recover only the data mapping or also the underlying physical sensitivity structure. Here we test this question using surface-wave dispersion. By comparing automatically differentiated gradients from a neural-network surrogate with theoretical sensitivity kernels, we show that the learned gradients can recover the main depth-dependent structure of physical kernels across a broad range of periods. This indicates that neural surrogate models can learn physically meaningful differential information, rather than acting as purely black-box predictors. At the same time, strong structural priors in the training distribution can introduce systematic artifacts into the inferred sensitivities. Our results show that neural forward surrogates can recover useful physical information for inversion and uncertainty analysis, while clarifying the conditions under which this differential structure remains physically consistent.

摘要（中文）: 数据驱动的神经网络在日益被用作地球物理学的替代正演模型时，其是否仅恢复数据映射关系，还是同样能够捕捉底层物理敏感结构，仍存在疑问。本文通过表面波频散现象对该问题进行了检验。通过比较神经网络替代模型的自动微分梯度与理论敏感性核函数，我们发现学习的梯度能够在较宽的周期范围内恢复物理核函数的主要深度依赖性结构。这表明神经替代模型能够学习具有物理意义的微分信息，而非仅作为纯粹的黑箱预测器。同时，训练数据中强烈的结构先验可能给推断出的敏感性引入系统性伪影。我们的研究结果表明，神经正演替代模型能够为反演和不确定性分析恢复有效的物理信息，同时明确了这种微分结构保持物理一致性的条件。

TLDR: This paper shows that neural network surrogates for geophysical forward models can recover physically meaningful sensitivity kernels, but training distribution biases can introduce artifacts.

Authors: Ziye Yu, Yuqi Cai, Xin Liu

Extended Hybrid Timed Petri Nets with Semi-Supervised Anomaly Detection for Switched Systems, Modelling and Fault Detection

Abstract (English): Hybrid physical systems combine continuous and discrete dynamics, which can be simultaneously affected by faults. Conventional fault detection methods often treat these dynamics separately, limiting their ability to capture interacting fault patterns. This paper proposes a unified fault detection framework for hybrid dynamical systems by integrating an Extended Timed Continuous Petri Net (ETCPN) model with semi-supervised anomaly detection. The proposed ETCPN extends existing Petri net formalisms by introducing marking-dependent flow functions, enabling intrinsic coupling between discrete and continuous dynamics. Based on this structure, a mode-dependent hybrid observer is designed, whose stability under arbitrary switching is ensured via Linear Matrix Inequalities (LMIs), solved offline to determine observer gains. The observer generates residuals that reflect discrepancies between the estimated and measured outputs. These residuals are processed using semi-supervised methods, including One-Class SVM (OC-SVM), Support Vector Data Description (SVDD), and Elliptic Envelope (EE), trained exclusively on normal data to avoid reliance on labeled faults. The framework is validated through simulations involving discrete faults, continuous faults, and hybrid faults. Results demonstrate high detection accuracy, fast convergence, and robust performance, with OC-SVM and SVDD providing the best trade-off between detection rate and false alarms. The framework is computationally efficient for real-time deployment, as the main complexity is confined to the offline LMI design phase.

摘要（中文）: 摘要： 混合物理系统结合了连续与离散动态特性，且两者可能同时受到故障影响。传统故障检测方法通常将这两种动态特性分开处理，限制了其捕捉交互故障模式的能力。本文提出一种面向混合动力系统的统一故障检测框架，通过将扩展时间连续Petri网（ETCPN）模型与半监督异常检测方法相结合来实现。所提出的ETCPN模型通过引入依赖于标识的流函数，扩展了现有Petri网形式体系，从而实现了离散与连续动态间的内在耦合。基于此结构，设计了模式依赖的混合观测器，其任意切换下的稳定性通过线性矩阵不等式（LMI）离线求解观测器增益来保证。该观测器生成反映估计输出与实测输出间差异的残差信号。这些残差采用半监督方法（包括单类支持向量机、支持向量数据描述和椭圆包络）进行处理，这些方法仅基于正常数据训练，避免了依赖已标注的故障数据。通过包含离散故障、连续故障及混合故障的仿真实验验证了该框架的有效性。结果显示：该框架具有高检测精度、快速收敛性和鲁棒性能，其中单类支持向量机与支持向量数据描述在检测率与误报率间取得了最佳平衡。该框架计算效率高，适合实时部署，因为主要计算复杂度集中在离线线性矩阵不等式设计阶段。

TLDR: Proposes a fault detection framework for hybrid dynamical systems using extended Petri nets and semi-supervised anomaly detection, validated in simulation.

Authors: Fatiha Hamdi, Abdelhafid Zeroual, Fouzi Harrou

Topological Sensitivity in Connectome-Constrained Neural Networks

Abstract (English): Connectome-constrained neural networks are often evaluated against sparse random controls and then interpreted as evidence that biological graph topology improves learning efficiency. We revisit that claim in a controlled flyvis-based study using a Drosophila connectome, a naive self-loop-matched random graph, and a degree-preserving rewired null. Under weak controls, in which both models were recovered from a connectome-trained checkpoint and the null matched only global graph counts, the connectome appeared substantially better in early loss, mean activity, and runtime. That picture changed under stricter controls. Training both graphs from a shared random initialization removed the early loss advantage, and replacing the naive null by a degree-preserving null removed the apparent activity advantage. A five-sample degree-preserving ensemble and a pre-training activity-scale diagnostic further strengthened this revised interpretation. We also report a descriptive mechanism analysis of the earlier weak-control comparison, but we treat it as behavioral characterization rather than proof of causal superiority. We show that previously reported topology advantages in connectome-constrained neural networks can arise from initialization and null-model confounds, and largely disappear under fair from-scratch initialization and degree-preserving controls.

摘要（中文）: 连接组约束神经网络通常通过与稀疏随机对照模型进行比较来评估，并由此解释为生物图拓扑结构能够提升学习效率。我们在果蝇连接组数据基础上，通过基于飞视平台的受控研究重新审视了这一观点，使用了果蝇连接组、朴素自环匹配随机图及度保持重连零模型进行对比。在弱控制条件下（两个模型均从连接组训练检查点恢复，且零模型仅匹配全局图计数），连接组在早期损失、平均激活水平和运行时间上均表现出明显优势。但在更严格的控制条件下，这一图景发生了变化。当两种图结构均从共享随机初始化开始训练时，早期损失优势消失；将朴素零模型替换为度保持零模型后，表观激活优势也不复存在。基于五样本度保持集成与预训练激活尺度诊断的进一步分析，强化了这种修正后的解读。我们还对早期弱控制比较进行了描述性机制分析，但将其视为行为表征而非因果优越性的证明。研究表明，先前报道的连接组约束神经网络拓扑优势可能源于初始化方式与零模型设计的混淆因素，在公平的从零初始化与度保持控制条件下，这些优势基本消失。

TLDR: This paper demonstrates that previously reported advantages of connectome topology in neural networks can be explained by methodological confounds, and largely disappear under stricter controls, challenging claims of causal benefits from biological graph structure.

Authors: Nalin Dhiman

Multirate Stein Variational Gradient Descent for Efficient Bayesian Sampling

Abstract (English): Many particle-based Bayesian inference methods use a single global step size for all parts of the update. In Stein variational gradient descent (SVGD), however, each update combines two qualitatively different effects: attraction toward high-posterior regions and repulsion that preserves particle diversity. These effects can evolve at different rates, especially in high-dimensional, anisotropic, or hierarchical posteriors, so one step size can be unstable in some regions and inefficient in others. We derive a multirate version of SVGD that updates these components on different time scales. The framework yields practical algorithms, including a symmetric split method, a fixed multirate method (MR-SVGD), and an adaptive multirate method (Adapt-MR-SVGD) with local error control. We evaluate the methods in a broad and rigorous benchmark suite covering six problem families: a 50D Gaussian target, multiple 2D synthetic targets, UCI Bayesian logistic regression, multimodal Gaussian mixtures, Bayesian neural networks, and large-scale hierarchical logistic regression. Evaluation includes posterior-matching metrics, predictive performance, calibration quality, mixing, and explicit computational cost accounting. Across these six benchmark families, multirate SVGD variants improve robustness and quality-cost tradeoffs relative to vanilla SVGD. The strongest gains appear on stiff hierarchical, strongly anisotropic, and multimodal targets, where adaptive multirate SVGD is usually the strongest variant and fixed multirate SVGD provides a simpler robust alternative at lower cost.

摘要（中文）: 许多基于粒子的贝叶斯推断方法在更新过程中对所有部分采用统一的全局步长。然而，在斯坦因变分梯度下降（SVGD）中，每次更新融合了两种性质不同的效应：一是向高后验区域的引力吸引，二是保持粒子多样性的斥力排斥。这两种效应可能以不同的速率演变，尤其在高维、各向异性或分层后验分布中，单一全局步长可能导致某些区域更新不稳定，而另一些区域更新效率低下。本文推导出多速率版本的SVGD，使这两个分量在不同时间尺度上更新。该框架产生了多种实用算法，包括对称分裂方法、固定多速率方法（MR-SVGD）以及具有局部误差控制的自适应多速率方法（Adapt-MR-SVGD）。我们通过一个广泛而严谨的基准测试套件评估了这些方法，涵盖六类问题：50维高斯目标、多种二维合成目标、UCI贝叶斯逻辑回归、多峰高斯混合分布、贝叶斯神经网络以及大规模分层逻辑回归。评估内容涵盖后验匹配度量、预测性能、校准质量、混合程度以及显式计算成本核算。在这六类基准测试中，多速率SVGD变体相较于原始SVGD，在鲁棒性和质量-成本权衡方面均有所提升。最大改进出现在刚性分层分布、强各向异性目标及多峰分布中，其中自适应多速率SVGD通常表现最优，而固定多速率SVGD则以较低成本提供了更简洁的鲁棒性替代方案。

TLDR: This paper proposes a multirate version of Stein Variational Gradient Descent (SVGD) for more efficient Bayesian sampling, improving robustness on stiff, anisotropic, or multimodal targets. It is a methodological advancement in sampling algorithms, evaluated on standard Bayesian inference benchmarks.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种用于更高效贝叶斯采样的多速率Stein变分梯度下降方法，在刚性、各向异性或多模态目标上提高了鲁棒性。这是一项采样算法上的方法论进步，在标准贝叶斯推理基准上进行了评估。

Authors: Arash Sarshar

Predict, Don't React: Value-Based Safety Forecasting for LLM Streaming

Abstract (English): In many practical LLM deployments, a single guardrail is used for both prompt and response moderation. Prompt moderation operates on fully observed text, whereas streaming response moderation requires safety decisions to be made over partial generations. Existing text-based streaming guardrails commonly frame this output-side problem as boundary detection, training models to identify the earliest prefix at which a response has already become unsafe. In this work, we introduce StreamGuard, a unified model-agnostic streaming guardrail that instead formulates moderation as a forecasting problem: given a partial prefix, the model predicts the expected harmfulness of likely future continuations. We supervise this prediction using Monte Carlo rollouts, which enables early intervention without requiring exact token-level boundary annotations. Across standard safety benchmarks, StreamGuard performs strongly both for input moderation and for streaming output moderation. At the 8B scale, StreamGuard improves aggregated input-moderation F1 from 86.7 to 88.2 and aggregated streaming output-moderation F1 from 80.4 to 81.9 relative to Qwen3Guard-Stream-8B-strict. On the QWENGUARDTEST response_loc streaming benchmark, StreamGuard reaches 97.5 F1, 95.1 recall, and 92.6% on-time intervention, compared to 95.9 F1, 92.1 recall, and 89.9% for Qwen3Guard-Stream-8B-stric, while reducing the miss rate from 7.9% to 4.9%. We further show that forecasting-based supervision transfers effectively across tokenizers and model families: with transferred targets, Gemma3-StreamGuard-1B reaches 81.3 response-moderation F1, 98.2 streaming F1, and a 3.5% miss rate. These results show that strong end-to-end streaming moderation can be obtained without exact boundary labels, and that forecasting future risk is an effective supervision strategy for low-latency safety intervention.

摘要（中文）: 在许多实际的大语言模型部署中，通常会采用单一的安全护栏机制同时进行提示词和响应内容的审核。提示词审核基于完整可见的文本进行操作，而流式响应审核则需针对部分生成内容做出安全判断。现有的基于文本的流式安全护栏通常将输出端问题定义为边界检测，通过训练模型识别响应内容已确定变得不安全的最早前缀位置。本研究提出了StreamGuard——一种统一的、与模型无关的流式安全护栏，其将审核任务重新定义为预测问题：在给定部分前缀文本的条件下，模型预测未来可能延续内容的预期危害程度。我们采用蒙特卡洛推演方法对此预测过程进行监督，使得系统能够实现早期干预，而无需依赖精确的词元级边界标注。 在标准安全基准测试中，StreamGuard在输入审核和流式输出审核两方面均表现出色。在80亿参数规模下，相较于Qwen3Guard-Stream-8B-strict模型，StreamGuard将输入审核的宏观平均F1分数从86.7提升至88.2，流式输出审核的宏观平均F1分数从80.4提升至81.9。在QWENGUARDTEST数据集的response_loc流式测试基准中，StreamGuard获得了97.5的F1分数、95.1的召回率以及92.6%的实时干预成功率，而Qwen3Guard-Stream-8B-strict模型对应指标分别为95.9、92.1和89.9%，同时将漏检率从7.9%降低至4.9%。我们进一步证明基于预测的监督方法能够有效迁移至不同分词器和模型架构：通过迁移学习目标，Gemma3-StreamGuard-1B模型实现了81.3的响应审核F1分数、98.2的流式审核F1分数以及3.5%的漏检率。这些结果表明，无需精确边界标注即可实现强大的端到端流式内容审核，且对未来风险的预测机制是一种有效的低延迟安全干预监督策略。

TLDR: This paper introduces StreamGuard, a forecasting-based LLM streaming guardrail that predicts future harmfulness of partial generations using Monte Carlo rollouts, improving moderation performance over boundary detection methods.

简要说明: 本文提出StreamGuard，一种基于预测的LLM流式护栏模型，它使用蒙特卡洛推演来预测部分生成的未来危害性，在审核性能上优于边界检测方法。

Authors: Pride Kavumba, Koki Wataoka, Huy H. Nguyen, Jiaxuan Li, Masaya Ohagi

Fused Multinomial Logistic Regression Utilizing Summary-Level External Machine-learning Information

Abstract (English): In many modern applications, a carefully designed primary study provides individual-level data for interpretable modeling, while summary-level external information is available through black-box, efficient, and nonparametric machine-learning predictions. Although summary-level external information has been studied in the data integration literature, there is limited methodology for leveraging external nonparametric machine-learning predictions to improve statistical inference in the primary study. We propose a general empirical-likelihood framework that incorporates external predictions through moment constraints. An advantage of nonparametric machine-learning prediction is that it induces a rich class of valid moment restrictions that remain robust to covariate shift under a mild overlap condition without requiring explicit density-ratio modeling. We focus on multinomial logistic regression as the primary model and address common data-quality issues in external sources, including coarsened outcomes, partially observed covariates, covariate shift, and heterogeneity in generating mechanisms known as concept shift. We establish large-sample properties of the resulting fused estimator, including consistency and asymptotic normality under regularity conditions. Moreover, we provide mild sufficient conditions under which incorporating external predictions delivers a strict efficiency gain relative to the primary-only estimator. Simulation studies and an application to the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey on multiclass blood-pressure classification.

摘要（中文）: 在许多现代应用中，精心设计的主研究为可解释建模提供了个体层面的数据，而通过黑箱、高效、非参数的机器学习预测可获取汇总层面的外部信息。尽管数据整合文献中已对汇总层面的外部信息有所研究，但利用外部非参数机器学习预测来增强主研究统计推断的方法仍较为有限。我们提出了一种广义经验似然框架，通过矩约束整合外部预测。非参数机器学习预测的优势在于其可产生丰富的有效矩约束类别，这些约束在温和的重叠条件下对协变量偏移保持稳健，且无需显式的密度比建模。我们以多项逻辑回归作为主模型，并针对外部数据源中常见的数据质量问题——包括结果粗化、部分协变量观测缺失、协变量偏移以及生成机制异质性（即概念偏移）——提出解决方案。我们建立了所得融合估计量的大样本性质，包括在正则条件下的相合性与渐近正态性。此外，我们给出了温和的充分条件，证明在满足这些条件时，整合外部预测相较于仅使用主研究的估计量能够实现严格的效率提升。通过模拟研究及一项基于国家健康与营养调查的多类别血压分类应用验证了该方法的有效性。

TLDR: This paper proposes a method for integrating summary-level external machine learning predictions into a primary statistical model, specifically multinomial logistic regression, to improve inference and handle data-quality issues like covariate shift. It is a methodology paper in statistics, not robotics or embodied AI.

简要说明: 本文提出一种方法，将外部机器学习预测的摘要级信息整合到主要统计模型中，特别是多项逻辑回归，以改进推理并处理协变量偏移等数据质量问题。这是一篇统计学方法论文，不涉及机器人或具身人工智能。

Authors: Chi-Shian Dai, Jun Shao

Biconvex Biclustering

Abstract (English): This article proposes a biconvex modification to convex biclustering in order to improve its performance in high-dimensional settings. In contrast to heuristics that discard a subset of noisy features a priori, our method jointly learns and accordingly weighs informative features while discovering biclusters. Moreover, the method is adaptive to the data, and is accompanied by an efficient algorithm based on proximal alternating minimization, complete with detailed guidance on hyperparameter tuning and efficient solutions to optimization subproblems. These contributions are theoretically grounded; we establish finite-sample bounds on the objective function under sub-Gaussian errors, and generalize these guarantees to cases where input affinities need not be uniform. Extensive simulation results reveal our method consistently recovers underlying biclusters while weighing and selecting features appropriately, outperforming peer methods. An application to a gene microarray dataset of lymphoma samples recovers biclusters matching an underlying classification, while giving additional interpretation to the mRNA samples via the column groupings and fitted weights.

摘要（中文）: 本文提出了一种对凸双聚类方法的双凸修正，以提升其在高压维环境下的性能。与预先丢弃部分噪声特征的启发式方法不同，本方法在发现双聚类的同时，能够协同学习并相应地对信息特征进行加权。此外，该方法对数据具有自适应能力，并配备了一种基于近端交替最小化的高效算法，其中包含了超参数调优的详细指导及优化子问题的高效求解方案。这些贡献具有理论支撑：我们在亚高斯误差假设下建立了目标函数的有限样本界，并将这些理论保证推广到输入亲和性不必均匀的情形。大量仿真结果表明，本方法在适当加权和选择特征的同时，能够稳定地恢复潜在的双聚类结构，其性能优于同类方法。在淋巴瘤样本基因微阵列数据集的应用中，本方法恢复出的双聚类与潜在分类一致，同时通过列分组和拟合权重为mRNA样本提供了额外的解释维度。

TLDR: The paper introduces a biconvex adaptation of convex biclustering for improved performance in high-dimensional data, with a focus on feature weighting and selection, validated through simulations and a gene microarray application.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种双凸双聚类方法，旨在改进高维数据下的性能，专注于特征加权和选择，并通过模拟和基因微阵列数据集进行了验证。

Authors: Sam Rosen, Eric C. Chi, Jason Xu

Lotka-Sharpe Neural Operators for Control of Population PDEs

Abstract (English): Age-structured predator-prey integro-partial differential equations provide models of interacting populations in ecology, epidemiology, and biotechnology. A key challenge in feedback design for these systems is the scalar $ζ$, defined implicitly by the Lotka-Sharpe nonlinear integral condition, as a mapping from fertility and mortality rates to $ζ$. To solve this challenge with operator learning, we first prove that the Lotka-Sharpe operator is Lipschitz continuous, guaranteeing the existence of arbitrarily accurate neural operator approximations over a compact set of fertility and mortality functions. We then show that the resulting approximate feedback law preserves semi-global practical asymptotic stability under propagation of the operator approximation error through various other nonlinear operators, all the way through to the control input. In the numerical results, not only do we learn ``once-and-for-all'' the canonical Lotka-Sharpe (LS) operator, and thus make it available for future uses in control of other age-structured population interconnections, but we demonstrate the online usage of the neural LS operator under estimation of the fertility and mortality functions.

摘要（中文）: 年龄结构的捕食者-猎物积分-偏微分方程为生态学、流行病学和生物技术中的相互作用种群提供了模型。此类系统反馈设计中的核心挑战在于标量 $ζ$，它由 Lotka-Sharpe 非线性积分条件隐式定义，作为从生育率和死亡率到 $ζ$ 的映射。为通过算子学习解决这一难题，我们首先证明了 Lotka-Sharpe 算子具有 Lipschitz 连续性，从而保证在紧致的生育率和死亡率函数集上存在任意精度的神经算子逼近。随后我们证明，在算子逼近误差通过其他非线性算子传播至控制输入的过程中，所得近似反馈律仍能保持半全局实际渐近稳定性。数值结果表明，我们不仅一次性学习了经典的 Lotka-Sharpe 算子，使其可用于未来其他年龄结构种群互连系统的控制，还展示了该神经 LS 算子在生育率和死亡率函数估计场景下的在线应用效能。

TLDR: This paper introduces a neural operator method to approximate the nonlinear Lotka-Sharpe integral operator for feedback control of age-structured population PDEs, proving stability of the approximate controller.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种神经算子方法来近似用于年龄结构人口偏微分方程反馈控制的非线性Lotka-Sharpe积分算子，并证明了近似控制器的稳定性。

Authors: Miroslav Krstic, Iasson Karafyllis, Luke Bhan, Carina Veil

Spatiotemporal Interpolation of GEDI Biomass with Calibrated Uncertainty

Abstract (English): Monitoring deforestation-driven carbon emissions requires both spatially explicit and temporally continuous estimates of aboveground biomass density (AGBD) with calibrated uncertainty. NASA's Global Ecosystem Dynamics Investigation (GEDI) provides reliable LIDAR-derived AGBD, but its orbital sampling causes irregular spatiotemporal coverage, and occasional operational interruptions, including a 13-month hibernation from March 2023 to April 2024, leave extended gaps in the observational record. Prior work has used machine learning approaches to fill GEDI's spatial gaps using satellite-derived features, but temporal interpolation of biomass through unobserved periods, particularly across active disturbance events, remains largely unaddressed. Moreover, standard ensemble methods for biomass mapping have been shown to produce systematically miscalibrated prediction intervals. To address these gaps, we extend the Attentive Neural Process (ANP) framework, previously applied to spatial biomass interpolation, to jointly sparse spatiotemporal settings using geospatial foundation model embeddings. We treat space and time symmetrically, empirically validating a form of space-for-time substitution in which observations from nearby locations at other times inform predictions at held-out periods. Our results demonstrate that the ANP produces well-calibrated uncertainty estimates across disturbance regimes, supporting its use in Measurement, Reporting, and Verification (MRV) applications that require reliable uncertainty quantification for forest carbon accounting.

摘要（中文）: 监测毁林驱动的碳排放，需要同时具备空间明确且时间连续的地上生物量密度估算，并附带经过校准的不确定性。NASA的全球生态系统动态调查项目通过激光雷达提供了可靠的地上生物量密度数据，但其轨道采样导致时空覆盖不规则，加之偶发的运行中断（包括2023年3月至2024年4月长达13个月的休眠期），在观测记录中留下了大范围的空白。先前的研究已利用机器学习方法，结合卫星衍生特征填补了全球生态系统动态调查的空间空白，但针对未观测时期（尤其是跨活跃干扰事件期间）的生物量时间插值问题，仍未得到充分解决。此外，标准的生物量制图集成方法已被证明会产生系统性校准错误的预测区间。为应对这些不足，我们将先前应用于空间生物量插值的注意力神经过程框架扩展至联合稀疏时空场景，并利用地理空间基础模型嵌入。我们对空间和时间进行对称处理，通过实证验证了一种"以空间替代时间"的形式，即利用其他时间邻近位置的观测数据来推断特定缺失时期的情况。研究结果表明，注意力神经过程能够在不同干扰机制下生成校准良好的不确定性估计，支持其在需要可靠不确定性量化的森林碳核算的测量、报告与核查应用中发挥作用。

TLDR: This paper proposes a neural process model for spatiotemporal interpolation of forest biomass using satellite data, with a focus on calibrated uncertainty quantification for carbon monitoring.

简要说明: 本文提出一种用于森林生物量时空插值的神经过程模型，利用卫星数据，重点为碳监测提供校准后的不确定性量化。

Authors: Robin Young, Srinivasan Keshav

SecureAFL: Secure Asynchronous Federated Learning

Abstract (English): Federated learning (FL) enables multiple clients to collaboratively train a global machine learning model via a server without sharing their private training data. In traditional FL, the system follows a synchronous approach, where the server waits for model updates from numerous clients before aggregating them to update the global model. However, synchronous FL is hindered by the straggler problem. To address this, the asynchronous FL architecture allows the server to update the global model immediately upon receiving any client's local model update. Despite its advantages, the decentralized nature of asynchronous FL makes it vulnerable to poisoning attacks. Several defenses tailored for asynchronous FL have been proposed, but these mechanisms remain susceptible to advanced attacks or rely on unrealistic server assumptions. In this paper, we introduce SecureAFL, an innovative framework designed to secure asynchronous FL against poisoning attacks. SecureAFL improves the robustness of asynchronous FL by detecting and discarding anomalous updates while estimating the contributions of missing clients. Additionally, it utilizes Byzantine-robust aggregation techniques, such as coordinate-wise median, to integrate the received and estimated updates. Extensive experiments on various real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SecureAFL.

摘要（中文）: 联邦学习（FL）允许多个客户端通过服务器协作训练一个全局机器学习模型，而无需共享其私有训练数据。在传统的联邦学习中，系统采用同步方式，即服务器需要等待大量客户端上传模型更新后，再进行聚合以更新全局模型。然而，同步联邦学习受到滞后客户端问题的制约。为解决这一问题，异步联邦学习架构允许服务器在收到任意客户端的本地模型更新后立即更新全局模型。尽管具有优势，但异步联邦学习的去中心化特性使其容易受到投毒攻击。目前已有多种针对异步联邦学习的防御机制被提出，但这些机制仍易受高级攻击的影响，或依赖于不切实际的服务器假设。本文提出了一种创新框架SecureAFL，旨在保护异步联邦学习抵御投毒攻击。SecureAFL通过检测并丢弃异常更新，同时估算缺失客户端的贡献，从而提升异步联邦学习的鲁棒性。此外，该框架利用拜占庭鲁棒的聚合技术（如坐标中位数法）对接收到的更新与估算更新进行整合。在多种真实数据集上的大量实验验证了SecureAFL的有效性。

TLDR: SecureAFL is a framework designed to secure asynchronous federated learning against poisoning attacks by detecting anomalous updates, estimating missing client contributions, and using Byzantine-robust aggregation methods.

简要说明: SecureAFL是一个旨在保护异步联邦学习免受中毒攻击的框架，通过检测异常更新、估计缺失客户端贡献并使用拜占庭鲁棒聚合方法来实现。

Authors: Anjun Gao, Feng Wang, Zhenglin Wan, Yueyang Quan, Zhuqing Liu, Minghong Fang

New insights into Elo algorithm for practitioners and statisticians

Abstract (English): This work reconciles two perspectives on the Elo ranking that coexist in the literature: the practitioner's view as a heuristic feedback rule, and the statistician's view as online maximum likelihood estimation via stochastic gradient ascent. Both perspectives coincide exactly in the binary case (iff the expected score is the logistic function). However, estimation noise forces a principled decoupling between the model used for ranking and the model used for prediction: the effective scale and home-field advantage parameter must be adjusted to account for the noise. We provide both closed-form corrections and a data-driven identification procedure. For multilevel outcomes, an exact relationship exists when outcome scores are uniformly spaced, but approximations are preferred in general: they account for estimation noise and better fit the data. The decoupled approach substantially outperforms the conventional one that reuses the ranking model for prediction, and serves as a diagnostic of convergence status. Applied to six years of FIFA men's ranking, we find that the ranking had not converged for the vast majority of national teams. The paper is written in a semi-tutorial style accessible to practitioners, with all key results accompanied by closed-form expressions and numerical examples.

摘要（中文）: 本研究调和了文献中关于Elo排名的两种视角：实践者将其视为一种启发式反馈规则，而统计学家则将其视为通过随机梯度上升进行的在线最大似然估计。在二元结果情形下，这两种视角完全一致（当且仅当期望得分函数为逻辑函数时）。然而，估计噪声的存在迫使排名模型与预测模型之间必须进行有原则的解耦：必须调整有效尺度和主场优势参数以考虑噪声影响。我们提供了闭式修正方法和数据驱动的识别程序。对于多级结果，当结果得分均匀分布时存在精确对应关系，但通常更推荐使用近似方法：这些方法能更好地处理估计噪声并更贴合实际数据。 这种解耦方法显著优于传统上直接复用排名模型进行预测的做法，并能作为收敛状态的诊断工具。应用于国际足联六年的男子排名数据，我们发现绝大多数国家队的排名尚未达到收敛状态。 本文采用半教程式写作风格，便于实践者理解，所有关键结论均配有闭式表达式和数值示例。

TLDR: The paper reconciles practitioner and statistical views of the Elo ranking algorithm, proposing a decoupled approach that adjusts for estimation noise and outperforms conventional methods, using FIFA ranking data as a case study.

简要说明: 本文调和了Elo排名算法的实践视角与统计视角，提出了一种解耦方法以修正估计噪声，其性能优于传统方法，并以FIFA排名数据作为案例研究。
